tamiḻmaṇ  patippakam
eṇ. 2,  ciṅkāravēlar teru,
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017.
to.pē. 24339030, cel: 9444410654


 tāḷ : 16.0 ki. mēplittō,  aḷavu : 1/8 temmi 
 eḻuttu : 11.5 puḷḷi,  pakkam : 320
kaṭṭmippu : iyalpu  paṭikaḷ : 1000 
 kaṇiṉi & nūlākkam : nallatampi, kō. cittirā 
 aṭṭi vaṭivamippu : celvaṉ pā. ari (hariṣ) 
  accu : veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ,  
āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉi - 600 006. 

kaḷañciyam
vaḷamāṉa vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭi vāḻum nilakkiḻār perunilakkiḻār ākiya uḻavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṉ uṭpakutiyil kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ amittiruppar. kaḷañciyam irukkum vīṭu eṉṟāl vaḷamikka uḻavarvīṭu eṉpatu poruḷ.
kaḷattil varum tavacaṅkaḷ payaṟu vakikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi aṅkē pūcciyarippu, maṭkutal ēṟpaṭāvakiyil taḷattiṉmēl uyarttiyum puṉku vēmpu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ taḻikaḷip parappiyum, pātukāppar. avvāṇṭu muḻumikkum payaṉpaṭum. aṭutta āṇṭu viḷivu kuṟintālum kaḷañciyap poruḷ kavaliyiṉṟi vāḻa utavum.
ēruḻavar pōlac collēr uḻavarām cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ, akkālattārkkē aṉṟi ekkālattārkkum payaṉpaṭum vakiyil pātukāttup payaṉ koḷḷac ceyvatu nūl kaḷañciyamākum. 
intiyap parappil tamiḻ moḻiyil tāṉ mutaṉmutal `kalik kaḷañciyam uruvāṉatu eṉpatu perumitappaṭattakkatām. ippoḻutu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattāl veḷiyiṭappaṭum centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam cevviyal moḻik koṭiyāka vantu, tamiḻmaṇ patippaka veḷiyīṭṭāl, tamiḻkūṟu nallulakap poruḷāvatu, cemmoḻic cemmal muṉivar tirumiku ka. irāmacāmi avarkaḷ tūṇṭal vaḻiyākat tulaṅkiya tulakkamākum. ātalāl paṭippāḷaṉ eṉṟa niliyil neñcārnta naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.


kaṭappāṭṭu uri
tamiḻ iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻippaṭṭa oli, vari, vaṭivukaḷiyum iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal, meyyiyal, vāḻviyal, paṭippiyal eṉpavaṟṟi aṭimaṉiyākavum koṇṭa moḻi.
ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē eṉṉum mūla muḻakkatti muḻuvatākak koṇṭatu nammoḻi eṉpati ōrāṟṟāṉ viḷakkuvatāka amintatu ik kaḷañciyam.
āṇivēr, pakkavēr, callivēr eṉpavi illāmal, veḷippaṭa evarum aṟintu koṇṭuḷḷa vaḻakku, ceyyuḷ eṉpavi koṇṭē viḷakkuvatu ikkaḷañciyam.
itaṉi, ūṉṟiyum virumpiyum kaṟpār tāmum ivvāyvu ceyya viyalum eṉpatit tūṇṭa valla āyvum itu! ātalāl, paṭippāḷiyip paṭippāḷi ākkavallatu eṉpati eṉ paṭṭaṟivut teḷivākak kaṇṭu varukiṟēṉ! eḷimiyāy - āḻnta nuṇukkaṅkaḷi - ōraḷavu kaṟṟārum, koḷḷa vaḻikāṭṭum `kiviḷakku aṉṉatu itu.
itaṉi mutal - iṭi - niṟivu eṉṉum mūṉṟu nili kaḷilum uṟṟa peṟalarum utavunarāka iruntu muṟṟuvittatuṭaṉ, tamiḻ ulakap payaṉpāṭṭukkum vaḻiyamittut tanta `kalaṅkari viḷakkam cemmoḻic cemmal muṉivar tiruttaku ka. irāmacāmi avarkaḷē āvar. ātalāl, avarkaḷi neñcārap pōṟṟuvatu eṉ talikkaṭaṉām!
tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli niṟuvaṉa vaḻiyē ippaṇi ceyya vāytta aḷavil coṟṟiraṭṭiyum meyppup pārttuma utaviya peruntaku kēṇmiyum uḻuvalaṉpum uṭiya muṉivar peruntaki ku. rumāṟaṉār avarkaḷukkum em eḻuttuppaṇiyit tam paṇiyāk koṇṭu toṭarntu ceytuvarum toyvilāt toṇṭar meyppup pārttalil vallār muṉivar pi. tamiḻakaṉār avarkaḷukkum neñcārnta naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
eṉ eḻuttip paṭiyeṭuttum, koṅku vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷit tokuttum utaviya pulavar kalimaṇiyār avarkaḷukkum, ippaṇiyil illi eṉiṉum uḻuva laṉpālum urimip pēṟṟālum eṉ eḻuttukaḷip paṭiyeṭuttu utaviya tirukkuṟaḷ cemmal perumpulavar mu. paṭikkarāmu avarkaḷukkum perunaṉṟiyum vāḻttum uṭiyēṉ.
ittoki aṉittiyum kaṇiṉippaṭuttiyum, meyppup pārttum, varavu celavu kaṇakkit takka vakit taṇikkikku uriyaviyākac ceytum, paṇiniṟivi oppaṭittum perunaṉṟāṟṟiya kavaṉakac cemmal muṉivar kali. ceḻiyaṉār aruntoṇṭip pārāṭṭi naṉṟi perituṭiyēṉ.
paṇi niṟivuppayaṉ, tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakap payaṉpāṭu ātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉē! illākkāl ceyta paṇiyāl āvateṉṉa? ceyyāmi oppatu tāṉē! avvāṟu ākāmal tāyttamiḻaka ārvalarkaḷuṭaṉ, ayalakat tamiḻp perumakkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉum tāyttamiḻaka ārvalarkaḷilum mēmpaṭṭa ārvalarkaḷākak kuviyattu, amerikkā, kaṉaṭā, maliyakam, ciṅkapuri, īḻam mutalāka vāḻvār ārvat taḷirppum tūṇṭalum muṉṉiṟkum pēṟu viyappukkum perunaṉṟikkum uriyatām;
ipperumakkaḷ aṉivar uḷḷamum ōruruvāka 
vāyttu, karuvi nūṟpaṇiyit toki tokiyāka vakippaṭutti vāṉ peruntoṇṭākac ceytalē em piṟavik kaṭaṉ eṉak koṇṭa tamiḻp pōrāḷi tirumiku 
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār veḷiyīṭṭup poṟuppi ēṟṟuk koṇṭu ceyaṟkariya ceyvār periyar eṉpati nilināṭṭiyatip pōṟṟuvataṉṟi eṉ colvēṉ?
kaḷañciyam kaṇiṉippaṭuttiya aḷavil niṟivuṟumā? pattut tokutikaḷiyum orumuṟikku mummuṟiyāy muḻutuṟa meyppup pārttal, eḷimip pāṭā? kaṇpuri vaḷarntālum iṭittaṭi nērntālum ittoṇṭu tamiḻaṉṉi taḷirkkum toṇṭu eṉṟu imip poḻutum cōrātu kaṭaṉāṟṟiya kaṭappāṭṭāḷar meyppuccemmal muṉivar 
pi. tamiḻakaṉārkkup peruṅkaṭappāṭu uṭiyēṉ; patippuc cumiyi ēṟṟami eṉ cumi ēṟṟamiyām!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nalaṉē! 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


tamiḻk kaḷañciyam
moḻiyiṉ vaḷam, ammoḻiyip pēcuvōr payaṉpaṭuttum coṟkaḷālum, am moḻiyil uḷḷa ilakkaṇa, ilakkiya vakikaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻiṉ vaḷam ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum makkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷilum niṟintuḷḷatu. coṟkaḷiṉ vaḷamum, perukkamum moḻiyiṉ ciṟappik kāṭṭum. coṟkaḷiṉ perukkik kāṭṭa ettaṉiyō nikaṇṭukaḷum, akarātikaḷum, kaḷañciyaṅkaḷum tamiḻil vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ pala piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum tamiḻc coṟkaḷāyk karutip poruḷ tantuḷḷaṉa. piṟa moḻikaḷil ēṟiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷum vēṟṟāṭi uṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi aṟintu tamiḻeṉak kāṭṭa, moḻi uṇarvum, ilakkiya ilakkaṇap pulamiyum, tamiḻ muḻutaṟi takutiyum, makkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷi vaḻaṅkum cūḻalil kēṭṭaṟitalum, coṟ poruḷāyvum, colliyal neṟimuṟikaḷum, vērc collāyvum, kūr matiyum niṟintirukka vēṇṭum.
ittamiḻc coṟ kaḷañciyam coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi maṭṭumā tarukiṉṟaṉa? coṟkaḷiṉ vērum, ataṉ virivum, avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkamum, cāṉṟukaḷum, makkaḷiṉ potu vaḻakkum, vaṭṭāra vaḻakkum, yāḻppāṇat tamiḻ vaḻakkum ceṟintuḷḷa tamiḻk kaḷañciyamām.
oru viṉic coṟkaḷ toki tokiyākiya toki ikkaḷañciyam. `aṭittal collaṭiyāka 145 coṟkaḷ uṇṇal vakikaḷ, ūrp peyar īṟukaḷ (504) nōy vakikaḷ (229), nōy viṉikaḷ (216), matil poṟi vakikaḷ (28) mali vakikaḷ (25) mutaliya ettaṉiyō vakikaḷum tokikaḷum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ tokuppu kaṟpāri viyakka vikkum!
ik kaḷañciyat tokutikaḷuḷ nūṟṟukkaṇakkil maraput toṭarkaḷ, iṇi moḻikaḷ, olikkuṟippukaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.
coṟkaḷ viḷakkattiṟku aṉṟāṭac ceytikaḷum (ceytittāḷkaḷ) cāṉṟākiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟṟilakkiya vakikaḷ toṇṇūṟṟāṟu eṉpar. āṉāl, paṟpala nūṟuvakikaḷ uḷḷamiyi ikkaḷañciyam kāṭṭum.
mēṉāṭṭu uṭikaḷi māṭṭiyatāl tamiḻar mēṉāṭṭār ākāraṉṟō? pajji ētō piṟamoḻi eṉṟē mayaṅki niṟpar. atu paccik kāykaṟikaḷāl ceyyappaṭuvatu.
tēvanēyap pāvāṇar uṭaṉ paḻakiyamiyālum, avar nūlkaḷit tōyntu tōyntu kaṟṟamiyālum, avarē pārāṭṭiyamiyālum aiyā, pala coṟkaḷukku vērccol vaḻi mūlamum kiḷiyum kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. puḻuti, pūḻti, pūti.
pala coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷum kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: virippu- virippu; viṟippu - toyviṉmi;
ikkaḷañciyam tavacaṅkaḷām coṟkaḷ koṭṭappaṭṭu niṟintuḷḷatu. paṭippār tattamakkut tēviyāṉa tavacaṅkaḷi aḷḷi aḷḷip payaṉ koḷḷalām. evvaḷavu aḷḷiṉum eṉṟum kuṟiyātu ikkaḷañciyam.
tamiḻariṉ, moḻi, iṉa, ilakkiya, ilakkaṇam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu, paṇpāṭu, nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ aṭaṅkalkaḷip paṭi paṭiyākat tiraṭṭit tokuttu veḷiyiṭum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil mutaliṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu.
aiyā mutumuṉivar, iḷami mutal tokutta coṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷic cērttuc cērmāṉamākkit tiraṭṭit tantuḷḷārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷē! iṅkē vammiṉ! kaḷañciyam koṇmiṉ!! tamiḻi vaḷarmiṉ!!!
pi. tamiḻakaṉ.

aṇinturi
centamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam eṉṉum innūl iṉṟu nammiṭiyē vāḻum tolkāppiyarāka viḷaṅkum 
mūtta tamiḻaṟiñar centamiḻ antaṇar mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa oṉṟākum. ēṟattāḻa 8000 tamiḻc coṟkaḷukkup poruḷ viḷakkattiṉi mēṟkōḷ cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkum innūl tōrāyamāka 3254 pakkaṅkaḷil pattut tokutikaḷāka veḷivantu taṉṉērilāta tamiḻ moḻikkut taṉipperumi cērkkum muṉṉōṭip paṇiyāka amintuḷḷatu. akaramutalikaḷil iṭampeṟāta coṟkaḷ, marapuccoṟkaḷ, vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ pōṉṟavi ikkaḷañciyattil iṭampeṟuvatu kuṟippiṭattakka tākum. ici, maruttuvam, kalveṭṭu, aṟiviyal ākiya tuṟikaḷ cārnta iṉṟiyamiyāta  coṟkaḷukkum poruḷ viḷakkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. akarattil toṭaṅkum coṟkaḷuk kāṉa poruḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē 276 pakkaṅkaḷil mutal tokutiyākavum taṉit tokutiyākavum amintiruppatu nūliṉ ceṟiviyum viriviyum paṟi cāṟṟuvatāka amintuḷḷatu. ayaṉ moḻic coṟkaḷ aṟavē tavirkkap paṭṭiruppatu nūliṉ taṉicciṟappākum.
coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyap paṇiyi niṟivēṟṟum vakiyil nūlāciriyarukkut tūṇṭutalāka amintatu olkāp pukaḻ tolkāppiyamē. kuṟippāka, ellāc collum poruḷkuṟittaṉavē (tol. 640) eṉṉum nūṟpā. in nūṟpāviṉi mūla muḻakkamākak koṇṭatu tamiḻmoḻi eṉpati viḷakkum poruṭṭē ikkaḷañciyam uruvākkap paṭṭateṉa nūlāciyir kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar kāṭṭiyavāṟu āṇivēr, pakkavēr, callivēr eṉak kāṇāmal veḷippaṭiyāka aṟiyakkūṭiya coṟkaḷukku maṭṭumē amitiyum tiripum poruḷ viḷakkamum kāṭṭuvatu ikkaḷañciyam eṉpatum nūlāciriyar kūṟṟākum. poruḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷiṉūṭē kīḻkkāṇum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭṭiṉiyum uṇaralām.
poruṇmi teritalum coṉmi teritalum
colliṉ ākum eṉmaṉār pulavar.	(tol. 641)
teripuvēṟu niliyalum kuṟippiṉ tōṉṟalum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa poruṇmi niliyē. 	(tol. 642)
poruṭkup poruḷteriyiṉ atuvaram piṉṟē.	(tol. 874)
poruṭkut tiripilli uṇartta valliṉ	(tol. 875)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.	(tol. 877)
oru poruḷ pala coṟkaḷ mikavum nuṭpamāy ārāyap paṭṭut tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. `aḻaku poruṇmiyik kuṟikkum 67 taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷi aṭiyāḷaṅkaṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku nuṭpamāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷit tantiruppatu āciriyariṉ nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattiṟkum vaḷamāṉa paṭṭaṟiviṟkum cāṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
perumatippiṟkuriya aiyā iḷaṅkumarāṉāriṉ neṭuṅkāla avā innūl veḷiyīṭṭiṉ vaḻi niṟivēṟiyuḷḷami makiḻvaḷikkiṟatu. itu kālatti veṉṟuniṟkum nilitta paṇi. tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa taliciṟanta nōkkīṭṭu nūl. ulakeṅkum vāḻum tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷ innūliṉi varavēṟṟup pōṟṟuvārkaḷ eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli. kaḷañciyap paṇi oru toṭar paṇi. ippattut tokutikaḷi aṭittaḷamākak koṇṭu toṭarntu nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tokutikaḷ veḷivantu tamiḻiṟku vaḷamcērkka vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ avā.
taṉittamiḻp paṟṟāḷarākavum ariya tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ patippuc cemmalākavum viḷaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar tiru. kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār ikkaḷañciyatti arumuyaṟciyuṭaṉ ciṟappuṟa veḷikkoṇarntiruppatu pārāṭṭiṟkuriyatākum.
muṉivar ka. irāmacāmi
muṉṉāḷ pērāciriyar - tuṇi iyakkunar
intiya moḻikaḷiṉ naṭuvaṇ niṟuvaṉam, micūr
muṉṉāḷ poṟuppu aluvalar
cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉam, ceṉṉi

nuḻivuri
tamiḻ ilakkiya, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil putintu kiṭakkum aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit taṉakkē uriya naṭiyil iruntu  tamiḻiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷi akaḻntu kāṭṭum ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ niṉivāṟṟali niṉintu niṉintu  makiḻpavaṉ.
avar arukil irukkavum, avaruṭaṉ uriyāṭavum, vāḻnāḷil eṉakkuk kiṭitta perum pēṟākavum karutukiṟēṉ. moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, centamiḻ aṟiñar ilakkuvaṉār, mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār mutalāṉa perumakkaḷi eṭṭi iruntu pārttavaṉ. avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu nalaṉkaḷik paṭittuc cuvittavaṉ. ip perumakkaḷiṉ motta uruvamāka ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉārip pārttu neñcam nekiḻpavaṉ.
ayyā avarkaḷiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷik kaṭanta kālattil pēraḷavil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu avariṉ uḻippi tamiḻ kūṟum ulakukku aḷittatil niṟivu aṭikiṟēṉ.
iṉṟu uṅkaḷ kikaḷil tavaḻum centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam eṉum ivvaruntamiḻt tokuppi uruvākkuvataṟku evvaḷavu kālam uḻittiruppār eṉpati niṉittup pārkkiṟēṉ. viyantu pōkiṟēṉ. iccentamiḻt tokuppu tamiḻ akarāti varalāṟṟil tamiḻukkup putu varavākum. ituvari yārum ceyya muṉvarāta perumpaṇi yākum. ayyāviṉ ilakkiya, ilakkaṇap perumparappit tāṅkivarum ippoṟ kuviyali ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkuvatil perumakiḻcci aṭikiṟēṉ.
 pāvāṇar pōl vērkāṭṭi, vaḷam koṭukkum coṟkaṭalāy vāḻum ayyāviṉ pēruḻippāl viḻinta ilakkiya ilakkaṇak kaḷañciyatti veḷiyiṭuvataṉ mūlam eṉṉiyē nāṉ perumip paṭuttik koḷkiṟēṉ. eṉ vāḻkkiyil kiṭitta perum pēṟākak karutukiṟēṉ. 
ayyāviṉ 75ām (30.01.2005) akavi niṟivi tiruccit tirunakaril tamiḻar aṉivarum makiḻcciyuṭaṉ koṇṭāṭiya nikaḻvil pulamikkut talivaṇakkam eṉum niṟivumalariṉi tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu makiḻntatu. anta malaril malēciyap pāvaracu cu.ai.ulakanātaṉ eḻutiya pāṭaliṉi kīḻē cuṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ.
vāyttirukkum akaviyelām
     varalāṟu paṭikkiṉṟār
      viyakamē vantu pōṟṟu
nam muṉ cāṉṟāka vāḻum `aiyā avarkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa ikkaḷañciyatti tamiḻ ulakukku vaḻaṅkuvatil perumiyum, pūrippum aṭikiṟēṉ.

	kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ


āciriyar vivaram
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār
piṟappu 	: 	30. 1. 1930
peṟṟōr 	: 	irāmu - vāḻavantammi (uḻavarkuṭi)
ūr 	: 	caṅkaraṉkōvil vaṭṭam, vāḻavantāḷpuram
kalvi 	: 	toṭakkak kalvi - uḷḷūril 
naṭunilik kalvi - pāvāṇar payiṉṟa muṟampu paḷḷi
vittuvāṉ - tamiḻt tērvu - taṉit tērvar
āciriyar paṇi : 
toṭakkap paḷḷi - 16ām akaviyil toṭakkam
uyarnilip paḷḷi - tamiḻāciriyappaṇi
āyvuppaṇi : 
pāvāṇaruṭaṉ - centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutalit tiṭṭam, maturik kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakattil - kalic col viḷakkam tokuppup paṇi, tamiḻaka vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc col tokuppup paṇi.
tamiḻppaṇi : 
kaṭṭuri, kaviti, kati, nāṭakam, kāppiyam, ilakkaṇa - ilakkiya uri - tamiḻc collāyvu - paḻantamiḻnūl patippukaḷ - taṉimaṉita - ilakkaṇa - iyakka, ici, varalāṟu.  tamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam - nāṭakam.  kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ - āyvup poḻivu - tokuppu nūlkaḷ nāḷoṉṟukku 18 maṇinēra eḻuttup paṇi tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli niṟuvi iyakkutal.  centamiḻc coṟporuṭ kaḷañciyam ...
nūlkaḷ : 
civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, maṇivācakar patippakam, īrōṭu kuṟaḷāyam, tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccāli, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi 420kkum mēl
veḷināṭṭu payaṇam : 
tamiḻīḻam, ciṅkappūr, malēyā, amerikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil tamiḻ amippukaḷil poḻivukaḷ.
tirumaṇam nikaḻttutal : 
tamiḻakam, ciṅkappūr, malēyāvil 4000kkum mēṟpaṭṭa tamiḻneṟit tirumaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻttunar.
iyakkappaṇi : 
tamiḻakat tamiḻāciriyar kaḻakam, ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakam, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakam, tirukkuṟaḷ pēravi, īrōṭu kuṟaḷāyam, tamiḻccāṉṟōr pēravi
virutu : 
ilakkiyac cemmal, centamiḻ antaṇar, mutumuṉivar mutaliyaṉa.

patippāḷar vivaram
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ		:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr		:	uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625						tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi		:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷit 				tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimik kaḻakam eṉṉum amippiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻittavar. iḷam talimuṟikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkiyi neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭa maṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakatti niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyum, putiya paṭippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum, 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷit tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumiyākap poruḷ vaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttiri patittu varupavar.
potunili
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, tanti periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā,  moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōri vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkikaḷi niṟivēṟṟa   ayarātu uḻittu varupavar.

‘ca’  varicik coṟkaḷ

ca 
tamiḻ uyirmey variciyil mūṉṟām eḻuttu itu. oru māttiri aḷaviṉatām kuṟṟeḻuttu. valliṉa eḻuttu variciyil iraṇṭām eḻuttu. nāṉku eṉṉum tamiḻeṇ cakarak kīṟṟiṉ mēl nīṭṭalākum (Ì).
cakaram, cakāram eṉpaṉa cāriyi iṇinta vaṭivām. cakaram mutalām moḻikaḷi,
caricamaḻppuc caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi
caḷicakaṭu caṭṭi cavaḷi - cavicaraṭu
cantu cataṅki caḻakkāti yīriṭattum
vantaṉavāl cammutalum vi
eṉkiṟatu naṉ. mayili nātaruri viḷakkam (105).
valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa	- tol. 19
cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē	- tol. 62
caricamaḻppu eṉṉum eṭuttukkāṭṭu veṇpāviṉ aṭikkuṟippāka itu, cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiratti maṟutturippār kūṟṟup pōlum eṉṟu eḻutukiṟār u.vē.cā.
cakkaram, cakaṭam, cakaṭu, caṭi, caṇpakam, catukkam. caturam, cantam, cantaṉam, canti, cantu, camam, camayam, camaḻppu, camaṉ, calam, cavaṭṭal ivi paḻantamiḻc collaṭivil uḷḷavi. caṅkam, cataṅki, camar, camiyam, camiyal, calaṅki eṉpaṉavum tamiḻē!
cakati, cakuṉam, cakkaṭṭi, cakkari, cakkaḷiyal, cakkiri, cakki, caṅkaṭam, caṅkāyam, caccaravu, cañcalam, caṭakku, caṭaṅku, caṭacaṭappu, caṭalam, caṭuti, caṭivu, caṭṭakappi, caṭṭakam, caṭṭam, caṭṭi, caṭṭuvam, caṭṭeṉa, caṇappu, caṇal, caṇṭāḷaṉ, caṇṭi, caṇṭu, caṇṭi, caṇpu, catacatappu, catir, caturam, cantaṭi, cantuceytal, cantuvāy, canti, cappāṇi, cappu, cappi, camaṇ, camati, camam, camaram, camar, camaṉici, camaṉili, camital, campal, campaḷam, campā, campāram, campu, carakku, caracarappu, caraṭu, caram, caravaṭi, cari, cariki, carikkaṭṭutal, carikkuccari, carital, caripaṅku, caripāti, carippaṭutal, carivu, caruku, carukkari, caruccari, caruvutal, calittal, calippu, callaṭam, callaṭi, callari, calliki, callikkal, callivēr, cavam, cavaṅkal, cavaṭi, cavaṭu, cavari, cavar, cavarkkāram, cavali, cavaḷam, cavaḷi, cavaḷutal, cavukku, cavi, cavvu, caḻakku, caḻital, caḷacaḷappu, caḷḷi, caṟukkal, caṟṟu, caṉṉam, caṉṉalpiṉṉal
iviyellām iruvaki vaḻakkukaḷilum iṭampeṟṟa cakaramutaṟ coṟkaḷ. ivvāṟākavum cakaram moḻi mutalākātu eṉal eṇṇat takkatām.
cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
aai auveṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (62).
iḷavaḻakaṉār tolkāppiya mūlappatippil,
avi, auveṉum oṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉap pāṭavēṟupāṭu kāṭṭuvār (kaḻakappatippu 1943). 1942- l veḷivanta puṉṉivaṉanātar mūlappatippil,
cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
aai auveṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉṟē nūṟpā uḷatu. pāṭavēṟupāṭu illi. 
1996-l paṉṉāṭṭut tirāviṭa moḻiyiyaṟ kaḻakap patippu (tol. mūlam) veḷivantatu. atu palvēṟu ēṭukaḷ, patippukaḷi āyntu veḷivanta ariya patippu. atil innūṟpāviṉ aṭikkuṟippāka, innūṟpāviṟku, aviau eṉṉum oṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē eṉṟa pāṭavēṟupāṭum uḷḷatu eṉap pāvāṇar eḻutukiṟār. āṉāl entac cuvaṭiyilum patippilum ippāṭam kāṇappeṟavilli. itu avar tiruttam. eṉṟuḷḷatu.
ippāṭavēṟupāṭu kaḻaka mūlappatippil muṉṉarē uḷḷatāl, pāvāṇar ataṉpiṉ ataṉic cuṭṭi nac. uriyil eḻutiṉār (1944). itu avar tiruttam eṉpatu aṉṟu.
iṉi, mayilinātar kāṭṭiya cakaramutaṟ coṟkaḷ palavum caṅkac cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷil iṭampeṟṟavi. tirukkuṟaḷilum cila iṭampeṟṟavi. piṉṉē akara mutali vaḻiyē kāṭṭappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ mikappalavātal teḷivu. ivi tamiḻc coṟkaḷē aṉṟip piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ alla.
akarak kiḷavi (tol. 115) vakarak kiḷavi (tol. 81) eṉpavi pōlac cakarakkiḷavi eṉpatum (tol. 62) cakara mutalākiya col eṉpatutāṉē! avvāṟāka annūṟpāvē cakaram moḻi mutalāka vārātu eṉpatu muraṇām aṉṟō!
cakara aikāramum, cakara aukāramum tamiḻil moḻi mutalākāmi veḷippaṭi. cakaram moḻi mutalātalum veḷippaṭi. āṉāl, tolkāppiyattil `cakara mutal col etuvum varavilli.
ēppuḻi ñāyil ēntunili araṇam kaṇṭa tamiḻaṉ, caṭṭi ceyya aṟiyāṉ eṉṟō ataṟkup peyariṭa aṟiyāṉ eṉṟō kūṟal takātu.
avvakiyāl, mayilinātar kāṭṭiya veṇpāviṉ āṇippaṭi,
cammutalum vi
eṉpatē muṟiyām.
cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
av, ai auveṉum iraṇṭalaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉa nūṟpā amintu av cuṭṭup peyarāy niṟka, ai, au eṉṉum iraṇṭalaṅ kaṭiyē eṉṟākavum, akarattiyum eṇṇiyamiyāl mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē eṉat tirittirukkak kūṭum. āyiṉum, ēṭṭuc cāṉṟu kiṭṭātavari, ippāṭamē pāṭamām. maṉampōl tiruttiṉ ennūlum nūlāka irātu.
cakaṭam 
nīrōṭṭattil uruṇṭu uruṇṭu varummaṇ cavaṭṭu maṇ. cavaṭṭumaṇ, pāṉi kuṭam mutaliyaṉa vaṉiyap payaṉām. ap pāṉi vaṉiyap payaṉpaṭuvatām cuḻal poṟi cavaṭu. atu cakaṭu eṉa vakaram kakaramākat tirintatu. cakaṭu ūrtiyāyatu; cakaṭam (cakkaram) vaṇṭi eṉa uruppeṟṟatu. atu, uruḷvatāyk kālpōl payaṉpaṭṭamiyāl cakaṭakkāl eṉṟāyatu. ac cakaṭam pōl vaṭṭamāy amikkappaṭṭa iṉippukkaṭṭi cakkari eṉappaṭṭatu. cakkaram, cākaram eṉa nilam cūḻnta kaṭalik kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. cuṟṟic cuḻalum tiriki cakkaṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. vāyil oṉṟi iṭṭup palkāl meṉṟu allatu palkāl poṟiyāl āṭṭi veḷiyē taḷḷuvatu cakki eṉappaṭṭatu. vaṭṭa vaṭivil, iḻittu ōṭaviṭṭa poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa ōr aṇikalam cavaṭi āyatu.
* cavaḷi kāṇka.
cakaṭu 
cakaṭu, oḷimikka viṇmīṉ. cakaṭu vaṭiviṉatu. tiṅkaḷōṭu cērntu niṟkum poḻutu maṇamaṅkala muḻuttap poḻutākak koḷḷappaṭuvatu.
aṅkaṇ iruvicumpu viḷaṅkat tiṅkaḷ
cakaṭa maṇṭiya tukaḷtīr kūṭṭattu	- akam. 136
vāṉūr matiyam cakaṭaṇiya	- cilap. 1: 50
cakaṭu  > cakaṭamām.
* cakaṭam kāṇka.
cakaṭi 
nīr uruṇṭōṭum mēṭṭic cakaṭi eṉpatu tirupparaṅkuṉṟa vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. mēṭṭil iruntu kīḻē cakkaram uruḷvatu pōl nīrum uruṇṭu ōṭutal kaṇṭu vikkappaṭṭa oppuppeyar itu. cakaṭi = cakkaram. cakaṭu = vaṇṭi. cākāṭu eṉpatum atu. cakaṭu, cākāṭu eṉpavi tolpaḻa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
* cakaṭu kāṇka.
cakkaṭi 
kēḻvaraku, kampu ākiya tavacaṅkaḷi māvākkik kaḷi uṇavāka uṇpatu muṉṉāḷip peruvaḻakku. kaḷiyākkum pōtu tuṭuppāl naṉku kiṇṭit tiraṭṭuvar. akkiṇṭaṟkuriya pāṉiyic caṟṟē kaviḻa vittuk kaṭivar. avvāṟu cāyttu vittuk kaṭitalic cakkaṭi eṉpar.
* cakkaṭṭi kāṇka.
cakkaṭṭi 
oruvarkku iraṇṭukālkaḷil oṉṟu caṟṟē neṭiyatāy maṟṟoru kāl caṟṟē kuṟiyatāy iruntāl avar naṭi iyalpāka irātu. ovvōr aṭiyum cakkuc cakku eṉṟu oliyuṇṭāka ūṉṟi naṭappār. avvoli cakkuc cakkeṉak kēṭṭalāl avaric cakkaṭṭi eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. itukāl māṟṟut tiṟaṉāḷareṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu paṇpiyal ciṟappām! iraṅkat takkatu, eḷḷalākātē!
cakkara māṟṟu 
oṉṟaṉ peyarkaḷi yellām muṟiyē kūṟic cakkarac cuḻaṟci pōla avi aṭuttaṭuttut talimi peṟumāṟu pāṭutal cakkara māṟṟu eṉappaṭukiṟatu. ivvakiyil tiruccakkara māṟṟākat tiruñāṉacampantar pāṭiya patikam oṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. atu cīrkāḻip patikamākum.
piramaṉūr vēṇupuram pukali veṅkurup perunīrt tōṇi
puramaṉṉu pūntarāy poṉṉañ cirapuram puṟavañ caṇpi
aramaṉṉu taṇkāḻi koccivaya muḷḷiṭaṅ kātiyāya
paramaṉūr paṉṉiraṇṭāy niṉṟatiruk kaḻumalam nāmparavu mūrē!
itu patika mutaṟpāṭal. piṉṉar vēṇupuram, pukali, veṅkuru, tōṇipuram, tarāy, cirapuram, puṟavam, caṇpi, kāḻi, kocci vayam eṉap pattup peyarkaḷ pattup pāṭalkaḷilum muṉṉāka viḷaṅkac cakkara muṟiyil cuḻalvatāl ippeyar peṟṟatām. iccakkara māṟṟilēyē mēlumoru patikamum pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. atu viḷaṅkiya cīrppiramaṉūr eṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cakkaram 
cakkaram:1
cakaṭu  > cakaṭam  > cakkaṭam  > cakkaram.
cakaṭu, cakaṭam, cākāṭu eṉpavi vaṇṭi eṉṉum poruḷaṉa. vaṭṭa vaṭivamākac ceyyappaṭṭa cakkariyum (carukkari) cakkaram eṉappaṭṭatu.
vaṭṭa vaṭivāṉa paṇam cakkaram ākum. aracariṉ āḻippaṭi cakkarappaṭiyām.
cakkara neṟi nil	 eṉṟār auviyār (ā.cū).
uruṇṭi (vaṭṭa) vaṭivāṉa ulakam cakkaram eṉappaṭṭatu.
muccak karamum aḷappataṟku nīṭṭiyakāl
iccak karamē aḷantatāl	- taṉip. paṭṭiṉap.
maṇpāṉi vaṉivār karuvi cakkaram ākum. virivāka vantu cellum viruntiṉariyum, ōriṭattut taṅkātu tiripavariyum kālil cakkaram māṭṭiyirukkiṟār eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
cakkaram eṉpataṉ kakara oṟṟut tokuttu, cakaramāki ulaka vaṭṭam cūḻum kaṭalik kuṟittatu.
cakkaram  > cakaram  > cākaram.
cakarar tōṇṭiya kaṭal cākaram eṉap puṉiyappaṭṭatu. kāṟṟiṉ iyaṟki cuḻalutal ātalāl ataṉic cakkaram eṉpatum, cakkarappā eṉpatum pulamiyar vaḻakku. nālāric cakkaram, āṟāric cakkaram, eṭṭāric cakkaram eṉpavi cakkarap pāvaki.
cakkaram:2
cakkaram vaṇṭikaḷukku uriyatu. cakkaramākiya vaṭṭa vaṭivil ākkiya cakkaram eṉṉum paṇam cēralattil vaḻaṅkiyatu. vaṭṭamākac ceyyappaṭṭa vellam cakkaram eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭutal muñciṟi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. cakkari (carukkari) potu vaḻakku.
cakki 
mēlē, muḷtōl; ataṉuḷḷē, vaḻukkit tōl; ataṉuḷ, pacittōl; ataṉuḷḷē, cuḷi; ataṉ uḷḷē, viti allatu koṭṭi. ataṟkum mēltōl; ivvāṟu vilakki otukkappaṭum tōṭu (cakki) mikap palavāka iruttalāl palāp paḻattic cakkip paḻam eṉpatu kumari māvaṭṭa vaḻakku.
cakkippōṭu 
cakkip pōṭu pōṭṭāṉ maḻi cakkippōṭu pōṭṭuviṭṭatu eṉpatu teṉtamiḻaka makkaḷ vaḻakku. itu mikuti eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatu. karumpi āṭṭic cāṟeṭutta eccam cakki. palāppaḻattiṉ cuḷi nīṅkiya tōl mūṭu mutaliyavi cakki. ivaṟṟil payaṉporuḷilum, cakki mikutiyātal kaṇkūṭu. itaṉāl cakki eṉpataṟku mikutip poruḷ uṇṭāyiṟṟu. cāram, cāṟu āyavaṟṟiṉum cakkiyē mikutiyātal perumpālum aṟiyattakkatu. itu teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakku.
cakki vittal 
cakki vittal = uṟuti ceytal.
māṭṭut tāmpaṇikaḷil (tāvaṇi) māṭu piṭippavarkaḷiṭam cakki vittal nikaḻvu kāṇalām. oruvar māṭṭi, oruvar vili pēcuṅkāl avvili ivvaḷavutāṉ; itaṟku māṟṟu illi eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷamākat tarakarkaḷ cakki vippar. cakki eṉpatu vikkōl, cakki, cetti eṉavum paṭum. cila iṭaṅkaḷil pullip paṟittuk kiyil tarutalum uṇṭu. ataṉi vāṅkiviṭṭāl pēccu māṟak kūṭātu eṉpatu irupakkattukkum uṟutiyākum. cakki vāṅkiviṭṭāl kaṭṭuppaṭṭu naṭattalik kaṭamiyākak koḷvar. cakki vittuviṭṭu māṟuvatu iḻivāka eṇṇappaṭum.
caṅkaṭamum caḷḷiyum 
caṅkaṭam = uḻippu mikutiyāl uṇṭākum uṭal tolli.
caḷḷi= māṟi māṟi uṇṭākum maṉattolli.
uṭalnōvum uḷanōvum muṟiyē caṅkaṭamum caḷḷiyum eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟi oṉṟu taḻuvi iyalpavi. ātalāl, ivviraṇṭum vēṟupāṭaṟa vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu.
caṅkaṭamāṉa vēli maṉaccaḷḷi eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ poruḷi veḷiyākkum.
caṅkaṭam 
caṅku + aṭam = caṅkaṭam = tuyaram. aṭam = aṭarttu nerukkuvatu.
kattip pēciṉāl caṅki nerittuviṭuvēṉ eṉa vaṉmam kūṟuvar. caṅku meṉmiyāṉa kuḻal uṟuppu. aḻutti nerukkiṉāl kaṇpituṅkip pōkum; uyiriyum pōkkiviṭum. ataṉāl caṅki nerippatu pōṉṟa tuyar caṅkaṭam eṉappaṭṭatu.
caṅkamuḷ 
caṅkañceṭi, iṇṭañceṭi pōla muḷḷuṭiyatu. muḷ kūriyatu; naccut taṉmiyum amintatu. caṅkum iṇṭum ceṟintu kiṭappavi. ataṉāl ataṉ ūṭu cellal aritām.
* caṅku kāṇka.
caṅkam 
caṅku + am = caṅkam.
caṅkam eṉpatu aṟiñar aviyik kuṟittup piṉṉē palavakik kūṭṭattiṉariyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. taliccaṅkam, iṭiccaṅkam, kaṭiccaṅkam eṉpavi paḻantamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷ.
puttarukku oppa matikkappaṭṭatu putta caṅkam. caṅkam kūṭṭam eṉṉum potup poruḷum koḷḷum. caṅkup pūcci taṉittaṉiyē tirivatilli. ayiri mīṉpōl kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākavē irukkum. accaṅkukaḷip pōl kūṭiya kūṭṭam caṅkam āyatu.
paḻavaḻakku, potiyil, aviyam, kūṭal, puṇarkūṭṭu eṉpaṉa; kaḻakam eṉpatum atu. caṅkattiṉum eṇṇikki kuṟintatu kuḻu. potumakkaḷ avi kūṭṭam eṉpatām.
caṅkāyam 
caruku, cetti mutaliyavaṟṟic caṅkāyam eṉpatu tiruvātavūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. poruntāta naṭpu allatu toṭarpi otukkuvatu, oṭṭiya tūciyiyum aḻukkiyum taṭṭuvatum tuṭippatum pōṉṟatākaliṉ uṉ caṅkāyam vēṇṭā eṉpatu maturi vaḻakku. uṉ caṅkāyattē vēṇṭā eṉpatum atu. uṉ caṅkāttamē vēṇṭā eṉpatu ceṉṉi vaḻakku.
caṅku 
uṭal uṟuppukaḷuḷ oṉṟu caṅku; avvāṟu pūkkaḷāl kāṭci tarum oru ceṭiyiṉ peyar, caṅkañceṭi; caṅkiṉ vaṭivu mūlam caṅkup pūcci. ataṉ kūṭē oli eḻuppum caṅku eṉavum, pālūṭṭum caṅku eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
caṅku aṉiya niṟattilum caṅku aṉiya vaṭivilum aminta mali caṅku mali. caṅku vaṭivilāṉa āṇkaḷ kaḻuttu caṅku eṉappaṭṭu, ellāk kaḻuttukaḷiyum caṅku kuṟippatāyiṟṟu.
āṭṭiṉ caṅki iṟukkip piṭittu atu meṉṟu viḻaṅkum uṇavi eṭuttup pāloṭu kalantu kuḻanti maruntākap payaṉkoḷḷal iṉṟum ciṟṟūr vaḻakkam.
caṅkam pū veṇṇiṟamāṉatu; piṉṉē nīlam mañcaḷ niṟattilum caṅkam pūkkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
caṅku ūtutal 
caṅku ūtutal = cātal.
iṟappukku aṭiyāḷamākac caṅku ūtuvatum, cēkaṇṭi aṭippatum naṭimuṟiyil uḷḷaṉa. kōyil viḻāvilum ivi uṇṭu eṉiṉum caṅku ūtiviṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟāl cāvāki viṭṭatu; uṭalit tūkkap pōkiṟārkaḷ eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ātalāl caṅku ūtutal cāviṉ aṭiyāḷap poruḷākiviṭṭatu. caṅku ūtumaḷavum avaṉ kuṇam māṟātu eṉṟum, uṉakkuc caṅku eppōtu ūtuvārkaḷō, eṅkaḷ caṅkaṭam tīrumō? eṉṟum, tīmiyil ūṟi niṟpavarkaḷip paṟṟi avarkaḷ ceyyum tīmikku āṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ niṉippatu iyalpāyiṟṟu.
caṅku ūtutal oruvar vāḻvil muppōtu uṇṭu eṉpar.
piṟappiṉ pōtu   taliccaṅku
maṇattiṉ pōtu   iṭiccaṅku
iṟappiṉ pōtu   kaṭiccaṅku
eṉpaṉa avi.
kaṭiccaṅku ūtiya caṅkatti mīṭṭeṭuttār pāṇṭitturi nāṉkām caṅkam tōṟṟuvittu eṉpatu oru taṉippāṭal.
caṅku muṭṭi 
muttu uṭiyatu caṅku; oliyuṭiyatum atu. muṭṭikaḷil keṭṭup pōṉavi keṭātavi eṉa nīril mitakka viṭṭuk kāṇpatu vaḻakkam. keṭṭup pōkāta muṭṭi mēlē mitakkum. maṟṟavi tāḻum. keṭṭup pōkāta nalla muṭṭiyic caṅkumuṭṭi eṉpatu mukavi māvaṭṭa vaḻakku.
vaṭṭa vaṭivamum veṇṇiṟamum nīril pātiyaḷavāy mitakkum niliyum caṅku niṉivi ūṭṭiyirukkalām.
caṅkip piṭittal 
caṅkip piṭittal = nerukkutal.
caṅku uyirppāṉa iṭam. mūccukkāṟṟuc cellum vaḻiyaṉṟō atu. ataṉi nerukkutal uyirp pōkkit taṭuppatām. caṅki otukkutal eṉpatum ituvē. caṅki aḻuttip piṭittālē mūccut tiṇaṟi viḻi pituṅkum. ettakiya valiyavaṉiyum caṅkip piṭittuviṭṭāl ceyalaṟavē nērntuviṭum. ātalāl oruvar vāṅkiya kaṭaṉi nerukkik kēṭkum pōtō, kaṭaṉ nerukkaṭi avarukku uṇṭākum pōtō kaṭaṉ eṉṉic caṅkip piṭikkiṟatu eṉpar. tappa muṭiyāta nerukkaṭi eṉpatu  poruḷām.
caṭampu 
caṭampu = caṇal nār.
uṇalāl amintu uyirōṭu viḷaṅkuvatu uṭampu! atu pōl, caṇalāl amintatu caṭampu āyiṟṟu. caṇalnār piṉṉal, tittal, kaṭṭal ākiya payaṉkaḷukku uriyatu. puḷicci pōṉṟa oru ceṭivaki atu. ataṉ taṇṭiṉ mēl aminta paṭṭiyi nīril ūṟa vittu nār eṭuppar. kayiṟu, vaṭam, miti āyavaṟṟukkum ākum.
caṭukuṭu 
tamiḻaka viḷiyāṭṭu vakiyuḷ oṉṟu. caṭukuṭu eṉṟu pāṭik koṇṭu etiriyip piṭittalāl caṭukuṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. itukāl kapaṭi āyiṟṟu. āṭṭam atutāṉ! peyar māṟṟam orumippāṭṭukkākavām! kuṭatti eṉpatu ma.va.
caṭi 
caṭi:1
caṭu  > caṭi = taṭi, cikku, kaṟṟi, ceṟivu.
caṭuttam = virivu.
caṭukuṭu āṭṭattil virintu ōṭalum taṭuttalum (taṭṭu) uṇṭu. kiḷittaṭṭu eṉpatu peyar. koṭi piṉṉic ceṟintu kiṭattalāl naṭiyiṭat taṭiyām. koṭittaṭi atu. caṭippaṉi eṉpatu pārvi maram; kūntal paṉi eṉpatum atu. iyaṟkiyām caṭikku māṟu ceyaṟkiyāka uṇṭākum caṭi. cilar muṭiyil aḻukkum piciṟum cērtalāl caṭiyum caṭikkaṟṟiyum uṇṭām. ataṉic cīppāl pirikka iyalāc cikkalākiviṭum. caṭiyaṉ, caṭicci, caṭiyappaṉ, caṭiyāṇṭi eṉpavi civaṉiyam cār peyarkaḷ.
tāḻcaṭip polinta aruntavat tōṟkē	- puṟam. kaṭa. vā.
marattiṉ vērum vīḻtum piṉṉik kiṭattalāl caṭi eṉappaṭum. (ve.vi.pē.a.)
caṭi:2
aṭarttal  > caṭartal  > caṭi.
aṭara vaḷarntu neruṅkutal, kaṟṟiyāyt toṅkutal. caṭiccampā, caṭipparutti, caṭiyavari, caṭināy, caṭicci, caṭiyāṇṭi ceṟintu kaṟṟiyāyt toṅkum mayirmuṭi. (tēvanē.)
caṭivu 
caṭivu:1
caṭivu = maṉamāṟṟam; ciṉattal.
nāṉ avar coṉṉapaṭi kēḷātatāl caṭittuk koṇṭār eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
caṭivu:2
caṭivu = cōrvu.
nēṟṟuk kaṭumiyāṉa vēli; uṟakkamum illi; uṭal caṭivākiviṭṭatu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
caṭṭatiṭṭam 
caṭṭam      =       aracāl vakukkappaṭṭa oḻuṅkumuṟi.
tiṭṭam = camutāyattāl tiṭṭappaṭuttappaṭṭa oḻuṅkumuṟi.
caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭu naṭakka vēṇṭum eṉṟu valiyuṟuttuvatum etirpārppatum nākarika nāṭukaḷukkellām potu viti.
caṭṭaṅkaḷ aracāl allatu araciṉ amippāl uruvākkap peṟṟālum camutāya amitiyum naṉmiyum camutāyattāl tiṭṭamiṭṭē uruvākkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, ivviraṇṭum oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu vilakkamillāt toṭarpiṉavām. āṉāl, taṉittaṉit taṉmi uṭiyaṉavām.
caṭṭi eṭuttal 
caṭṭi eṭuttal = irantuṇṇal.
ōṭeṭuttal pōlvatu caṭṭi eṭuttal. ōṭu, tiruvōṭu; caṭṭi, maṇcaṭṭi; ilvāḻvil iruppavarum vaṟumikku āṟṟāmal caṭṭi eṭuppatu uṇṭu. tuṟaviyar peritum tiruvōṭu eṭuppatē vaḻakku. ituvē vēṟṟumi.
caṭṭi māṟṟal 
oru caṭṭiyil kuḻampō kāykaṟiyō vittiruppar. atil tāḷittuk koṭṭuvataṟkup piṟitoru caṭṭiyil eṇṇey cuṭavittu, kaṭuku paruppu kaṟivēppili mutaliyavaṟṟip pōṭṭup poriyac ceyvar. tāḷippuc caṭṭiyil uḷḷatik kuḻampu kāykaṟic caṭṭikaḷil viṭṭuvippar. tāḷittal eṉpatic caṭṭi māṟṟutal eṉpatu mutukuḷattūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku.
caṭṭiṉi 
caṭṉi eṉpatu peruvaḻakkuc col. iṭṭavi, tōci irukkum iṭaṅkaḷilellām iruppatu caṭṉi. itaṉit tamiḻ marapil caṭṭiṉi eṉa eḻutuvar. iṭliyi iṭṭili eṉa eḻutuvatu pōla eṉka.
caṭṉi tamiḻccollē eṉa ārvattāl colpavarkaḷ caṭṭuṇi eṉa vaṭivamittuk koḷvar. caṭṭu eṉpatu virivuk kuṟippuc col. caṭṭeṉavā caṭṭeṉṟu etiyum ceyya māṭṭāṉ eṉpavi vaḻakkil uḷḷavi. caṭṭuppuṭṭu eṉṟu pārttu viṭṭuvā eṉa iṇiccollākiyum virivuk kuṟippu uṇarttum. ātalāl, caṭṭeṉa virivāka uṇpataṟkut tuṇiyāvatu caṭṭuṇi eṉap poruttam kāṭṭiṉar.
ratā eṉa oru col vantatu. viḷivu eṉṉa āṉatu? entak kuppi eṉiṉum putitu eṉṟāl uṭaṉē paṟṟik koḷḷum vaḻakkē vaḻakkākkik koṇṭuvarum pōlit taṉmiyil tiḷikkum tamiḻar, ettaṉi coṟkaḷik kiviṭṭaṉar? atar, āṟu, neṟi, vaḻi, paṭi, pati, pāti, taṭam, taṭi, cāli, peruvaḻi, neṭuvaḻi, taṉivaḻi, potuvaḻi mutalām tamiḻccoṟkaḷi ōṅki utittu oḻittuk kaṭṭiviṭṭati varalāṟṟulakam naṉku aṟiyum. itu kaṇṇēril kāṇum kāṭci.
cāli mutaliya coṟkaḷ oru ratāvāl ippaṭip pōyiṉavē eṉa varuntiyavarkaḷ, maṅkammāḷ cāli maṟantu viṭātē cōli eṉpatu pōṉṟa paḻamoḻi potumakkaḷ vaḻakku ākiyavaṟṟi eṇṇic cāliyit talikāṭṭa vittaṉar. āṉālum mēlum eṉṉa āṉatu?
āṅkila rōṭu vantatu. nāṭellām paraviyatu. tamiḻccālikku mūṭuviḻāvit tamiḻarē muṉintu naṭattiviṭṭaṉar. ippaṭittāṉ caṭṉiyiṉ āṭci nikaḻntatu.
caṭṉi eṉpatu urutuc col. accol varumuṉ tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya col tuviyal. oru tuviyalā vaḻakkil iruntatu? irukkiṉṟatu!
miḷakāyt tuviyal, pori kaṭalit tuviyal, tēṅkāyt tuviyal, kaṟivēppilit tuviyal, payaṟṟut tuviyal, mallit tuviyal, putiṉāt tuviyal eṉpaṉa nām aṟiyātaṉavā?
tuviyaliṉ cuvi viḷaṅkak kārat tuviyal, uṟipput tuviyal, puḷit tuviyal eṉavum, ataṉ iṟukka nili nekiḻnili ākiyavi viḷaṅkak kaṭṭit tuviyal, karituviyal eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ammiyil vittut tuvittu (aṭittut taṭṭi) ākkuvatāl tuviyal āyiṟṟu. tuṇi calavi ceyvatit tuvittal eṉpatu aṟika.
tuviyal pōlavē poriyal, aviyal, vaṟiyal, vaṟṟal, kūṭṭu, kuḻampu mutalāṉavi toḻil vaḻivanta peyarkaḷē!
tuviyal vaḻakku muṟṟāka aḻintu viṭavilli. iṭli tōciyoṭu kūṭukiṉṟa niliyiltāṉ aḻintuviṭṭatu. cōṟṟuṇavuṭaṉ cērum pōtu taṉ āṭciyi viṭāmal vittuk koṇṭuḷḷatu. kaṭṭit tuviyal cōṟṟukku eṉṟāl, karituviyal tōci iṭṭavikku!
kari tuviyaliṉ taliyil kivitta caṭṉi kaṭṭit tuviyaliyāvatu viṭṭatā? kaṭṭic caṭṉi eṉṉum peyarāl ataṉ taliyiyum taṭaviviṭṭatu. iṭam koṭuttāl maṭam piṭuṅkuvāṉ, ūci nuḻiya iṭam tantāl oṭṭakam nuḻintuviṭum eṉpavi paḻamoḻikaḷ.
oru racam vantatu; miḷakucāṟu, miḷakutaṇṇīr, miḷakunīr eṉpavi aḻintaṉa. puḷiccāṟu eṉpatum avvakiyiṉatē. puḷikkuḻampu kaṭṭiyāṉatu. atu vēṟu; puḷiccāṟu vēṟu. āṅkilavarum miḷaku taṇṇiyik koṇṭaṉar. nam tamiḻarō iracattil tiḷikkiṉṟaṉar. cāṟṟiṉ iṭatti iracam paṟṟik koṇṭatu eṉpatāl payaṉeṉṉa?
paḻaccāṟu kaṉiccāṟu eṉap paḷicciṭum eḻuttil puttam putu vaṇika niliyaṅkaḷil kāṇpatu illiyā! anta uṇarvu vantuviṭṭāl potuvaḻakkāki viṭum. cilar tuṇintu naṭimuṟikkuk koṇṭu vantāl, palarum ēṟkum nili kaṭṭāyam ēṟpaṭṭē tīrum.
nām, caṭṉi eṉpati yāḻppāṇattār paccaṇi eṉkiṉṟaṉar. ēṉ? pacci miḷakāy, paccit tēṅkāy (avikkāta - kāyavikkāta - porikkāta tēṅkāy) ittakiya poruḷkaḷi arittu ākkum tuviyalip paccaṇi eṉpatu takkatē. nām paccaṭi eṉa oruvakik kaṟiyamittuk koḷvatu illiyā?
paccaṇi arittu ākkuvatu; paccaṭi camittu ākkuvatu. muṉṉatu, ciṟṟuṇavut tuṇi; piṉṉatu, pēruṇavut tuṇi.
iṉik kiccaṭi eṉpatoru colluṇṭu. atu kōyil tiruvuṇavuḷ oṉṟeṉak kōyiloḻuku kūṟum; kiccaṭi ceṉṉip pakutiyil oruvakic ciṟṟuṇavu; nellip pakutiyil caṭṭiṉikku oru peyar kiccaṭi eṉpatu. ik kiccaṭiyum tamiḻccol aṉṟu. marātticcol eṉpar.
nām caṭṭiṉiyi yāḻppāṇattār vaḻaṅkuvatu pōl paccaṇi eṉalām. allatu paḻiya vaḻakkākiya karituviyali āṭcikkuk koṇṭu varalām. moḻikkāval eṉpatu coṟkāval! putuccollākkam; paḻañcollāṭciyip putukkal eṉpavi coṟkāval vaḻikaḷil taliyāyavi.
uṟaṅkiyavaṉ kaṉṟu kaṭāk kaṉṟu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. paruppuk kuḻampiṉ iṭattic cāmpār paṟittuk koṇṭatu. ataṉi eḷimiyākat taṉittamiḻil paṟṟuṭiyavarkaḷālum mīṭṭuk koḷḷa muṭikiṟatā?
caṭṭiṉi iṭattilē paccaṇi varumā? karituviyal varumā? iraṇṭumē varumā? vaḻakkukkuk koṇṭu varum uṇarvāḷarkaḷip poṟuttatu atu. uṇavu viṭutikkārar evarkkēṉum moḻikkāval uṇarvu uṇṭākātō? uṇṭākiviṭṭāl, ōrāyiram tamiḻkkāvalarkaḷiṉ orumotta vaṭivāka avar toṇṭāṟṟa muṭiyum!
caṭṭeṉṟu 
caṭṭeṉṟu = virintu.
caṭṭeṉṟu ōr aṭi aṭikka ākum poḻutuḷ vā eṉpatu poruḷām. collokkum kaṭiya vēkam (kampa. pāla. 412) eṉpatu pōlvatu. caṭāreṉa eṉpatum vaḻakkē.
caṭṭi 
caṭṭi āṅkilar vantapiṉ vanta varavaṉṟu. caṭṭi. paḻamiyāṉatu. pāmpu caṭṭi urippatik kāṇalām. ataṉ uṭaṟpōrviyāka iruntatu caṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. caṭṭakam eṉpatu uṭaṟkoru peyar. elumpālāya uṭal eṟpuc caṭṭakam eṉappaṭum. (yā.vi.).
caṭṭi iṭṭa piratāṉikaḷ eṉpār aracavikkuc caṭṭiyuṭaṉ cellum urimiyar. caṭṭakap pōrviyāy amintatu caṭṭiyām. tiyal toḻil ceyvār tuṉṉakārar eṉappaṭṭār. tuṉṉakārar vīti maturiyil iruntatu. itukāl tiyalkārat teruvāka uḷḷatu.
caṇṭi 
caṇṭu  > caṇṭi = vēlikku ākāta māṭu.
cuṟucuṟuppu aṟṟatum vēlikku utavātatum vaṇṭiyilō ērilō pūṭṭiṉāl paṭuttuk koḷvatumām māṭu caṇṭi māṭu eṉappaṭum.
vaṇṭik kāḷi iraṇṭu; iraṇṭu māṭum caṇṭi; vaṇṭikkāraṉ noṇṭi, tiṉṉumām oruvaṇṭi; tirumpātām orumaṭakkuc caṇṭi eṉpavi paḻamoḻikaḷ. uḻiyāmi = caṇṭittaṉam.
caṇṭu cāvi 
	caṇṭu	= 	nīrvaḷamaṟṟō nōyuṟṟō viḷivukku 			varāmal ularntu pōṉa  taṭṭi, tāḷ, 			vikkōl, caruku mutaliyavi.
	cāvi 		=  	maṇipiṭikkāmal kāyntu pōṉa katirum 			pūṭṭiyum.
caṇṭu payiril nikaḻvatum, cāvi katiril nikaḻvatum ākum. viḷivukku vārāmal aṟukkap peṟum nel tāḷ, caṇṭu - vikkōl eṉappaṭuvatum. cāvi - patar eṉappaṭuvatum, karutuka. patar, pataṭi eṉal ilakkiya vaḻakku.
* pataṭi kāṇka.
caṇṭu vaṟṟal, caruku vaṟṟal:
caṇṭu vaṟṟal	=	nōyppaṭṭu vempi vetumpip 					pōṉavaṟṟal.
caruku vaṟṟal	= 	kāmpum vitiyum kaḻaṉṟa vaṟṟal.
miḷaku vaṟṟal allatu miḷakāy vaṟṟalil taram pirippār caṇṭu vaṟṟaliyum caruku vaṟṟaliyum ivvāṟu otukkuvar. caṇṭu caruku āyaviyum āliyil poṭiyāki viṟpaṉikku vantuviṭal naṭimuṟi.
caṇṭi 
caṇṭi:1
caḷḷi = tolli, iṭiyūṟu, maṉattuyar.
nāy caḷḷu caḷḷu eṉak kurittuk koṇṭu vantu kaṭiyāyk kaṭittuviṭṭatu, caḷḷi piṭittavaṉ avaṉ eṉpavi makkaḷ vaḻakkukaḷ.
caḷ  > caṇ; caḷḷi  > caṇṭi.
vāyccaṇṭi, kiccaṇṭi, oḻiyāccaṇṭiyām. caṇṭikku niṟpavaṉ caṇṭaṉ, caṇṭāḷaṉ, caṇṭāḷi.
caṇṭāḷi  cūrppanaki  tāṭaki  pōlvaṭivu
koṇṭāḷip  peṇṭeṉṟu  koṇṭāyē - toṇṭā
ceruppaṭitāṉ  cellāuṉ  vāḻkkieṉ  vāḻkki
neruppilē  vīḻntiṭutal nēr	- auvi. taṉip.
caṇṭi:2
caṇṭi = ilakkiya vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
talivaṉ nāṭu ūr ciṟappu āyavaṟṟik kūṟi, vañciṉam kūṟal, pōr purital, kaḷakkāṭci mutaliyavaṟṟi virittuk kūṟal caṇṭi eṉṉum nūṟ poruḷākum. pāñcālaṅ kuṟiccic caṇṭi paṭṭitoṭṭi yellām nāṭṭup pāṭalāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kaṟṟaṟiyārkkum payaṉpaṭumāṟu caṇṭi nūlkaḷ eḻuntaṉavām.
caṇṭi caccaravu 
	caṇṭi		= 	māṟupāṭṭāl uṇṭākum kikkalappu.
	caccaravu		= 	māṟupāṭṭāl uṇṭākum vāy (vacik) 					kalappu (kalakam).
vīṭu, teru, ūr aḷavil naṭappavi caṇṭi eṉṟum, nāṭu taḻuviya aḷavil naṭappatu pōr eṉṟum vēṟupāṭu koḷḷalām. caṇṭiyil perumpālum karuvikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭā. ākaliṉ, kikkalappu eṉal takum. cilar kikkalappu iṉṟi vāykkalappu aḷavil amivatum uṇṭu. iraṇṭiyum iṇittuc caṇṭi caccaravu pōṭātīrkaḷ eṉṟu taṭuppar.
caṇṭi pōṭuvataṟkākavē cēvaṟkōḻikaḷi vaḷarttup payiṟci taruvar. cēvaṟcaṇṭi, cēvaṟpōr, kōḻic caṇṭi eṉpavi avi. piṉṉarc cūtāṭṭamākavum āyiṉa. ataṉāl caṭṭattāl taṭukkappaṭṭatu. muṟṟilum oḻikkappaṭavilli eṉpatu tirikaḷāl viḷakkamākiṉṟatu.
caṇpakam 
caṇpu  > caṇpakam.
caṇpu = naṟumaṇamuṭiya oruvakippul. appulliṉ maṇam pōṉṟa naṟumaṇa malarkaḷiyuṭiyatu caṇpakam.
peruntaṇ caṇpakam cerīi	- tirumuru. 27
campaṅki eṉṉum pūvum naṟumaṇamuṭiyatē. atu ceṭivaki. caṇpakam makkaḷ vaḻakkil ceṇpakamāka vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. mēli mali aṭivārattil ceṇpakat tōppu uṇṭu. ceṇpaka aruvi kuṟṟālac cāralil uḷḷatu.
caṇpakak kāṭutōṟum	- kampa. pāla. 31
cataṅki kaṭṭal 
cataṅki kaṭṭal = araṅkil āṭaviṭal.
āṭuvār, kālukkuc cataṅki kaṭṭal vaḻakkam. āṭaviṭutalukku ēṟpāṭu ceyvār ceyaliliruntu itu vantatām. cilar tāmē nēril vantu collamāṭṭār. attuṇivu avarkku illi; eṇṇamum kūṭa illi. attakiyaric cilar tūṇṭi viṭṭum ēviviṭṭum kiḷappiviṭuvar. avar vantu tuṇivuṭaṉ pēcuvar; collik koṭutta coṟkaḷi ellām colvar. attakiyar ceyalik kaṇṭu viyappaṭintavar. uṉakkuc cataṅki kaṭṭi āṭa viṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ; nīyākavā āṭukiṟāy? uṉṉit teriyātā eṉakku? eṉa nakiyāṭuvar. itaṉāl cataṅki kaṭṭal, āṭaviṭaṟ poruḷatātal teḷivām.


catācivam 
catācivam eṉṉum peyar, peyarik kuṟiyāmal eṇpatu paṇattik kuṟikkum vaḻakkam ceṭṭināṭṭil uṇṭu. eṇkuṇam eṉpatu pōṉṟa oru kuṟippu vaḻi ēṟpaṭṭa vaḻakkāka iḵtu irukka vēṇṭum. iṟivaṟkuk kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻaṉ eṭutta kōyilkaḷip pōṉṟatoru eṇ vaḻippaṭṭatākum. marutupāṇṭiyar maturik kōyil vāyilil 1008 caraviḷakku amittu, 1008 tirukkōyilkaḷukku viḷakkēṟṟiyatākiya karuttup pōṉṟatu ittakiyavi.
catukkam 
caturam  > catukkam.
nāṟpakka mēṭi catukkam ākum. caturam eṉpatu uṭal. kaḻuttiṉ kīḻum iṭuppiṉ mēlum uḷḷa pakuti caturamāka iruttalāl uṭal cataram eṉappaṭṭatu. atu caturam āyatu. avvaṭiva mēṭi catukkamāyatu. cantiyum catukkamum - iṇimoḻi. catukkam nāṟcanti cantikkum iṭattil amintiruntatu.
catukkamum cantiyum putuppūṅ kaṭampum	- muruku. 225
poruḷ: catukkamum nāṟcantiyiṉum (uri, nac.).
catura araṅkak kaṭṭu 
caturaṅkam = catura araṅkam. araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭāṭalik kūṟiṉār vaḷḷuvaṉār. araṅkak kāykaḷi nakarttuvatu pōlac carivākavum valamiruntu iṭamākavum, iṭamiruntu valamākavum mēlē ceṉṟu, piṉṉarc carivāka iṟaṅki muṭivatu pōṉṟa amippil pāṭappaṭuvatu itu.
taṇṭapāṇi aṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiya tiruvaraṅkat tiruvāyirattil ippantam iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. itu kaṭṭaḷik kalittuṟi, veṇpā ākiya pāvakiyil amikkappaṭum.
kutirip pāyccal amippil varuvatu, caturaṅka turakapati pantam eṉappaṭum. cittira kavi viḷakkam eṉṉum nūl kāṇka. turakam = kutiri.
cattiram cāvaṭi 
	cattiram		 = 	kācilāc cōṟṟu viṭuti.
	cāvaṭi	 	 =	kācilāt taṅkal viṭuti.
cattiram cāvaṭi kaṭṭutalum avaṟṟi aṟapporuḷāy naṭattutalum neṭunāḷ vaḻakkam. cattiram cāvaṭikaḷukku nilakkoṭi purinta ceytikaḷ mikap pala. ivaṟṟoṭu kiṇaṟu, cōli, taṇṇīrp pantal mutaliyaṉa amitta ceytikaḷum paḻamiyāṉavi. ūrūrkkuc cattiram uṇṭu; cāvaṭiyum uṇṭu; cattirap peyarāl ūrkaḷum, cāvaṭip peyarāl ūrkaḷum terukkaḷum mikap palavām.
cattiram uṇavu vaḻaṅkuvatōṭu maruttuvamaṉiyākavum viḷaṅkiyatu eṉalām; cattiram vittal eṉpatu aṟuvip paṇṭuvam ceytal ātalāl. cāvaṭi eṉpatu ucāvaṭiyām. ūrūrkku uḷḷa vaḻakkukaḷi ucāvit tīrppuk kūṟum iṭamāka viḷaṅkiyatākum. avvaḻakkam aṟṟup pōṉatāl cattiram cāvaṭi eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ viḷakkamum aṟṟup pōyatām.
cattiram vittal calliyakkiriyi eṉappaṭṭatu (va). maruttuvaccāli ātular  cāli eṉappaṭṭatu (va).
cantam 
cantam eṉpatu ici. cantappāṭal pāvakiyuḷ oṉṟu. iṅkē collap paṭum cantam aḻaku eṉṉum poruḷil kalkuḷam vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. taḷiriṉ niṟamum meṉmiyum kavarcci mikkavi. māniṟam eṉpatu māntaḷir niṟamākum. pallavattiṉ (taḷiriṉ) cantam (aḻaku) maṭiya (aḻiya) vaṭivāl maruṭṭiya tāḻkuḻalē eṉpatu yāpparuṅkalak kārikiyiṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭṭu.
ariya ilakkiya āṭciyum, eḷimiyāka makkaḷāṭciyil iṭampeṟum eṉpataṉ cāṉṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu cantam.
canti 
oṉṟum oṉṟum nērmuṉ niṟpatu cantiyākum. atil iruntu canti eṉṉum poḻutum, canti eṉṉum teruvum, canti eṉṉum col uṟuppum, cantippu eṉṉum kūṭutuṟikaḷum uṇṭāyiṉavām. iraṇṭu kūṭuvatu canti. mūṉṟu, nāṉku kūṭiṉāl muccanti, nāṟcanti. ūrkūṭik kaṭi vāṇikam ceytāl canti; cantiyil uṇṭākum palavaki oli cantaṭi; palavaki vērkaḷ piṉṉip piṇitalāl maṇam uṇṭām maram cantaṉam, cāntam. oṉṟi oṉṟu oppiṭṭuk kāṇum aḻaku cantam. iciyum iṉimiyum iṇinta pā, cantappā.
canti catukkam 
	canti       	=  		iraṇṭu terukkaḷō mūṉṟu terukkaḷō					 cantikkum iṭam 	cantiyām.
	catukkam 	= 		nāṉku terukkaḷ cantikkum iṭam catukkamām.
mūṉṟu terukkaḷ cantippati muccanti eṉṟum, nāṉku terukkaḷ cantippati nāṟcanti eṉṟum kūṟuvatumuṇṭu.
cantiyum catukkamum mikap paḻamiyāṉavi eṉpatu caṅka nūlkaḷil iṭam peṟṟiruppatu koṇṭu aṟiyalām.
* catukkam kāṇka.
cantu 
iraṇṭu toṭikaḷ cērumiṭam cantu; iraṇṭu terukkaḷ cantikkum iṭamum cantu; iraṇṭu terukkaḷi iṇikkum ūṭuvaḻi, ūṭu cantu; cantittuc cellum ūrti niliyam cantippu; palar kūṭi vaṇikam ceyyum iṭam canti; pakalum iravum cantikkum poḻutu canti; iruvar etirīṭṭi nīkki iṇittal cantu ceytal.
cantu pontu 
	cantu	=	iraṇṭu cuvarkku allatu iraṇṭu taṭuppukku iṭiyēyuḷḷa kuṟukalāṉa kaṭappu vaḻi cantu ākum. kaṭavu eṉpatum atu.
	pontu	= 	eli tavaḷi naṇṭu mutaliyavi		kuṭiyirukkum puṭiallatu vaḷi, pontu ākum.
cantu ponti aṭikkāmal etiyāvatu kāppāka vittirukka muṭiyumā? eṉpar. ivvaḷavu cantu pontu iruntāl pūcci poṭṭu varāmal irukkumā? eṉpatum vaḻakkē.
cuvaril iru ceṅkalkaḷukku ūṭuḷḷa iṭiveḷi cantu eṉpatiyum, pontu eṉpataṟkup pottal, poḷḷal eṉpatiyum karutuka.

canti 
terukkaḷ cantikkum iṭattil amikkappaṭṭa vaṇikappakuti canti ākum. putaṉ canti = putaṉkiḻami canti kūṭumiṭam. ūrāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. cantip pēṭṭi eṉpatum canti kūṭiya iṭamām.
capikkiruttal 
avi  > cavi  > capi; capikku iruttal eṉpatu ūravi kūṭiyuḷḷa pōtu; aviyil kālmaṭakki amarum nili capikku iruttal ākum. kālmēl kāl pōṭṭu iruttalō kuttuk kāl pōl naṭṭukkālil iruttalō, kāl piṉ maṭippāki iruttalō illāmal cammaṇam kūṭṭi iruppatākum. tavanili irukkiyākac camaṇat tuṟavar (ciramaṇar) irukkum nili cammaṇam ākum. capikkiruttal eṉpatu ucilampaṭṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakku.
cappaḷāk kaṭṭi 
cap eṉpatu olikkuṟippu. cappeṉa aṟiviṭṭāl teriyum eṉpatil cap eṉpatu olikkuṟippāvatu teriyum. cappuc cappu eṉa ippaṭiyā melluvatu eṉpar. cappu iṭutal = cappīṭu, cāppāṭu.
cappuc cappeṉa olikkumāṟu iraṇṭu marappalakikaḷi ottolikkac ceyvatu cappaḷā. akkaruvi, cappaḷākkaṭṭi. cappu eṉpatu cuvipporuḷum tarutal ma.va. kuḻampil uppuccappu illi, uppuccappu illāta cāppāṭu.
cappāṇi 
naṭakka muṭiyāmal kuntiyiruntu nakarvatu cappāṇi. apparuvak kuḻantiyik kittāḷamiṭṭu - iraṇṭu kikaḷiyum taṭṭi oli eḻuppi - avvāṟē atuvum ki taṭṭi makiḻac ceyvatu piḷḷittamiḻ ilakkiyattil cappāṇip paruvam eṉappaṭum. koṭṭuka cappāṇi eṉpatu atu. cappeṉat taṭṭi oli eḻuppal cappāṇi. paṇṇuvatu, - pāṇi.
cappātti 
kōtumi māvip picintu uruṭṭi, avvuruṭṭiyic cappuc cappeṉa aṟintu akalac ceytu, irumpuk kallil pōṭṭu vēka vikkum aṭṭil muṟiyip pārppavarkku viḷakkamāka ipporuḷ poruttam pulappaṭum. cappu olikkuṟippu.
akaṟṟi  > āṟṟi  > ātti.
x.neh.:
akaṉṟōr  > āṉṟōr.
capputal 
kaṇṇamutu (pāyacam) tēṉ amutu mutaliyavaṟṟic cappuk koṭṭic cuvittu uṇṇal capputalākum. cappuc cappeṉal olik kuṟippākum. cappeṉa aṟital eṉpatil avvolik kuṟippu aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatām. kaṉṟu tāyiṉ maṭiyil vāy vittup pāl iḻuttuk kuṭittalum capputalām. viralic capputal pōlvatākaliṉ.
cappi 
kāl paṭalam virintu noṇṭiyāki naṭappavar, naṭakkumpōtu cappu cappu eṉṟu oli kēṭkum. ataṉāl avar kālic cappik kāl eṉpar. cappik kāl piḷḷiyār eṉpār kāḷamēkap pulavar (taṉip.).
kāliṉ cappi vaṭivu pōl, mūkku uyaramillāmal taṭṭiyāka iruntāl ataṉic cappi mūkku eṉpar.
camati 
camamāka allatu camaṉpāṭāka iruppatic camati eṉpatu irācapāḷiya vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. yār eṉṟālum avariṭam camatiyākap pēcuvār; camatiyāka uṭkāra vippār eṉpatu vaḻakku. camamāka eṉpatu camati eṉappaṭutal nalla coṟ curukkam.
camanili yōṅkal (camacaṅkiṉāmam):
peṇ, āṇ oruvaṉuṭaṉ camaṉili kūṟa avaṉ ac camaniliyiṉ uyarntu niṉṟu avaḷi veṟṟi peṟṟatik kūṟuvatu ippaṉuvaliṉ poruḷamitiyām. itaṉi,
arivi, camaṅkūṟa vēntaṉ camantappi vēṟal
camacaṅki ṉāma meṉṟē cāṟṟu
eṉkiṟatu pirapantat tiraṭṭu (62).
īṉṟa poḻutiṟ perituvakkum taṉmakaṉic
cāṉṟōṉ eṉakkēṭṭa tāy	- tiruk. 69
eṉṉum poruḷ vaḷamikka poyyā moḻikkē,
peṇṇiyalpāl tāṉāka aṟiyāmiyiṉ, kēṭṭa tāy eṉavum kūṟiṉār eṉṟu parimē. poruḷviḷakkam kūṟuvatik karutiṉ, camacaṅkiṉāmam eṉa nūṟ peyar eḻuntami viyappaṉṟām.
camam 
camam:1
camar eṉpatum amar eṉpatum atu. pōr aṟam oppāṉavaroṭu pōriṭalēyām. poru >  pōr. poru = oppu.
ciṟappuṭi araciyalāvaṉa, maṭinta uḷḷattōṉiyum, makap peṟātōṉiyum, mayirkulintōṉiyum, aṭipiṟakkiṭ ṭōṉiyum, peṇ peyarōṉiyum, paṭi iḻantōṉiyum, ottapaṭi eṭātōṉiyum piṟavum ittaṉmi yuṭiyōriyum kollātu viṭutalum kūṟip porutalum mutaliyaṉavām (tol. puṟat. 65 nac.).
camam:2
camam = camaṉili; naṭuvunili. camaṉ eṉpatum itu.
camaṉceytu cīrtūkkum kōl	- tiruk. 118
camaḻppu 
camaniliyil tāḻtal; tali kaviḻtal.
tanta kaḷvaṉ camaḻppu mukam kāṇmiṉ	- pari. 20 :36	
camital 
maṇavāḻvukku uriya takutiyām pūppu aṭital. paruvamaṭital eṉpatum itu.
piḷḷi camintirukkiṟāḷ; kuccil kaṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu ma.va.
camippu 
amippu  > camippu.
camippu:1
camiyal ceytal.
camippu:2
cili vaṭittal. ciṟpi cili camittāṉ. aṇikalaṉ camittal.
muṭimutal kalaṉkaḷ camippēṉ	- cilap. 19:114
camippu:3 
karuvuṟal.
maṉiviyar niṟivayiṉ vacitaṭi camippil	- pari. 5:39
campanti 
kiḷi eṉpatum uṟavu eṉpatum koṭuppavar uṟavup peyarākavum, kēḷ eṉpatum uṟṟār eṉpatum koḷpavar uṟavup peyarākavum irunta nāḷ uṇṭu. kēḷ, uṟṟār eṉpavi tanti vaḻiyar. kiḷi, uṟavu eṉpavi tāy vaḻiyar uṟavup peyarkaḷ. ivi māṟip potumiyāyaṉa. campanti eṉṉum vēṟṟuc col avviṭattip paṟṟik koṇṭatu. campanti eṉpatu uṟavup peyarāka illāmal, oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu cērntu oṉṟākum tuviyal eṉṉum poruḷil kumari māvaṭṭattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
campal 
campal:1
campal eṉpatu vili kuṟital eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum teṉtamiḻaka vaṇika vaḻakkuc col. vaṟṟal vili campal; veṅkāya vili campal eṉpar. poruḷ mikutiyāvatic cavattup pōtal eṉpar. vatiyaḻital eṉṟum kūṟuvar. malivu eṉpatum atu. campal, cāmputal. vāṭutal eṉṉum poruḷatu. kāykaḷ cāmpip pōṉāl - campip pōṉāl - ciṟuttup pōyviṭum. itaṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa vaḻakkuc col itu.
campal:2
campal eṉpatu vili malivu eṉṉum poruḷatu. atu potuvaḻakku. āṉāl viruntu eṉṉum poruḷil paḻaṉi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. viruntukku varuvārkkuk koṭukkum uṇavu nelluc cōṟē. cōḷam, kēḻvaraku, kampu āyavi iruppiṉum neṟcōṟu ākkip paṭittalē vaḻakkam. ataṉāl campu (campā) ākkip paṭikkum viruntāḷ campal eṉap paṭṭirukkalām. iṉi aṭikkaṭi varum viruntiṉari nōkkiyum vaḻaṅkiyatākalām. campal malivup poruḷatu.
campaḷam 
campu + aḷam = campaḷam.
campu    = nel; nelvakiyuḷ oṉṟu campā.
aḷam = uppu; uppu viḷiyumiṭam uppaḷam.
nāḷ vēli, māta vēli eṉac ceyvārkku nellum uppum ākiya iraṇṭumō, oṉṟō vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa paḻanāḷil avviraṇṭiyum cuṭṭum vakiyil campaḷam eṉṟaṉar. uppiṉṟit tavacamākat tantatik kūli eṉṟaṉar.
* kūli kāṇka.
campā 
nel vakiyuḷ oṉṟu. campā nel eṉpatu itu. neṭiya uyaramum nīṇṭa kālamum peruṅkatirum koṇṭa nel vaki. kuṟuṅkālap payir kuṟuvi eṉappaṭum. itu neṭuṅkālap payirām.
campi 
campā nel pōla vaḷarum kōrippul. campaṅkōri vaḷamāka vaḷarum kāl varattu ūr.
e-ṭu:
campi   (mukavi māvaṭṭam).
carakku 
carakku:1
palacarakku eṉpavaṟṟuḷ palavum ucili eṉappaṭum macālip poruḷkaḷēyām. carakku eṉpavi ular poruḷkaḷāṉavi. pala eṉpati vilakkic carakku eṉṟālē macāliyik kuṟippatāka yāḻppāṇa vaḻakku uṇṭu.
carakku:2
carakku = cārāyam.
carakku eṉpatu kāynta poruḷām. palacarakkuk kaṭiyil uḷḷavi ularntu kāynta poruḷkaḷē eṉpati aṟika.
ularnta paṭṭikaḷit taṭṭip pōṭṭu ūṟa vittu vaṭittuk kāyccuvatu cārāyam ātalāl ataṉic carakku eṉpatu vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. paṭṭic cārāyam eṉpatum ataṉ mūlap poruḷi viḷakkuvatām.
carakku muṟukkā? vaṇikar muṟukkā? eṉpatiluḷḷa carakkup poruḷ eṉṉum poruḷatu. carakkup pōṭṭirukkiṟāṉ pōlirukkiṟatu; naṭiyum pēccum terikiṟatē eṉpatil carakkiṉ poruḷ cārāyam, matu mutaliyavi eṉpatu viḷakkamām.
caraṭu kaṭṭutal 
car  > cara  > caraṭu = nīḷamāyt tirittal, nīḷamāyc ceytal.
caraṭu eṉpatu kayiṟu. iṉi, kaḻuttilē pūṭṭum caraṭu eṉpatoru poṉṉaṇikalamum uṇṭu. mañcaḷ kayiṟṟil tāliyik kōttuk kaṭṭutal tirumaṇac caṭaṅku ākum. caraṭu kaṭṭutal eṉpatu ucilampaṭṭi vaṭṭārattil tirumaṇam ceytal eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tāli kaṭṭutal eṉpatu potu vaḻakku.
caraṇi 
caraṇi = carivu; ōram.
ōram cāram eṉpatu iṇimoḻi; ōramum ōram cārnta iṭamum ōram cāram ākum. cāraṇi eṉpatu caraṇi eṉakkuṟuki ōram eṉṉum poruḷil aṟantāṅki vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. maliccāral, malicārntatu maḻic cāral maḻiyic cārntu peyyum ciṟu tūṟal, cāral.
cari 
iruvar uriyāṭukiṉṟaṉar. oruvar urikku oruvar cari,cari eṉkiṟār eṉṟāl, uṅkaḷ karuttu ēṟṟatu, cemmiyāṉatu, nāṉ oppukiṟēṉ eṉpataṉ aṭiyāḷamām. tampi iṉṟu viṭiyalilēyē puṟappaṭa vēṇṭum; ataṟku vēṇṭiya vaṟṟic cey eṉṟu kūṟuvati oruvar cari eṉṟāl appaṭiyē ceykiṟēṉ eṉpatām.
oru maliyiṉ carivip pārkkiṟōm! atu maliyoṭu toṭarpum toṭarcciyum uṭiyatu eṉpatu viḷakkamākiṟatu. oru carivāṉa iṭam eṉpatu, oṉṟic cārntuḷḷa iṭamām. āṅkup peykiṉṟa maḻi, maliyic cārnta maḻi cāral maḻi eṉappaṭukiṟatu. cāral nāṭaṉ eṉiṉ mulli nāṭṭukku aracaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. cēraṉ eṉil kaṭal cērnta nāṭṭut talivaṉ.
cārtal, cārpu, cārcci, cārki eṉpavi ellām oṉṟit taḻuvi niṟpavi. cārntataṉ vaṇṇamātal iyaṟki muṟi. ivaṟṟāl cari eṉpataṉ poruḷviḷakkam teḷivām.
oruvar vaḻiyiṉi - colliṉi - ēṟpataṉ aṭiyāḷam cari eṉpatām. cari ceytal eṉpatu mēṭu paḷḷam tiruttal ceppam ceytal, pakiyi māṟṟi naṭpu ākkal, māṟupāṭṭi akaṟṟal ākiya ottiyipup poruḷiyē tarutal aṟiyalām.
caripārttal eṉpatu ceyyappaṭṭatu cīrāka - cevvitāka - irukka vēṇṭum vakiyil uḷḷatā eṉa āytalām. cariyāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟō cari ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟō cari ceyya iyalāk kuṟiyuṭiyatu eṉṟō tīrppu eḻututalum ēṟkalām ēṟka iyalātu eṉa muṭivu ceytalum taṇikkiyar pārviyām.
uriya tuṇiyuṭaṉ allatu nūluṭaṉ cārnta paṭṭiḻi cariki eṉappaṭum.
caricamam eṉpatu aḷavilum oppīṭṭilum ēṟṟa māṟṟa maṟṟavi eṉpati veḷippaṭuttum. ēṟkat takkatu cariyāṉatu, ēṟkat takkavar cariyāṉavar eṉiṉ uḷam oppavar eṉpatām. ivaṟṟāl cari eṉpatu iyivup poruḷ tantatu.
caricamaṉ eṉpatil ēṟṟat tāḻvaṟṟa nili pulappaṭum. ariyirukki, cariyirukki (ariyācaṉam cariyācaṉam) eṉpavi kampar taṉippāṭal.
carikkaṭṭal eṉpataṉ poruḷ, tam iyalāmi muṭiyāmi kāṭṭi, cikkali oru vakiyāl tīrttuk koḷḷum muṟiyām.
cari cari eṉṟāl ām ām eṉpatām.
caruku pōṭutal 
caruku pōṭutal = veṟṟili pōṭutal, uvappuṟutal.
caruku eṉpatu veṟṟiliyik kuṟikkum. atu nāṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil kāyntu allatu ularntu pōṉa veṟṟiliyik kuṟippatāka amintatām. kaṭiyil vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷi vāṅkik koṇṭu oru caruku koṭuṅkaḷ eṉa ilavayamākak kēṭṭup peṟuvar. caruku, veṟṟilip poruḷ taruvatāl caruku pōṭutal veṟṟili pōṭutal eṉa vaḻakkil amintatu. atu pāluṟavuk kuṟippākavum vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
carēl eṉa 
virintu varutalic caracara eṉa carēleṉa vā eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. caracaraveṉa carēleṉa virintu celvatum kaṇṇi mūṭit tiṟakkumuṉ maṟintu viṭuvatum pāmpām. ataṉ celavu caracara eṉappaṭum. cārip pāmpu eṉṉum peyarik karutuka. caraḷik kal uruḷuvatum carcar eṉa aṟuppatum carakkuc carakkeṉa naṭappatum eṇṇuka.
calacalappu 
calacalappu = accuṟuttal.
intac calacalappuk kellām añciya āḷā nāṉ? eṉpatil varum calacalappu accuṟuttal poruḷatām. nari kāṭṭil vāḻvatu. calacalappiṉ iṭiyēyē vāḻvatu. ataṉāl calacalappu accamaṟṟatu atu. intac calacalappuk kellām inta nari añcumā? eṉṟum tammi nariyākak kūṟuvar.
calacalappu - olikkuṟippu. nīrōṭṭam, iliyacivu, kalam karuvi olikaḷ āyavi calacalappām. ic calacalappu, accapporuḷil varutal vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭatām. antac calacalappi iṅkē vittuk koḷḷātē eṉpatu eccarippām.
calam 
calam:1
calam = nīr.
nīr calacalattu ōṭalāl calam eṉappaṭṭatu.
taṇṇan tuvarpala ūṭṭic calaṅkuṭivār	- pari. 10:90
vāykkālil nīr ōṭukiṉṟatu. atil oruvaṉ taṉ kiyi vittu nil nil eṉat taṭukkiṉṟāṉ. nīr, nillāmal cala cala eṉṉum oliyuṭaṉ ōṭukiṉṟatu. kiyi vittu nīrit taṭuttavaṉukku, tāṉ taṭuttu niṟuttiyataṟku nīr aḻuvatu pōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu! ataṉāl,
irinta teṉ aḻuviyō
cel! cel!
eṉa viṭuttāṉ. itu maṉōṉmaṇiya nāṭakattil varum kāṭcikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
nīr iriccaliṭṭuc celvatu evarum aṟintatu. nīriṉ iriccal oli vevvēṟu vakiyākak kēṭkum. ataṉāl aruvi, muḻavam pōl olikkiṉṟatu eṉṟum, kalleṉak karintu vīḻkiṟatu eṉṟum, olleṉat tavaḻkiṟatu eṉṟum, calacala eṉṟu ōṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟum olikkuṟippōṭu colvatu vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. ivvolik kuṟippukaḷil oṉṟē cal cal, cala cala, calam eṉa amintatām.
nīr cala cala eṉṉum oliyuṭaṉ ōṭuvatāl calam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu. calam pūvoṭu tūpam maṟantaṟiyēṉ eṉṟār apparaṭikaḷ. cala cala mummatam coriya eṉpatum oru tamiḻppāṭal aṭiyē! (cīvaka. 82).
calam ākiya nīr camiyalukkuk kaṭṭāyam vēṇṭum. accamiyal aṟiyilētāṉ kalaṅkaḷit tēyttal, kaḻuvik koṭṭal, vaṭittal ākiyaṉa nikaḻum. ātalāl avvaṟikkuḷ vaṭikkappaṭṭa nīr puṟam pōtaṟku vāyppākak kuḻiyum tuḷiyum amippar. avvamippiṉ peyar, calakkālpuri eṉpatu. atu calakkāppuri, calakkappuri eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟatām. calam = nīr; kāl = vaḻi; puri = tuḷi.
calakkālpurikku iṉṉoru peyar aṅkaṇam eṉpatu. aṅkaṇak kuḻi eṉpatum atuvē. akam + kaṇ + am = aṅkaṇam eṉappaṭṭateṉka.
aṅkaṇattuḷ ukka amiḻtik 	kūṟum tirukkuṟaḷ (720).
ūraṅkaṇa nīri	 urikkum nālaṭiyār (175).
aṅkaṇam calatāri eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. nīr oḻukumiṭamē calatāri eṉka! nīr paḷḷam nōkki nīṇṭu celvatu. ātalāl tār eṉṟum, tāri eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. tāri vārttal eṉpatu nīr vārttal tāṉē!
ōṭi āṟu mutaliyavaṟṟil ōṭum nīr iṟutiyil kaṭalil kūṭutal iyaṟki. calam kūṭum iṭattiṟku - kaṭalukkuc calati eṉpatu oru peyar.
tamiḻeṉum aḷapparum calati eṉpār kampar (tāṭaki. 38).
pala kiṇṇaṅkaḷil palvēṟu aḷavukaḷil, nīrviṭṭu olikkum icikkaruvi calataraṅkam eṉappaṭuvatum, kalippāvil uṟuppukaḷuḷ oṉṟu ampōtaraṅkam eṉappaṭuvatum eṇṇattakkaṉa.
kaṭalnīr alital iṭiyīṭu illātatu. ali ōyvatu eppoḻutu? tali muḻukuvatum eppoḻutu? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyē aliyiṉ iṭiyīṭaṟṟa alivit terivikkum. avvali pōla alipāyum uḷḷamum calam eṉappaṭum. avvuḷḷamuṭiyāṉ, calavaṉ eṉṟum, avvuḷḷa muṭiyāḷ calati eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvar.
calavar eṉpatu kaṭalōṭikaḷiyum, muttuk kuḷippavar kaḷiyum kuṟikkum. calaṅkukkārar eṉpavarum muttuk kuḷippavarē. calāpam eṉpatum muttuk kuḷittaliyē kuṟikkum. nīrāṭuvāric calaṅkuṭivar eṉṉum paripāṭal (10:90) calaki eṉpatu tōṇiyiyum calañcalam eṉpatu valampuric caṅkiyum (cīvaka. 184) calam pukaṉṟu eṉpatu māṟupaṭṭurittaliyum (maturik. 112) kuṟittal aṟiyattakkaṉa.
nīril tōyttu veḷukkum toḻil calavi eṉappaṭuvatum, veḷukkappaṭṭa tuṇi calavit tuṇi eṉappaṭuvatum, puttam putu paṇattāḷ calavit tāḷ eṉappaṭuvatum calavit tūḷ, calavik kaṭṭi eṉac cavarkkāra vakikaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatum, nīr tatumpi vaḻitalum ōyātu pēcutalum calamputal eṉappaṭuvatum vaḻakkil uḷḷami aṟintāl calam eṉṉum poruṭperukkam naṉku viḷaṅkum.
oru collil, vēṟṟeḻuttu oṉṟi amittu eḻuti viṭṭatāl maṭṭum vēṟṟuc col ākiviṭātu eṉpatum, oru vēraṭiyil vanta pala coṟkaḷ kiṭikkumāyiṉ accolliṉ mūlam, ammoḻikkuriyatē eṉpatum, kuṟippāka olikkuṟippu vaḻi vanta col oṉṟu, vēṟupaṭṭa irumoḻikaḷilum kūṭa iṭampeṟak kūṭumeṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
nīr, puṉal mutaliya coṟkaḷ peruvaḻakkil iruntamiyāl, calam eṉpatu arukik kāṇappaṭuvatu koṇṭum, jalam eṉṉum collil iruntu vantatu eṉpār karuttuk koṇṭum aṭippaṭik karaṇiyam teḷivāka amintuḷḷa colli vēṟṟuc colleṉa vilakka vēṇṭuvatilli eṉat teḷika.
peṭṭiyum, peṭṭiyil uḷḷa poruḷum nammuṭiyavi yāy irukka, evarō oruvar peṭṭi mēl vitta poṭṭu oṉṟāl maṭṭum avar peṭṭiyum avar poruḷum ākiviṭum eṉalāmā?.
calam:2
calam = nīrali pōl alikkaḻikkum vañcam.
calampuṇar koḷkic calati eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷār (9:69). calam eṉpatu vañcakam eṉṉum poruḷiyum, calati eṉpatu vañcaki eṉṉum poruḷiyum taruvaṉavām.
calam:3
calam = cīṟṟam.
calampuri taṇṭēntiṉavi	- pari. 15:58
calam:4
calam = puṇṇīr.
kaṭṭi uṭintu calam varukiṟatu ma.va.
calittal 
calittal:1
tuḷiyuṭiya caṭṭi calippuccaṭṭi, kaṇcaṭṭi eṉappaṭum. callaṭi eṉappaṭuvatu periyatu. māc callaṭi, maṇal callaṭi mutal palavāka makkaḷ payaṉpāṭṭil uṇṭu. callaṭi pōṭṭut tēṭutal eṅkum tēṭutal eṉpatām. tēṭik kāṇāmiyāl calittup pōtal uṇṭu. calittal = varuntutal, veṟuttal. 
añcit tarumamum calittatammā 	(kampa. uyut. 269).
calittal:2
callaṭik kaṇpōl pōrkkaruviyāl uṭal tuḷikkappaṭutal  calittalām.
avaṉiyē calikkum nīrāl	- kam. āraṇ. 667

cal taṇṇīr 
payirukku nīr pāycci, orunāḷ viṭṭu maṟunāḷ viṭum taṇṇīri eṭupput taṇṇīr eṉpatu potu vaḻakku. ataṉic caltaṇṇīr eṉpatu iṟiyūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. īram iruppatāl calacalattu ōṭum nīric cal taṇṇīr eṉpatu olikkuṟippu vaḻippaṭṭatu. calacala, calacalappu, calavi, calatāri, calavaṉ, calpiṭittal eṉpaṉa vellām olikkuṟipput tōṟṟa vaḻivantavi. calupput taṇṇīr eṉpatu vaṭamaturi vaṭṭāra vaḻakku.
calli 
calli:1
calacalappu oli taruvatu  calli. calliki  ōr icikkaruvi.
calli:2
kalluṭikkum pōtu viḻum ciṟutuṇṭu  calli. callikkal, caraṉikkal, kaṭṭumāṉap poruḷ.
calli:3
āṅkilavar kālattil ōr urūpāvukku 192 callikkācu eṉṉum nāṇayam iruntatu. ippoḻutu nūṟu calli, ōr urūpā eṉappaṭukiṟatu.
calli:4
ciṟu ceyal ceytu tirivāric calli; callippayal eṉal ma.va.
calli kaṭṭu 
calli= cal cal eṉac calacalattu varum kāḷi.
kaṭṭu= kaṭṭip piṭittu aṭakkutal.
ēṟu taḻuvutal, mañcuviraṭṭu eṉpavi kāṇka. māṭupiṭi eṉpatum atu.
callicu 
calli  > callicu.
calli = ciṟiyatu. atu eḷimip poruḷ tarutal callicu eṉpatām.
atic ceyvatu callicu, vili callicāka irukkiṟatu ma.va.

calli 
toraṭṭi eṉṉum poruḷil calli eṉpatu karuvūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. muḷ marattil ēṟip paṟikka iyalātu. piṭittu uluppavum iyalātu. āṉāl toraṭṭi koṇṭu vaḷikkavō, paṟikkavō, uluppavō eḷitāka iyalum. ātalāl calli eṉṉum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. callitu(cu) eṉpatu eḷitu, kuṟintatu eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkuc collāka iruppatāl aṟiyalām. callicākap paṟikkalām; callicāka vāṅkalām eṉpavi vaḻaṅku moḻikaḷ.
* callicu kāṇka.
cavaṭṭutal 
palkāl mitittal; puraṭṭutal; mika mellutal; nalittal.
* cavaḷi kāṇka.
cavaṭṭu metti 
cavaṭṭutal, cavaḷumāṟu aṭittal mitittal ākiyavi ceytalām. maḻi perukak koṭṭalum, poḻital pōrkkaḷa aḻipāṭum cavaṭṭutal eṉappaṭuvatu paḻanāḷ vaḻakku. palkāl palliṭaṅkaḷil aṭittuvarum maṇ cavaṭṭu maṇ. cavaṭṭu metti eṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭil kālmitiyiṉ peyarāka uḷḷatu.
cavattal 
īrappatamāka iruppatic cavattal eṉpatu aṟantāṅki vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. īrappatamāka iruppatu virivil keṭṭup pōkumātalāl ataṉi vili kuṟittu malivāka viṟpar. ātalāl cavattal eṉpataṟku malivu eṉṉum poruḷ maturi vaṭṭārattil uṇṭu. cavattal eṉpatu pōlavē patattal eṉpatu īrappatam kuṟittal potu vaḻakkākum. maḻiyāl patappaṭṭup pōṉatu eṉpar.
cavar 
cavar eṉpatu pāmpu eṉṉum poruḷil viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. cavar  > caval  > cavaḷ  > cavaṭu. cavaṇṭu pōyk kiṭappatum vaḷintu vaḷintu celvatumām pāmpic cavar eṉpatu, cavaḷ eṉpataṉ vaḻi vantatēyām. cavaṭṭutal kāṇka.
cavarkkāram 
uvar  > cavar + kāram = cavarkkāram (Soap).
cavarkkāram eṉpatu  vaḻali (cōppu). uvar, aḻukkup pōkkum maṇ. calavikku uvarmaṇ payaṉpaṭuttuvatu ciṟṟūr vaḻakku. uvar  > cavar ac cavart taṉmiyum kaṟiyi eṭukkum erivum koṇṭatu cavarkkāram. peritum tuṇiyiṉ aḻukkup pōkkap payaṉpaṭuttuvatu.
cavaḷi 
perunakaraṅkaḷil ellām vaṇṇa vaṇṇa eḻuttukaḷil javuḷik kaṭikaḷ kāṇap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. uḷḷē irukkum vaṇṇat tuṇikaḷi yellām veḷippaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu pōl, paṭṭoḷi ceyyum palavaṇṇa miṉ viḷakkukaḷ ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, javuḷi eṉpatō peyarp palakiyil moḻik kolikku muṉṉōṭiyāy irukkiṉṟatu.
vaṇikap perumakkaḷ uḷḷam vaṇṭamiḻp pakkam cāravilliyē eṉṟa varuttattāl pāvēntar, tamiḻakattiṉ tamiḻt teruvil tamiḻtāṉ illi eṉṟār. urimam peṟutaṟkuk kaṭṭāyamākat tamiḻilum viḷamparap palaki irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu, āṇi piṟappitta piṉ, vēṇṭā veṟuppāy ōrañcāraṅkaḷil oṭṭuk kuṭittaṉam pōl vikkap peṟṟa tamiḻ viḷamparap palakiyilēyāvatu piḻiyillāt tamiḻ irukkac ceyya eṇṇam varak kūṭātā?
āṅkilattilē palaki eḻuta vēṇṭumāṉāl, atil teḷivuṭiyavarkaḷiṭam kēṭṭu eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. piḻi ēṟpaṭṭu viṭumāṉāl nāṇukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, tamiḻik keṭuppataṟku maṭṭum mottak kuttaki eṭuttavarkaḷpōl tāmum aṟiyāmal, aṟintavariṭamum kēḷāmal, tavaṟāmal piḻi ceykiṉṟaṉar! tamiḻttāy peṟṟa piḷḷikaḷukku avaḷi iḻivu ceyvataṟku urimi illiyā eṉṉa?
pālāṭiyō nūlāṭiyō eṉṟu aiyuṟumāṟu āṭi neytārkaḷām! puki pōlavum nuri pōlavum, pāmpiṉ uri pōlavum āṭi iruntatām. mūṅkiliṉ uḷḷē uḷḷa veṇpaṭalam pōlavum āṭi iruntatām. attakiya nākarikam vaḷarnta nāṭṭilē, avaṟṟi viṟkum kaṭikku oru peyar illāmalā pōyiṟṟu? maṭimaṭiyāka aṭukki vittu naṟumaṇam ūṭṭap peṟṟa kaṭikaḷē nirampiya vītiyē maturiyil iruntatāmē!
nūliṉum mayiriṉum nuḻinūṟ paṭṭiṉum
pālvaki teriyāp paṉṉū ṟaṭukkattu
naṟumaṭi ceṟinta aṟuvi vīti
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ (cilap. 14:205-207). ivvaṟuvi vīti, tuṇikkaṭikaḷē irunta perunterukkaḷē! aṟuvik kaṭi, tuṇikkaṭi, puṭavik kaṭi eṉṉum peyarkaḷittām tam kaṭikaḷukku vikkavilli! piḻiyillāt tamiḻilāvatu peyariṭa vēṇṭāvā?
javuḷik kaṭiyiṉar, ippeyari virumpi virumpi vippataṉ mayakkam oru pōli mayakkam! javuḷi eṉpatu vaṭacol eṉak koṇṭa mayakkam atu. cavaḷi eṉpatu tūya tamiḻc collākum.
cavaṭṭutal eṉpatu paḻantamiḻc col. aḻittal, tuṉpuṟuttal, varuttal, citittal, aṟital, vāṭṭutal mutaliya poruḷkaḷil atu varum. palkāl mellutalum cavaṭṭutalum ākum; palkāl mitittalum, avvāṟu cavaṭṭutalē ākum.
maṉpati cavaṭṭuṅ kūṟṟa muṉpa
eṉṟu patiṟṟup pattum (84),
vampa vaṭukar pintali cavaṭṭi
eṉṟu akamum (375),
aṟikkal iṟuvarimēl pāmpu cavaṭṭi
eṉṟu kārnāṟpatum (17),
pañcāyk kōri palliṟ cavaṭṭi
eṉṟu perumpāṇum (217) kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
maḻi cavaṭṭi viṭṭatu eṉṟum, nōy ūriyē vaḷittuc cavaṭṭiviṭṭatu eṉṟum, katiric cavaḷa mitikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, veṟṟiliyi ōyāmal meṉṟu, cavakkaḷittu viṭṭatu eṉṟum kūṟuvatu peritum vaḻakkattil uḷḷaṉa. cōrntavaṉ, melintavaṉ maḻuṅkiyavaṉ ākiyavaṉic cavaṅkal eṉṟum, cavaṇṭa payal eṉṟum paḻippatum vaḻakkē. cavaḷi (cavali)yi aṟiyār evar? cavali veṇpā eṉa oru pāvaki ilakkaṇattil iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭatē. iṭivarum nāṟcīraṭi oṉṟil muccīr varutal cavali veṇpāvām.
paṭāppāṭu paṭuttutal cavaṭṭutal eṉṟum, paṭāppāṭu paṭutal cavaḷutal eṉṟum mēlē kūṟiya iruvaki vaḻakkukaḷālum aṟiyalām.
nīr, muṭṭi mōti alittuk kulittu aḷḷi vantu pōṭappaṭṭa maṇ, cavaṭu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatum, accavaṭṭu maṇ, pāṉi mutaliyavi ceytaṟkum, ceṅkal ākkuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭuvatum yāvarum aṟintatē. cavaṭṭu maṇ koṇṭu mitittu nittup pāṉi vaṉivāric cavaḷikkārar eṉpatum vaḻakkē. aṉṟip pañcu nūlip pāvil iṭṭu aṭittup paciyēṟṟit tuṇi neyvāric cila pakutikaḷil cavaḷikkārar eṉpatum aṟiyat takkatē. poṉṉi urukki oḻuka viṭṭuk kampiyāka iḻuttup piṉṉal vēli ceyyum ōr aṇikkuc cavaṭi eṉṉum peyar veḷippaṭiyē.
neruppiliṭṭu oṉṟi vāṭṭac cavaḷi yākum (tuvaḷum) eṉṟum, nōyaṭippaṭṭu noynta piḷḷi (cavaḷi) eṉṟum, cavvuc cavveṉa vaḷivatic cavaḷal eṉṟum, cavaḷum vaḷāriyuṭiya puḷiya marattic cavaḷa maram eṉṟum, mitivaṇṭiyic cila iṭaṅkaḷil cavaṭṭu vaṇṭi eṉṟum, kaṭaliṉi aṭuttu mukavāyiṟ kiṭakkum karuṅkuṟu maṇalic cavaḷi eṉṟum, tōṇi celuttum kōlic cavaḷik kōl (tuṭuppu) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkāṟukaḷ yāvum nōkkat takkaṉa.
iṉip paruttikkup paṉṉal eṉṟoru peyar uṇṭu eṉpati ilakkiyavaḻi aṟiyalām. paṉṉal vēlip paṇinal lūrē eṉpatu puṟappāṭṭu (345); paṉṉal vēli iṉṉatu eṉa viḷakkuvatu pōlap parutti vēli eṉṟum puṟappāṭalkaḷ (299, 324) kūṟum. paṉṉal tarum pañciyum, nūlum paṉuval eṉap peṟṟaṉa. pañcittaṉ collāp paṉuval iḻiyāka eṉṟu āṭi nūl pōla āyvu nūli uruvakitturittalum vaḻakkāyatu.
cavaḷi eṉpatu, paṉṉal eṉpatu pōl paruttikkuriya peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpati, maruttuvak kaliccol viḷakkam ceykiṉṟatu. parutti iliyi nittu eṭukkap peṟṟa cāṟṟic cavaḷi eṉpatum maruttuva nūlōr vaḻakkē. cavaḷiyām paruttiyiṉiṉṟu eṭutta pañci nūlāl ceyyap peṟṟatum cavaṭṭic cavaṭṭip paṇi ceyyap peṟṟu uṇṭāyatum oṉṟap poruḷ poruntac cavaḷi eṉṟu muntiyōr peyariṭṭaṉar. accavaḷip peyaric cavuḷi yākki, ataṉ piṉṉar javuḷiyākkip paṇṭamāṟṟu vēli ceyyap paḻakip pōṉa perumakkaḷ eḻuttu māṟṟu vēliyit toṭarntu nōkkiya iṭattellām nīkkamaṟac ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ uṇarvuṭiya teḷivuṭiya cilar cavaḷikkaṭi eṉat tirutti vāṭikkiyākki viṭṭāl, ataṉip pārttēṉum oruvar oruvarāy uṇmi uṇarntu eṅkum cavaḷik kaṭikaḷāyk kāṇa vāykkum.
tuṇi eppaṭic cavaṭṭappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu viḷakka vēṇṭiyatu illi!
pañcunēr ulakap pāṭṭilē melinta pāviyēṉ
yāṉpaṭum pāṭu pañcutāṉ paṭumō
eṉpaṉa paṭṭaṟivāl colliya paruvaral moḻikaḷ.
pañcu paṭumpāṭu paṭāppāṭu tāṉē! maṇiyil iṭṭuk koṭṭi piritteṭutta pañcu, villāl puṭikkappaṭṭu, maṭṭiyāl uruṭṭappaṭṭu, nūlāy iḻikkappaṭṭu, nūlip pāvāy iḻukkappaṭṭu, paciyūṭṭappaṭṭu, mottum aṭiyum tarappaṭṭu, mitipaṭṭu, aṭipaṭṭu neyyappaṭṭu, piṉṉum piṉṉum aḻukkup pōka aṟipaṭṭu - nipaṭṭu - ettaṉi paṭṭup paṭṭāyk kiḻipaṭṭup pōkiṉṟatu! cavaṭṭap peṟavilliyā, ettuṇiyō vakikaḷil nilikaḷil? ataṉic cavaḷi eṉpatu caritāṉē!
cavittal 
palkāl meṉṟu cuvi koḷḷal, cavittalām. cavittaṟkeṉṟē caviyam eṉa oṉṟu (cūyiṅkam) kaṭikaḷil viṟpatu nām aṟintatē! cavvu miṭṭāy, eṉpatum cavittaṟkeṉṟu amintatē.
caḻintu cappaḷintu 
	caḻital		= 	neḷintu pōṉa oṉṟu mēlum 				neḷital caḻital ākum.
	cappaḷital	= 	caḻinta atucīrākka iyalā					vaṇṇam citivuṟutal 					cappaḷitalām.
neḷital eṉpatu vaḷital, tirukutal. oruporuḷ neḷintu pōṉāl ataṉi neḷivu eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttuvar. atu caḻintu pōṉāl atip paḻiya nilikkuk koṇṭu vara muṭiyā viṭṭālum payaṉpaṭuttum aḷavukkuc cīr ceyyalām. cappaḷittu pōṉāl mīṇṭum uruppaṭutta muṭiyātu. urukki vēṇṭumāṉāl māṟṟuruk koṭukkalām. īyam alumiṉiyak kalaṅkaḷē perumpālum caḻital  cappaḷitalkaḷukku āṭpaṭum.
caḷḷi 
caḷḷu caḷḷu eṉak kurittuc caṇṭi pōṭutal nāyiṉ kuṇam. oṉṟi oṉṟu kaṭippatum puraṭṭip puraṭṭi eṭuppatum kaṇṭa oruvar karuttil caḷḷi eṉṉum col uruvākip paraviyuḷḷatu. vārāt tuyar varutalum, tīrā nōyp paṭutalum caḷḷi eṉa makkaḷ vaḻakkil ūṉṟiyuḷḷatu.
caṟṟu 
ciṟu  > ciṟṟu  > caṟṟu = ciṟitu.
caṟṟēṉum ēṟumāṟāka eṉpatu auviyār taṉippāṭal. caṟṟunēram ciṟitu nēram eṉpatu kaṟṟōr vaḻakku. kallār peruvaḻakku cetta eṉpatāyiṟṟu. cetta nēram (ciṟitu nēram) poṟu eṉpar. ivvaḻakku aṟiyāviṭil, cetta nēram eṉpataṟku akarātiyil poruḷ tēṭiṉāl eṉṉa aṟivar?
caṉiyaṉ 
caṉikkōḷukkuriyavaṉākak kūṟappaṭupavaṉ caṉi. avaṉi, nōymukaṉ eṉṟār pāvāṇar. karimukaṉ eṉpāṉum avaṉ. kuccaṉ eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. caṉiyaṉ kōyil koṇṭa ūr kuccaṉūr eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. ciṉṉamaṉūr cārnta ūr; tēṉi māvaṭṭam. ēḻariyāṭṭiyāṉ eṉpatum caṉiyē.
caṉṉam 
poṉvēli ceyvār poṟpoṭiyic caṉṉam eṉpar. poṭi allatu tūḷ eṉṉum ciṟumip poruḷ taruvatu atu. kuṟiyō muyaṟci iṉmiyō iruntāl caṉṉam caṉṉamākac cariyākiviṭum eṉpatu nelli, mukavi vaḻakkākum. ciṟitu ciṟitāka eṉpatē ataṉ poruḷām. ciṉṉam eṉpatum ciṟitu eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatē.
* ciṉṉam kāṇka.

caṉṉal piṉṉal 
	caṉṉal	=	caṉṉamāka allatu mellitāka 				nīṇṭiruttal.
	piṉṉal	=	curuṇṭu piṉṉip piṇintu kiṭattal.
pāmpip paṟṟiya viṭukati oṉṟu. caṉṉal piṉṉal koṭi, cātiliṅkak koṭi, miṉṉi maṟiyum koṭi, eṉṉa koṭi? eṉavarum.
tōṭṭattil koṭikaḷ nerukkik kiṭantāl koṭi ippaṭic caṉṉal piṉṉalākak kiṭantāl eppaṭik kāykkum eṉa viṉāvuvar.

cā  varicik coṟkaḷ

cā 
cakara ākāram.  uyirmey neṭil.  ēval - cāvu.
cākāṭu 
cakaṭu  > cākāṭu = vaṇṭi.
cakaṭatti - cakkaratti - yuṭiyatu cākāṭu.
āric cākāṭ ṭāḻcci pōkkum
uraṉuṭi nōṉpakaṭ ṭaṉṉa eṅkōṉ	- puṟam. 60
cāṭu eṉpatum itu. cakaṭu  > cāṭu.
cākkutti 
cāva + kutti = cākkutti.
cāvakkutti eṉpatu  tokuttalāyc cākkutti yāyatu.
koḷvārik koḷvārik kōṭṭuvāyc cākkutti- kali. 105
poruḷ: taḻuvuvārit taḻuvuvārik kōṭṭiṭattē cāmpaṭi kutti nac.
cākkup pōkku 
	cākku 		= 	kuṟṟattit taṉakku vārāmal 					vēṟoruvar mēl pōṭṭut 					tapputal.
	pōkku 		= 	uriya vaḻiyi viṭṭu vēṟoru 				vaḻi kāṭṭi  allatu  pōkkuk 				kāṭṭit tapputal.
cāṭci eṉpatic cākki eṉpatum cāṭci col pavaṉic cākki eṉpatum iṉṟum naṭimuṟiyil uḷḷavi; cāṭṭu eṉpatu cākku eṉa varukiṉṟatām. piṟarip poṟuppāḷi yākkic cāṟṟutal  > cāṭṭutal (kūṟutal) cākku āyiṟṟām. cākkiṭutal eṉpatum atu. pōkku eṉpatu pōkkuk kāṭṭit tapputalām.
cākkōṭṭi 
cākkōṭṭi = kīḻē cāyntu viḻumāṟu talicuṟṟal.
karukkoṇṭa makaḷir cuṟṟalum vāntiyumāka irukkum niliyi macakki eṉpatu peruvaḻakku. iraṇiyal vaṭṭārattil karumayakkattic cākkōṭṭi eṉkiṉṟaṉar. nāvaṟaṭci, talicuṟṟu, kaṇmayakku, niṟkamuṭiyāmi ākiyavi kaṇṭu avalamāka irukkum niliyi ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar eṉalām.
macakki = mayakkam.
cāṅkiyam 
caṭaṅkiyam  > cāṅkiyam.
cāṅkiyam eṉpatu caṭaṅku eṉṉum poruḷil teṉtamiḻakam, koṅku ākiya pakutikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. cāṅkiyam perumpālum makaḷir muṉṉiṉṟu ceyyum caṭaṅkiyē ākum. caṭaṅkiyam eṉpatu cāṅkiyam eṉṟākiyatu eṉalām. iṉi, antiyūr vaṭṭārattil cāṅkiyam eṉpatu paḻamoḻi eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. caṭaṅkukaḷil vaḻivaḻiyākac collappaṭum toṭarkaḷi mutaṟkaṇ kuṟittu, piṉṉarp paḻamoḻiyik kuṟittatākalām.
cāṭā 
nelli ālaṅkuḷam vaṭṭārattil, pūrāṉ eṉṉum ūriyic cāṭā eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. itu, iyakka vakiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa uvami vaḻiyatu ākum.
pūrāṉ ūrntu cellutal, vaṇṭi cellum celavu pōṉṟatākum. cakaṭu  > cāṭu = vaṇṭi. cāṭu  > cāṭā. itaṉic cārā eṉṟu nākarkōyil vaṭṭārattilum, cārāṭi eṉat tirumaṅkala vaṭṭārattilum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. caracara eṉac cellutal vaḻippaṭṭavi cārā, cārāṭi eṉpavi.
cāṭi 
vāṭṭa cāṭṭam eṉpavi iṇimoḻikaḷ. vāṭṭam vāṭi eṉṟum, cāṭṭam cāṭi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. mara aṟuvi iṭam vāṭi eṉappaṭṭatu (maravāṭi) pōl amintatu cāṭi. cāṭutal ōṭutal, pukutal. naṇṭu vaḷiyik kīḻappāvūr vaṭṭārattār cāṭi eṉpar. ivvāṭci kūriya pārviyum koḷḷum poruḷamitiyum uṭiyatākum.

cāṭikku ēṟṟa mūṭi 
cāṭikku ēṟṟa mūṭi = kaṇavaṉukku ēṟṟa maṉivi.
kalattiṉ mēlvāyum, mūṭiyiṉ uḷvāyum poruntiyamiyac ceyyappaṭum. ataṉiyē cāṭikku ēṟṟa mūṭi eṉal vaḻakku. ceppiṉ puṇarcci eṉa naṭpiyalik kūṟuvār tiruvaḷḷuvar (887). kuṭumpattil kaṇavaṉ karuttukku oppi naṭakkum maṉivi vāyttu viṭṭāl, avaḷic cāṭikku ēṟṟa mūṭi eṉac colvatu vaḻakkamām. nalla karuttā; alla karuttā eṉpatu paṟṟiyataṉṟu ceyti. iruvar karuttum otta karuttu eṉpatē kuṟippu. piṟarkku avarkaḷ ceyaṟpāṭu ettakiya tāṉālum avarkaḷukkuḷ oru piṇippu iruttal avarkaḷ vāḻvukkut takka oṉṟutāṉē.
cāṭi 
ivarip pārttāl avar cāṭiyāka irukkiṟār illiyā? eṉa aiyuṟṟu viṉāvuvār uḷar. cāṭi oppup poruḷil cāyalik kuṟittu varukiṉṟatu. itu nelli, kumari, mukavi ākiya teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakkākum. cāṭi eṉpatu uvami urupoṭu oṭṭakkūṭiyatu.
cakaṭi = vaṇṭic cakkaram; iraṇṭum oppāṉavi yātal kaṇkūṭu. atu koṇṭu cakaṭi  > cāṭi ākiyirukkalām. rfL> cāṭu = vaṇṭi.
cāṭiyam 
cāṭi eṉpatu pōla eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatu ātalāl cāṭiyam eṉpatum ataṉi oppap poruḷ koṇṭatu. oruvar naṭiyuṭi oppak koṇṭu mēṭi yēṟṟuvatu nāṭakam (kūttu) ākum. tiṇṭukkal vaṭṭārattil naṭikar uṭiyic cāṭiyam eṉpatu vaḻakkākum. oppa aminta uṭikkōlamē kaṇṭatum aṭiyāḷam kāṇavikkum. ariya col vaḻakku ītām.
* cāṭi kāṇka.
cāṭṭuk kūṭi 
pirampuk kūṭi eṉpatu nelli vaṭṭāra vaḻakkil cāṭṭuk kūṭi eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. uḻavar payaṉpaṭuttum tārkkōl, cāṭṭikkōl eṉappaṭal uṇṭu. cāṭṭikkōl eṉpatu mūṅkil pirampē ākum. ātalāl, mūṅkil pirampu cāṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa kūṭi, cāṭṭik kūṭi āyatu. cāṭṭi, nīḷamākavum vaḷintum cellutal. itu kaṭṭuk koṭi, mūṅkil mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iyaṟki.
cāṭṭi 
cāṭṭik kampu allatu cāṭṭik kōl nīḷattiṉum atil kaṭṭappaṭṭa vār nīṇṭirukkum. vār = nīḷam. ātalāl cāṭṭi eṉpataṟku nīḷam eṉṉum poruḷic cempaṭṭi vaṭṭārattār vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. kampil iruntu toṅkum vāriṉ toṅkutal nīḷam āyavi kuṟittu oppumip poruḷ vakiyāl, caṭi eṉa nākarkōyil vaṭṭārattār vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
cāṇam 
cāṇakam, cāṇam, cāṇi eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaṉa oru poruḷaṉa. civaṉiya aṭiyāḷamām tirunīṟṟukku mūlap poruḷ āviṉ cāṇamām.
cāṇakamām cāṇam, maruttuvap payaṉ uṭiyatu. puṇ, vīkkam āyavi uṇṭāṉāl avaṟṟi āṟṟac cāṇa oṟṟaṭam koṭuppatu ciṟṟūr vaḻakkam. yāṉiyiṉ cāṇakam puṇṇi āṟṟumeṉa ataṉi mitippār uṇṭu. puṇṇāṟṟum āṟṟal cāṇattiṟku iruppatāl cāṇam eṉpataṟkut taḻumpu - vaṭu - eṉṉum peyar paṇṭē uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
kōṇam tiṉṟa vaṭuvāḻ mukatta
cāṇam tiṉṟa camantāṅku taṭakki	- maturik. 592- 593
cāṇi 
veṅkāyattik keṭāmal pātukākka vikkum aṭitaṭṭiyip paṇṭaṭi eṉpatuṭaṉ cāṇi eṉpatum uṇṭu. atu mukavi vaḻakku. cāli paṉṉacāli eṉpavi ili taḻikaḷāl ākkappaṭupavi. cāli  > cāḷi  > cāṇi ākiya vaṭivam itu.
teṉṉik kīṟṟut taṭṭiyāl kūṭupōl ceyyappaṭuvatu atu. cila tiṅkaḷ aḷavukkup pātukākka utavuvatu. paṇṭaṭi, cāṇi eṉpavi vēḷāṇ vaḻakku.
cātal 
cā  > cāy  > cāytal  > cātal.
katircāyntu maṟital pōl, niṉṟāṉ iruntāṉ kiṭantāṉ eṉac cāyntu mūccaṟṟup pōyviṭal cātal ākum.
cātal iyaṟkiyik katir kāṭṭuvatip 	puṟanāṉūṟu kāṭṭum (27).
nāḷeṉa oṉṟupōl kāṭṭi uyirīrum vāḷ 	eṉak kāṭṭum 			tirukkuṟaḷ (334).
ivi cāytal - cātal - viḷakkamām. cātal iyaṟki eṉpatic,
cātalum putuva taṉṟē	- puṟam. 192
eṉpati ulakam kāṭṭum.
iṟantāri eṇṇik koṇṭaṟṟu	- tiruk. 22
cātaliṉ iṉṉāta tilli	- tiruk. 230
eṉpavi cātal viḷakkaṅkaḷām.

cāttu 
cāttu:1
eṟumpukaḷ varici variciyākap pōkum. eṟumpiṉ varici eṟumpuc cāri eṉappaṭum. oṉṟic cārntu oṉṟu celvatāl cāri eṉavum, cāri eṉavum vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa.
cāttu:2
cāttu eṉpatu vaṇikak kūṭṭam; cāttaṉ, vaṇikaṉ. kōvalaṉ tanti mācāttaṉ, peruvaṇikaṉ. avaṉ, kāl naṭikaḷ kālkaḷiyuṭiya ūrtikaḷ vaḻiyē (kāl - cakkaram) vaṇikam ceytavaṉ. nilavaṇikaṉ.
kalattiṉum kāliṉum taruvaṉar īṭṭak
kulattil kuṉṟāk koḻuṅkuṭic celvar	- cilap. 2:7-8

cārttu, cāttu, cārtal cērtal eṉpār pāvāṇar (paṇṭit tamiḻa nākarikamum paṇpāṭum.) cāttu vaḻivanta cāttaṉām teyvam, piṉṉar aiyaṉār eṉappaṭṭār.
cāttoṭu pōntu taṉittuyar uḻantēṉ	- cilap. 11:190
cāntam 
cāntuk kalavi tarum maram cāntam eṉpatuṭaṉ ataṉ naṟumaṇamum, palarum virumpip pūcum iyalpum palarum virumpum poṟumi amiti āya paṇpik kuṟikkum kalic collāyiṟṟu.

* cāntu kāṇka.
cāntāṟṟi 
cāntu + āṟṟi = cāntāṟṟi.
cāntu nīrāḷamākap pūcappaṭuvatu; kuḷircciyāṉatu. ataṉāl pūcappaṭṭa atu uṭaṉ ularvataṟkākak kāṟṟi viciṟiyāl ākki ularac ceyvatu vaḻakkam. avviciṟikkuc cāntāṟṟi eṉpatu peyar. akaṟṟi = āṟṟi; nīrppatatti akalac ceyvatu.
* viciṟi kāṇka.
cāntu 
cāntu:1
cārntu  > cāntu = cantaṉa maṇam.
cāntiṉ naṟumaṇam maramutirvāl maṭṭum ēṟpaṭuvatu aṉṟu; ataṉvēr maṟṟi maṟṟi vērkaḷoṭu piṉṉip piṇintu kiṭakkumāṉāltāṉ mikumaṇamākum eṉpar. ataṉ vaḻiyākap peṟṟa peyar cāntu.
cāntu  > cantu  > cantaṉam. cantaṉam koṭuttu varavēṟṟalum pūci viṭutalum varavēṟpum makiḻvum kāṭṭum cāṉṟākaliṉ amitip poruḷum tantatu. cantu eṉpatu iruvari iṇittal eṉavum āyatu.
cāntu:2
cāntu  > cantu  > cantaṉam.
kaṉmāviyō, (kal māvu) cuṇṇāmpiyō nīrviṭṭuk kuḻippatic cāntu eṉṟu makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkam. cāntu eṉpatu arittuk kuḻikkappaṭuvatu eṉpatit teḷivikkum.
cantaṉak kalavi, cantaṉak karical, cantaṉak kaṭṭi eṉpaviyum cantaṉak kaṭṭiyik kallil tēyttuk kuḻampākkip pūcutalum cāntiṉ poruḷ viḷakkamām.
cāppāṭu pōṭal 
cāppāṭu pōṭal = tirumaṇam ceytal.
naṇparkaḷukkuḷḷum aṉparkaḷukkuḷḷum tirumaṇa akavi uṭiyavarkaḷeṉiṉ, eṉṉa eppoḻutu cāppāṭu pōṭa eṇṇam? pōkiṟa pōkkip pārttāl cāppāṭu pōṭum eṇṇamē illiyā? eṉa viṉavum cāppāṭu, tirumaṇap poruṭṭatām.
tirumaṇam eṉṟālē palavakik kaṟikaḷ kaṇṇamutu (pāyacam) appaḷam vaṭiyuṭaṉ cāppāṭṭuc ciṟappē peruñciṟappākap pēcappaṭuvatākaliṉ cāppāṭē tirumaṇap poruḷ taruvatāyiṟṟu. tāli kaṭṭu muṭintāl ili muṉṉarttāṉ palarip pārkkalām! avvaḷavu pāṭu, cāppāṭu.
cāppāṭum kūppāṭum 
cāppāṭu = palar kūṭic cāppiṭutal.
kūppāṭu  = palar kūṭic cāppiṭum pōtu uṇṭākum 				   pēroli.
cappu, cappiṭutal eṉpavi cāppāṭṭukku mūlam. kū, kūvutal, kūppiṭu, kūppīṭu, kūppāṭu eṉpavi kūppāṭṭiṉ mūlam.
palar cērntu cāppiṭum pōtu, itik koṇṭu vā, atik koṇṭu vā eṉṟu kūppiṭuvatum, palarum oru vēḷiyil kūppiṭutal uriyāṭutal, irital mutaliyavi nikaḻttuvatum kūppāṭu ākkukiṉṟatām. cilar cāppāṭu pōṭuvar; cāppāṭu pōṭum pōtē tiṭṭavum ceyvar. ataṉāl cāppāṭum vēṇṭām kūppāṭum vēṇṭām eṉṟu veṟuttuk kūṟum niliyum uṇṭu.
cāppiṭutal 
cappuc cappeṉa vāyoli eḻumāṟu uṇṇutal cāppiṭutal ākum. cappiṭutal, cappīṭu - cāppāṭu eṉa vantatu. naṉṟākac cāppiṭupavaṉi allatu cāppāṭē kuṟiyāka iruppavaṉic cāppāṭṭu rāmaṉ eṉpatu ikkāla vaḻakku.
cāmpal 
cāmpal:1
oṉṟu erintu aḻipaṭṭataṉ poṭi,- cāmpal. uyir pōy uṭal aḻipaṭal, -cātal.
viṟaku eru mutaliyavi eripaṭṭu aḻintāl avaṟṟiṉ eri tukaḷ cāmpal eṉappaṭum. uṭalum kaṭṭi eru āyavaṟṟoṭu aṭukki vikkappaṭṭu eriyūṭṭap paṭutalāl appoṭiyum cāmpal eṉappaṭṭatu.
māntar vāḻvu kaṭiciyil oru piṭi cāmpaltāṉ eṉa makkaḷ evarum colvar.
cāmpal,  ceṭi koṭikaḷukku maruntum uramumām. māntar uṭal erinta cāmpal karikkavum vaḻipāṭṭukku vikkavum niṉiviṭam eḻuppavumāka vaḻakkil ūṉṟiyuḷḷatu.
cāmpal:2
oṉṟaṉ koḻumi vāṭutal cāmputal (cāmpal) eṉappaṭum. kāy cāmpip pōyviṭṭatu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
cāmpal:3
cāmpal niṟam. veṇmiyum karumiyum kalantatu.
cāmpal niṟam orukuṭṭi	- pārati.
cāmpal pūcuṇi eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
cāmpal:4
talivaṉ pirivāl talivikku uṭalil uṇṭām pacali cāmpal eṉappaṭum. (ma.va.)
cāmpu 
campu  > cāmpu.
campu eṉpatu pulliṉ vakiyatu. ataṉip paṟittup parappip paṭukkiyākak koṇṭaṉar muntiyar. ataṉāl cāmpu eṉpatu paṭukki eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatāyiṟṟu. cāytal paṭuttal; cāytalukkup payaṉpaṭṭatu cāympu; cāmpumām.
ippoḻutum kōrippāy eṉpatu kōrip pullik koṇṭu neyyap paṭṭa paṭukkiyēyām. pāy palvēṟiṭaṅ kaḷil aḻaku aḻakāka neyyappaṭṭu vilimāṉa mikkatum vaṇṇakkōla vaṉappuṭiyatumāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
kācumīrappaṭṭu, kāñcippaṭṭu eṉpavi pōl teṉtamiḻakattil pattamaṭip pāykkut taṉic ciṟappuṇṭu.
