co cō ña  varicic coṟkaḷ

cokiṉam 
cokiṉam >  cakuṉam.
oru ceyalit toṭaṅka allatu ōriṭattiṟkuc cellap pārkkum kuṟi cokiṉam. caṅkac cāṉṟōr oruvar peyar tumpic cokiṉaṉār. cokiṉam pārttal cakuṉam pārttalāka vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
tumpic cokiṉaṉār	 (puṟam. 249 auvi. cu.tu.)
collum kuṟiyāka iruttalāl cokuṉam >  cokiṉam āki yirukkalām.
cokucu 
iyalpāka aminta amivilum uyariya amivum kavarcciyum nalamum aminta vāḻvu cokucu vāḻvu eṉappaṭṭatu. uḻippu illāmal vāḻpavar cokucu āṉa vāḻviṉar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. itukāl pēruntukaḷil cokucu vaṇṭi eṉṉum peyar paḷicciṭuvatuṭaṉ pala maṭaṅkuk kaṭṭaṇam vāṅkavum vikkiṉṟatu.
cokka - kavara - vippatu cokucu eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu eṉka. paḻanāḷil paṇakkārar cokucu vāḻviṉar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ippoḻutu cokucu vaṇṭi ulā varāta iṭamilli.
cokkappaṉi 
kōyil viḻākkaḷiṉ pōtu koḷuttappaṭum nikaḻccikaḷuḷ oṉṟu cokkappaṉi eṉpatu. paṉi maram oṉṟaṉi niṟutti, ataṉ maṭṭi ōli mutaliyavaṟṟāl cuṟṟum cūḻa mūṭi, tīmūṭṭi erippatu cokkappaṉi yākum. itu mukavi nelli vaḻakku. oḷiyāl kavarntu cokka vikkum paṉi cokkappaṉi.
cokkam 
kaṇṭārik kavarum uyarnta poṉ.
cokkap poṉ māṟṟuk kuṟiyāp poṉ. cokkaṉ= cokka vippavaṉ. uḷḷaṅ kavar kaḷvaṉ tēvā. ñāṉa. cokki = peṇpāl.
piṟari mayakkuvataṟku yārō cokkuppoṭi pōṭṭu viṭṭār eṉpatu ma.va.

cokkaṉ 
cokku eṉpatu aḻaku, viruppu mutaliya poruḷatu. cokkaṉ eṉpatu āṭ peyarāka illāmal kuraṅku eṉṉum poruḷil viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkil uḷḷatu - cokkutal, vaḷintu kūṉiyiruttal poruḷatākum. ataṉāl kuraṅkukku ākiyatu.
cokku 
cokka vippatu yātu? ataṉ peyar cokku cōmacuntararic cokkaṉ eṉṟē kuṟikkum nampi tiruviḷiyāṭal. ac cokkaṉ āṭṭattil cokkiyavaḷ cokkiyām umiyammi!
kavarvatu, miyūṭṭuvatu, tūyatu eṉṉum poruḷ tarum cokku eṉṉum col, attakavāl aḻakiyum cuṭṭuvatām.
coṅkal 
āḻam eṉṉum poruḷil cīrkāḻi vaṭṭārattil coṅkam eṉṉum col vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. curaṅkam eṉṉum tuḷittal poruṭcol coṅkam, coṅkal eṉṟāki irukkalām.
coṅki 
uḷḷīṭu aṟṟatip patar eṉpatu potuvaḻakku. uḷḷīṭu illāta katiric coṅku eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku. coṅki eṉpatu payaṉaṟṟavaṉ eṉṉum paḻippup poruḷil nelli vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. cōḷattiṉ mēloṭṭiya tōl coṅku eṉappaṭum. ataṉāl coṅkuc cōḷam eṉṟoru cōḷa vakiyum uṇṭu.
coṅku cōki 
	coṅku	= 	tavaca maṇiyiṉ mēl oṭṭiyuḷḷa pakku.
	cōki	= 	karumpu cōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tōki.
coṅku nirampa uṭiya cōḷam coṅkuc cōḷam eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atu civappuc cōḷam eṉavum peṟum. ataṉ coṅku peritākavum civantum irukkum.
cōki eṉpatu tōki eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatām. tōki eṉpatē cōki yāyiṟṟu eṉak koḷka.
coṭi 
veṭippu eṉpatu cuṟucuṟuppu eṉappaṭum. itu potu vaḻakku. coṭi eṉpatu cuṟucuṟuppu eṉṉum poruḷil kumari māvaṭṭa vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. coṭital eṉpatu payirkaḷiṉ katir kāyntu vaḷital poruḷil mukavi, nelli vaḻakkukaḷil uṇṭu.
coṭṭi 
coṭṭutal, tuḷituḷiyāka nīr viṭutal ākum. coṭṭuc coṭṭāka eṇṇi nāṉku coṭṭu viṭṭuk kuṭi eṉpatu maruttuva vaḻakku. payir nilattiṉ oru pakutiyil muḷiyāmalō, karukiyō pōyviṭṭāl eṉṉa coṭṭiyā? eṉpar. muṭi muḻumiyāka utirtal moṭṭi. āṅku āṅku utirntu vaḻukki ātal coṭṭi. mayir coṭṭiviṭṭatāl ēṟpaṭṭa peyar. itu potuvaḻakkākum.
coṇṭu 
coḷ >  coṇ >  coṇṭu. coḷ = arippu; arikkum pūcci.
taliyilum uṭaltōlilum arippu ākkum tōlpaṭi coṇṭu ākum. atu coṟiyac ceytalāl coṇṭu coṟi eṉṉum iṇiccollāyatu. avvāṟē coḷḷum coṇṭum eṉpatum iṇiccol āyatu. coṇṭu, taliyil uṇṭākum veṇṇiṟa nuṇpaṭar.
coṇṭu coḷḷi 
	coṇṭu	= kāykaṟi paḻaṅkaḷiṉ veḷiyēyuḷḷa cuṇi, pakku, veṭippu mutaliyavi coṇṭu eṉappaṭum.
	coḷḷi	= 	avaṟṟiṉ uḷḷē amintuḷḷa kēṭu coḷḷi eṉappaṭum.
cuṇi mikka oṉṟu pūcuṇi eṉpati aṟika. ataṉ kāmpu ilikaḷil muḷpōl aminta amippiyum veṇṇiṟac cuṇiyiyum karutuka. cuṇipaṭutalāl arippu uṇṭākum. arippu uṇṭākāmi cuṇi keṭṭatām. māṉa maṟṟavaṉic cūṭu coraṇi illātavaṉ eṉpatāl aṟika. coraṇiyum cuṇiyum oppām.

coṇṭu coṟi 
	coṇṭu	=	tōlil pakkuk kiḷamputal.
	coṟi	= 	tiṉavuṇṭākkum pori kiḷamputal.
tōlil uṇṭākum pakku coṟiyum pōtu utirum. mīṉiṉ uṭalil uḷḷa cetil pōlvatu atu. tōl vaṇṇattiṟkum ataṟkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
coṟi eṉpatu coṟi ciraṅkiṉ mūlamāṉatu. vērkkuruviṟkum coṟikkum vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. āyiṉ iraṇṭum uṭal vetuppāl ēṟpaṭuvaṉavēyām.
coti 
nelli vaṭṭāra viruntukaḷil taṉic ciṟappāka iṭam peṟuvatu coti eṉpatām. kaṭṭiyākavō, kaḷiyākavō, cāṟākavō, nīrākavō illāmal cota cotappāka - iḷamiyāṉa kūḻppatamāka aminta cuviyuṇavu - kaṇṇamutu pōṉṟatu - cotiyākum. coti yillāmalā viruntu eṉpatu paḻippuc col.
cotti coḷḷi 
	cotti	= 	puṟattē ōṭṭi pottal mutaliyavi uṭiya kāykaṟikaḷ.
	coḷḷi	= 	veḷiyē teriyāmal uḷḷē keṭṭup pōṉa kāykaṟikaḷ.
cotti eṉpatu cūṉ eṉṟum cūṉam eṉṟum cūtti eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
coḷḷi uḷḷē kēṭuṭiyateṉpatu veḷiyē pārttāl veḷḷi; uḷḷē pārttāl coḷḷi eṉṉum paḻamoḻi viḷakkum.
contak kālil niṟṟal 
kāl uṟuppuk kuṟi illāp piṟaviyar evarum contak kālil niṟpavarē. avarkaḷukku ūṉṟukōl, taḷḷukūṭu vēṇṭuvatu illi. avarkaḷ contak kālil niṟpār eṉak kūṟappaṭuvatu ilar. tam varuvāyāl tām piṟari etirpārāmal tam iyalpāṉa vāḻvukkākap piṟari nāṭāmal vāḻpavar evarō avarē contakkālil niṟpavarām.
avaṉ kaṟṟeṉṉa? celvam uṟṟatumeṉṉa? contak kālil niṟkat tiṟam keṭṭavaṉ eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
contam 
com + tam = contam.
tam cottāka matikkattakkavarkaḷ - vāḻpavarkaḷ - evarō avartam contam eṉappaṭuvar. cottik kavara iruppavarkaḷ allar ac contam eṉappaṭupavar. tam cottu utavuvatu pōl utavuvārē contam eṉpavarām.
com 
com:1
com eṉpatu paḻañcottu eṉṉum poruḷil ilakkiya vaḻakkuc collākum. apporuḷil māṟāmal kumari māvaṭṭak kōṭṭūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakkil com eṉpatu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
com:2
meṉmik kuṟippu.
pantu, commeṉa vīḻtal kaṇṭāḷ	- kuṇṭala. 46
corukucaṭṭi 
oru caṭṭiyuḷ iṉṉoru caṭṭi vēṟoru caṭṭi eṉap pala caṭṭikaḷi uḷḷaṭakki vippatum, atit tūkkākap payaṉpaṭuttuvatum vaḻakkam. aṭukkuc caṭṭi eṉṉum potuvaḻakkuṭiya ataṉic coruku caṭṭi eṉpatu ceṭṭināṭṭu vaḻakkākum.
colittu 
mūṅkiliṉ uḷḷē veṇṇiṟattatāyp paṭṭup pōl paḷicciṭuvatāyp paṭintuḷḷa poruḷi ataṉ oḷiyāl colittu eṉṟaṉar. paṭṭunūl evvāṟu iyaṟkik koṭiyō avvāṟu kiṭitta koṭiyē colittu. piṉṉē paḷapaḷappu oḷiyuṭiyavi colippatākiṉa. jūvāli vaḻiyāka ēṟpaṭṭa jolippu vaṭacol. colittu vaḻiyāka ēṟpaṭṭa colippu tamiḻccollēyām.
colittu	- ciṟupāṇ. 236
colucu 
mukaṭṭil iruntu tūmpu vaḻiyāka iṟaṅki vaḻiyumnīr viḻum iṭattic colucu eṉpatu tiruvātavūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. calacala, caḷacaḷa eṉpavi pōlac colacola eṉa olikkuṟippākic colicu colucu eṉa vaḻakkil vantirukkalām.
colu coluṉṉu maḻi peykiṟatu eṉpatu cūrampaṭṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. coluñ columṉṉu maḻi peykiṟatu eṉpatu kumuḷūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku.
col 
col eṉpatu palaporuḷ oru col. ataṟkuriya pala poruḷkaḷuḷ nel eṉpatum oṉṟākum.
nellil pāl piṭiyāp patar allatu pataṭiyi evarum nel eṉṉār; pālpiṭittu maṇi tiraḷāta arikkāyi nel eṉṉār; maṇi tiraṇṭum kaṟuttuc ciṟutta karukkāyi nel eṉṉār. maṇi tiraṇṭatiyē nel eṉpār! annellē colliṉ poruḷi uvami vakiyāl viḷakka vallatām. poruḷaṟṟatu collaṉṟām. ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (640).
col (nel) cūl, pacum pāmpāyt tōṉṟutaliyum, atu maṇi piṭikkumuṉ takavilāc celvar pōl tali nimirntu niṟṟaliyum, maṇi mutirnta piṉṉē kalvitēr māntar pōl tali tāḻntu vaḷitaliyum uvamiyāl cuṭṭiṉār tiruttakka tēvar (cīvaka. 53).
payaṉilāc collic colvāṉiyum ataṉik kēṭpāṉiyum ataṉi naṉṟeṉa nayantu pārāṭṭuvāṉiyum oruṅkē pataṭi eṉac cuṭṭiṉār poyyāmoḻiyār (196). payaṉaṟṟa nāḷip pataṭi vikal eṉṟa cāṉṟōr peyarē pataṭi vikalār āyatu (kuṟun. 323).
payaṉila collāmi cuṭṭiya vaḷḷuvarē, colvaṉmi kūṟiyatu coṟpayaṉ karutiyēyām. ātalāl, col eṉpatu maṇi pōṉṟatu eṉpatum. uḷḷīṭu uṭiyatu eṉpatum viḷaṅkum. colliyum nelliyum oppak kuṟikkum col eṉpatālē muṉṉōr colliya poruḷum viḷakkamum iviyām.
collākiya nelmaṇiyāl uṇavukkuc coṉṟi eṉpatu peyar. cōṟu eṉpatum col eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka vanta peyarē. nellāl ākkappaṭṭatiyē kuṟitta cōṟu eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar. piṉṉar et tavacattāl ākkiya uṇaviyum potuvākak kuṟikkum collāka virintatu. avvaḷavilum nillāmal kaḷḷic cōṟu, kaṟṟāḻañ cōṟu eṉṟum virintatu! cōṟṟuk kaṟṟāḻi eṉṉum peyariyum vaḻaṅkiyatu! col vaḻi vanta cōṟṟiṉ āṭcikaḷ ivi. collutal vakikaḷik kuṟikka ēṟattāḻa nāṟpatu coṟkaḷ tamiḻil uḷa eṉpatip paṭṭiyaliṭṭuk kāṭṭukiṟār pāvāṇar (paṇṭit tamiḻa nākarikamum paṇpāṭum pak. 27,28).
colliṉ āṭci eppaṭi eppaṭi yellām tikaḻkiṉṟatu! avarip pārttēṉ; vāy tiṟantu oru col kūṭac collavilli; avvaḷavu cerukku eṉṟu paḻi colvatu illiyā? itaṉāl, paṇpāṭṭiṉ ciṉṉam col eṉpatu pulappaṭum.
nīṅkaḷ oru col coṉṉāl pōtum; kaṭṭāyam naṭantuviṭum eṉpatil colvākkiṉ celvākku veḷippaṭukiṉṟatē!
coṟṟuṇi mika uyarnta tuṇiyām! taṉṉan taṉiyē kāṭṭu vaḻi pōkiṉṟavaṉukkuc coṟṟuṇi pōl entat tuṇiyum utavuvatu illiyē! avaṉ pāṭic collum collē avaṉukkut tuṇiyāvatu arumi allavō!
coṟṟuṇi eṉpatu pēccut tuṇi tāṉē. atu tāṉē, nāttuṇi vāyttuṇi eṉpaṉavum! coṟkō, colliṉ vēntar, nāvukkaracar, vākkiṉ vēntar eṉṟellām collappaṭum apparaṭikaḷ, iṟivaṉic coṟṟuṇi vētiyaṉāka allavō kaṇṭār! aruṇakiriyārkkuc cantac col coṭṭac coṭṭa vantatāl allavō, vākkiṟku aruṇakiri āṉār.
oruttikkup pukunta vīṭṭil oru pēccuttuṇi kaṭṭāyam vēṇṭum. avaḷ taṉṉi otta allatu iḷiya akaviyiṉaḷākavum vēṇṭum; akkuṭumpattiṉ uṟuppākavum vēṇṭum. ivvellām amintavaḷ taṉ koḻunaṉ uṭaṉpiṟanta taṅki eṉpatu evvaḷavu poruttam! avaḷukku oru peyar nāttuṇiyāḷ eṉpatē atu. innāḷil nāttuṇāḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷār itaṉi, nāttūṇ naṅki eṉpār (cilap. 16:19).
uḷḷaṅ kalantu uriyāṭum tuṇi uṇṭu; uṟṟuḻi utavi uvakiyūṭṭum tuṇiyum uṇṭu. attuṇikaḷ muṟiyē ucāttuṇi acāttuṇi eṉappaṭum. ivi paḻamiyāṉa collāṭcikaḷ.
nūlukku aḻakukaḷ iviyeṉap pattuk kūṟuvar. muṉṉi aḻakukaḷuḷ taliyāyatu curuṅkac colal; piṉṉik kuṟikaḷuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉatu kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉappaṭum. nūlaḻakē collaḻaku eṉpatu kuṟippām.
pāṭṭiyal nūlkaḷilē āṉantak kuṟṟam eṉa oṉṟu collappaṭum. āṉantak kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ collāṉantam eṉpatu coṟkuṟṟamēyām.
maṭṭu, tēṟal, ūṟal, kuṭi, kaḷ, matu ippaṭi ettaṉi peyarkaḷ iruntālum, taṇṇīr, veḷḷik kutiri eṉa ettaṉi māṟṟup peyarkaḷ iruntālum kaṭkuṭiyar vaḻakkamāka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil collappaṭuvatu colviḷampi eṉpatām. kuṭiyarkaḷ ēṉ, mayakkum kaḷḷic colviḷampi eṉṟu taṅkaḷukkuḷ collik koḷḷa vēṇṭum?
kuṭittavaṉ taṉ vāyil vantati ellām uḷaṟi viṭuvāṉ allaṉō! maṟikka vēṇṭiyatu - collak kūṭātatu - eṉa avaṉ aṭakkik koḷḷa māṭṭāmal aviḻttu viṭṭu viṭuvāṉ allavō! ataṉi veḷippaṭuttum poruṭ peyarē colviḷampi eṉka!
col eṉpataṟkuk kaḷ eṉpatum oru poruḷ. taṉ piḷḷiyi ūrār mecciviṭṭāl kaḷ veṟi ēṟum eṉpatu nāmaṟiyāta pāṭṭā? kuṭikkāmalē, colveṟiyil ettaṉi pērkaḷ, āṭāta āṭṭamellām āṭak kaṇṇēril kāṇkiṉṟōm allavō!
teṉṉuṇ tēṉiṉ ceñcol eṉṟār kalviyil periyavar. avarē collokkum kaṭiya vēkac cuṭucarattiyum, collalaṅkārap paṇpākac cellum ampukaḷiyum puṉintār. villampiṟ collampē mēl eṉṟu avar pāṭiya taṉip pāṭṭoṉṟum kāṭṭum! vaḷḷuvarō, col vallāḷaric collē ruḻavarākac cuṭṭiṉār; uḻavu pāṭiya kiḻavar avarallarō!
cummā iru, collaṟa eṉpatum collāl muḻakkilō cukamilli eṉpatum collic collit taḻumpēṟiyavarkaḷ, colvati ellām colliviṭṭu, aminta niṟinili. ataṟkum kūṭa, collē vēṇṭi vantatu! ivi colliṉ perumi eṉpatiyaṉṟi vēṟeṉṉa? ataṉāltāṉ, collāta col - maṟic col eṉa akaramutalikaḷ colkiṉṟaṉa.
colliṉ vakikaḷic colli muṭiyātu! vallāṉ vakuttatē vāykkāl eṉpatu collukkum poruntuvatē! colli eṇṇi eppaṭik kaṇakkiṭṭu viṭa muṭiyum? viṇṇaka mīṉi eṇṇuvatu pōṉṟa muṭivaṟṟa vēliyākavē muṭiyum?
col amivum poruḷ virivum 
pāvāṇar aṭi eṉum collic ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya viṉiyākak koṇṭu 49 poruḷkaḷiyum, ceyap paṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāta viṉiyākak koṇṭu 64 poruḷ kaḷiyum, peyarccollākak koṇṭu 32 poruḷkaḷiyum kūṟukiṉṟār.
aṭittal: (ce.ku.vi.)
	1.	viḷiyāṭutal: kuṇṭaṭittal, kōliyaṭittal, noṇṭiyaṭittal.
	2.	taṉiyāka allatu kūṭik kūttāṭutal: kūttaṭittal, 	kummiyaṭittal.
	3.	tuṭittal: irunāḷāy eṉakkuk kaṇṇaṭittuk koḷkiṟatu.
	4.	cāṭi kāṭṭutal: avaṉ avaḷip pārttuk kaṇṇaṭikkiṟāṉ.
	5.	uṭampu cuṭutal: avaṉukkuk kāyccal aṭikkiṟatu.
	6.	pūcutal: vīṭṭiṟku veḷḷiyaṭikka vēṇṭum.
	7.	alapputal: nāḷ muḻuvatum araṭṭiyaṭikkiṟāṉ (ulakavaḻakku).
	8.	acital: vēṭṭi kāṟṟil paṭapaṭaveṉṟu aṭik kiṟatu  (u.va.).
	9.	maṇiyaṭittal: kōyil maṇiyaṭikkiṟatu.
	10.	kāṟṟu vīcutal: puyaṟkāṟṟaṭikkiṟatu.
	11.	mūkkiṟkup pulaṉātal: pūkkaṭikkup pōṉāl nalla maṇamaṭikkiṟatu.
	12.	oḷirtal: miṉṉal aṭikkiṟatu.
	13.	ākka  vāyppu (atirṣṭam): avaṉukku atirṣṭam aṭikkiṟatu.
	14.	tākkutal: vāṭiyaṭikkiṟatu, paṉiyaṭikkiṟatu.
	15.	iyaṅkutal: kutiri piṉṉukkaṭikkiṟatu.
	16.	cēṟu teṟittal: vaṇṭivarum pōtu vilaku, cēṟaṭikkum   (u.va.).
	17	.nīntukiyil kikāl acittal: nīccaṭittal.
	18.	tirumputal: kuppuṟa aṭittu viḻuntāṉ.
	19.	cūḷurittal: tiruṭavilli eṉṟu taliyilaṭittuc col.
	20	.uṇarvu keṭutal: mayakkamaṭittu viḻuntāṉ.
	21.	tērvil tavaṟutal: avaṉ tērvil kōṭṭaṭittu viṭṭāṉ.
	22.	cuṇṭuvillaṭittal: nāṉ pōṉa pōtu māṭṭuk kārappiyaṉ kavaṭṭi yaṭittuk koṇṭiruntāṉ.
	23.	mārpil aṟital: peṇkaḷ māraṭittu aḻukiṟārkaḷ.
	24.	tuḷiyaṭittal: cuvaril ippaṭiyā tuḷik kaḷaṭippatu?
	25.	pūcuṇam pūttal: mūṭi vitta palakārattil pūñcāṉ aṭittuviṭṭatu.
	26.	paṭartal: pāciyaṭitta kuḷattil iṟaṅkātē.
	27.	maḻittal: tampikku moṭṭiyaṭi.
	28.	taṅkutal: atu mūtēviyaṭitta mukam.
	29.	oru tici nōkkiyiyaṅkutal: kākki valamaṭittatu.
	30.	pulamputal: naṭuccāmattil pēyaṭittup pōkum.
	31.	malaṅkaḻital: mūṉṟu muṟi vayiṟṟālaṭittatu.
	32.	neñcu patittal: iruvar neñcamum aṭittuk koṇṭaṉa.
	33.	tirital: ūriyellām cuṟṟiyaṭittuviṭṭu vantāṉ.
	34.	tīmpu ceytal: celvac cerukkāl koṭṭa maṭikkiṟāṉ.
	35.	oṭṭāram paṇṇutal: erutu moṇṭiyaṭikkiṟatu.
	36.	cāytal: vaṇṭi paḷḷattil pōṉapōtu ōcāra maṭittuviṭṭatu.
	37.	naṭittal: avaṉukku nōyilli; nōyāḷi pōl nāṭaka maṭikkiṟāṉ.
	38.	puṟappaṭṭup pōtal: viṭumuṟiviṭṭatum ūrukku aṭittu viṭṭāṉ.
	39.	vīṇāka varuttik koḷḷutal: ēṉ ippaṭi aṭittuk koḷkiṟāy?
	40.	kālil kaṭṭiyaṭittal: intap pērāciriyar tiṟam maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ kālil kaṭṭiyaṭikkak kūṭak kāṇātu.
		aṭi ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟā viṉiyāka amintu poruḷpaṭutal:
	41.	taṇṭittal: aṭikkum oru ki; aṇikkum oru ki (paḻamoḻi).
	42.	tōṟkaruvi iyakkutal: avaṉ naṉṟāka mirutaṅkam aṭippāṉ.
	43.	maṇiyolippittal: niliyattil maṇiyaṭitta vuṭaṉ vaṇṭi puṟappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
	44.	ciṟaku iyakkutal: kuñcu, ciṟakaṭittup paṟantupōy viṭṭatu.
	45.	kalleṟital: kallāl oru māṅkuliyi aṭittu viṭṭāṉ   (u.va.).
	46.	taṇṇīrip pīyccutal: mukattil taṇṇīri aṭi.
	47.	maruntu teḷittal: puṉceykaḷukkup pūcci marun taṭittāṉ.
	48.	mōtutal: aliyaṭikkiṟatu.
	49.	tuvittal: vēṭṭiyi iraṇṭaṭi aṭittut tā.
	50.	māppicital: appattiṟku māvaṭikka vēṇṭum.
	51.	uṭittal: nilatti uḻutapiṉ kaṭṭi yaṭikka vēṇṭum.
	52.	pōril tākkutal: ellip pōril etirikku nalla aṭi.
	53.	vaṇṭi kuluṅkutal: pēruntu ōṭukiyil tūkki yaṭikkum.
	54.	kālaṅkaṭattutal: vāṅkiya kaṭaṉik koṭukkāmal iḻuttaṭik kiṟāṉ.
	55.	muttiri poṟittal: muttiri yaṭittal.
	56.	patittal: nilattil muḷiyaṭikka vēṇṭum.
	57.	acciṭutal: accaṭikkum iṭam.
	58.	poṟiyil tittal: oru caṭṭiyi aṭittuk koṭu.
	59.	veṭṭutal: nīrppācaṉattiṟku oru kiṇaṟaṭikka vēṇṭum.
	60.	kallāl karuvi camittal: ammi, tiriki, yural aṭikkumiṭam.
	61.	vīṭukaṭṭutal: marattālaṭitta maṉi.
	62.	paṭamāṭam (Tent) amittal: carkka naṭattak kūṭāram aṭittirukkiṟārkaḷ.
	63.	nakiyāṭik kūṟutal: maṇamakaṉip peṇkaḷ niyāṇṭi yaṭippatu vaḻakkam. (itukāl, kiṇṭa laṭittal peruvaḻakku.)
	64.	kuṟicollutal: avaṉ kaṭṭiṉa vīṭṭukku vakkaṇiyaṭippāṉ.
	65.	ōṭṭutal: vaṇṭiyaṭittal, ēraṭittal, piṇi yaṭittal.
	66.	vaṇṭiyil koṇṭu varutal: nāluvaṇṭi maṇalaṭittāṉ.
	67.	uḷviḻutal: erukkampāl aṭittal vēṇṭum.
	68.	vārik koṇṭupōtal: nelli veḷḷamaṭittuk 	koṇṭu pōṉatu.
	69.	kavartal: kōḻik kuñcip paruntu aṭittuk koṇṭupōṉatu.
	70.	nīkkutal: anta māṇavaṉ peyari aṭittu viṭṭārkaḷ.
	71.	maṟuttal: avaṉ colvatiyellām aṭittup pēcu.
	72.	vellutal: avaṉi ōṭṭap pantayattil aṭittu viṭṭāṉ.
	73.	veṉṟu peṟutal: avaṉ pēccup pōṭṭiyil oru 	veḷḷikkiṇṇatti aṭittuviṭṭāṉ.
	74.	kollutal: avaṉ pēyaṭittuc cettāṉ.
	75.	aḻittal: tirupati vañciṉam kavuravar marapi aṭittuviṭṭatu.
	76.	maṉappāṭañ ceytal: pāṭatti uruvaṭittu viṭṭāṉ.
	77.	kiyāḷutal: āṅkilamum tamiḻum kalantaṭikkiṟāṉ.
	78.	arittal: puttakattip pūcciyaṭittuviṭṭatu.
	79.	vaḻittal: aṇṇaṉukku mīciyaṭi.
	80.	kaḻittal: ciṟuvaṉ niṉṟukoṇṭē aṭikkiṟāṉ (naki).
	81.	aḻuttutal: vaṇṭiyil muṉpāram aṭikkiṟatu.
	82.	viti nacukkutal: atu kāyaṭitta kāḷi  (u.va.).
	83.	tolivari viṭutal: uṭaṉē varaccolli tantiyaṭittāṉ.
	84.	cāyttal: kāṟṟu, vēliyi aṭittuviṭṭatu.
	85.	nittal: inta vēriyaṭittup puṇṇilmēl 		vittukkaṭṭu (u.va.)
		aṭi eṉum col peyarc collāka amintu poruḷ tarutal:
	86.	kīḻppākkam, kīḻppakuti: aṭivayiṟu, aṭivāram, aṭittaḷam.
	87.	kīḻiṭam: marattaṭiyil uṭkār.
	88.	maṇṭi: (Sediment) (maṇṭiyi) aṭiyiluḷḷatik kīḻē ūṟṟu.
	89.	kāltaṭam: immaṇṇil patintuḷḷa aṭi evvaḻic cellukiṟatu?
	90.	paṉṉiru aṅkulam: antat tēriṉ uyaram 106 aṭi.
	91.	ceyyuḷ varikaḷ: kuṟaḷaṭi, cintaṭi, aḷavaṭi.
	92.	toṭakkam: naṭuviṉ muṭiviṉ aṭiyiṉ naṉṟāṉa poruḷ (ñāṉavāciṭṭam).
	93	.mūy« (Source): itaṉaṭiyāka itu piṟantatu.
	94.	kaṭavuḷ (Supreme Being): ārēyaṟivār aṭiyiṉ perumi (tirumantiram 2126).
	95.	giHik(Antiquity): aṭiyiṭṭa centamiḻiṉ (tāyu.)
	96.	vaḻimarapu: vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyeṉa vanta tirukkūṭṭam.
	97.	maravēr: aṭiyaṟṟa marameṉṉa aṭiyilē vīḻntu (tāyu.)
	98.	colvēr: mukam eṉum col muku eṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatu.
	99.	icippāṭṭiṉ pallavi: āyiram pāṭṭiṟku aṭiteriyum, oru pāṭṭiṟkum urut teriyātu (paḻamoḻi).
	100.	celvam (Riches, wealth) aṭiyuṭiyārk kellām cātittuk koḷḷalāmē (īṭu 4-2:9) (cen. cel. cilampu-85, paral-7 māci)
collāmal koḷḷāmal 
	collāmal	=	eṇṇiyatu iṉṉatu eṉṟu collāmal.
	koḷḷāmal	= 	eṇṇiyataṟku ēntāṉatip peṟṟuk koḷḷāmal.
ūrukkuc collāmal koḷḷāmal pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉpatil collāmal eṉpatu viṭipeṟāmal eṉpatiyum, koḷḷāmal eṉpatu vaḻic celavukku vēṇṭum toki peṟāmal eṉpatiyum kuṟikkum. collik koḷḷāmal eṉṟālum ipporuḷ taruvatēyām. celavu urittalum, vaḻiccelavu tantu viṭi tarutalum vaḻakkāka irunta nāḷil, avvaḻakkil iruntu vantatu itu.
collutal vaki 
collutal vakikaḷik kuṟikka ēṟattāḻa nāṟpatu coṟkaḷ tamiḻil uḷa eṉpatip paṭṭiyaliṭṭuk kāṭṭukiṟār pāvāṇar (paṇṭit tamiḻar nākarikamum paṇpāṭum  pak. 27,28).
	1. 	acittal: aciyaḻuttattuṭaṉ collutal. (aci aḻuttam Accent)
		acittu ici kōṭaliṉ aciyē
		acittal nāvi nilimāṟṟi iyakkutal.
	2.	aṟital: aṭittu (vaṉmiyāy maṟuttu)c collutal.
e-ṭu:
		aṟipaṟi aṉṉar kayavartām kēṭṭa
	   maṟipiṟarkku uytturikka lāṉ	- tiruk. 1076
		cevippaṟi atirumāṟu vaṉmiyuṭaṉ kūṟutal.
	3.	icittal: ōci vēṟupāṭṭuṭaṉ collutal. iyi >  ici.
		uḷḷam oṉṟik kēṭkumāṟu iṉituṟak kūṟal. icital = ēṟṟal, opputal. pukaḻcci moḻikaḷ amiyac collutalum icittalām.
	4.	iyamputal: icikkaruvi iyakkic collutal.
		iyam = icikkaruvi. pal iyam = palliyam; palavaki icikkaruvi muraciyampiṉa eṉpatu cilampu (maṅ.).
	5.	eṉṉutal: eṉṟu collutal.
		eṉṉa colluvatu eṉṉum poruḷilum iccol āḷappaṭum. eṉṉutal kiṉṟa tēḻi eyiṉaṉēṉ eṉpatu kampar moḻi.
	6.	ōtutal: kātiṟkuḷ mellac collutal.
		piṟar aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭā vakiyil kēṭpavar kātil maṭṭum viḻumāṟu collutal.
		ōtutal = kaṟṟal.
		ōtāmal orunāḷum irukka vēṇṭā- ulaka.
	7.	kattutal: kuraleḻuppic collutal.
e-ṭu:
		kaḻuti kattutal.
		ēṉ kattukiṟāy? metuvākap pēcu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
	8.	karital: aḻittuc collutal.
		kākam karital, kākam taṉakku makiḻvu uṇṭām poḻutil karivatum, avvoli kēṭṭu maṟṟik kākaṅkaḷ varuvatum karital - aḻittal - cāṉṟu. kākki karavā karintuṇṇum (tiruk. 527).
	9.	kaḻaṟutal: kaṭintu collutal.
		colvār kaṭumiyuri kēṭṭu maṟuttuc collavō, kēṭṭuk koṇṭu irukkavō muṭiyāmal akalumāṟu collutal. kaḻalutal = akaltal.
	10.	kuḻaṟutal: nāttaḷarntu collutal.
	11.	kūṟutal: kūṟupaṭuttic collutal.
	12.	cāṟṟutal: palar aṟiyac collutal.
	13.	kiḷattal: iṉṉateṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuc collutal.
	14.	kiḷattutal: kuṭumpa varalāṟu collutal.
	15.	kuyilutal: kuyiṟṟu - kuyil pōl iṉkuralil 		collutal.
	16.	cepputal: viṉāviṟku viṭi collutal.
	17.	navilutal: nāviṉāl olittup payilutal.
	18.	nutalutal: oṉṟic collit toṭaṅkutal.
	19.	nuvalutal: nūliṉ nuṇporuḷ collutal.
	20.	noṭittal: kati collutal.
	21.	pakartal: paṇṭaṅkaḷip pakuttu vili collutal.
	22.	paṉṉutal: niṟutti niṟuttic collutal.
	23.      paṟital: maṟi (irakaciyam) veḷippaṭuttic 		collutal.
	24	paṉuvutal: ceyyuḷiṟ pukaḻntu collutal.
	25.	pukalutal: virumpic collutal.
	26.	pulamputal: taṉakkut tāṉē collutal.
	27.	pēcutal: oru moḻiyiṟ collutal.
	28.	poḻital: iṭiviṭātu collutal.
	29.	māṟutal: uriyāṭṭil māṟic collutal.
	30.	miḻaṟṟutal: maḻali pōl iṉimiyāyc collutal.
	31.	moḻital: coṟkaḷit teḷivākap palukkic 		collutal.
	32.	valittal: kēṭpār maṉattip piṇikkac collutal.
	33.	viṭutal: meḷḷa veḷiyiṭṭuc collutal.
	34.	vitattal: ciṟappāy eṭuttuc collutal.
	35.	viḷattutal: virittuc collutal.
	36.	viḷḷutal: veḷiviṭṭuc collutal.
	37.	viḷamputal: ōr aṟivippic collutal.
colvaki 
peyarccol = orukālikku orukāli peyartal iyaṟkiyaminta col.
e-ṭu:
taḷir, tuḷir, koḻuntu, ili, paḻuppu, caruku ivaṟṟiṉ vaḷarvām peyarnili aṟika. piḷḷi, kuḻavi, kumaram, kāḷi, vāliyam, mutiyam, perumutiyam eṉa oru piṟappil ākum peyarnili aṟika.
viṉiccol = viṉavutaṟku iṭamāka amintu coṟṟoṭariṉ   payaṉniliyāka ami col.
e-ṭu:
paṭittāṉ - viṉimuṟṟu.
paṭitta - peyareccam (paṭitta puttakam).
paṭittu - viṉiyeccam (paṭittu muṭittāṉ).
iṭiccol	= peyarccol viṉiccol āyavaṟṟiṉ iṭattic cārntatāy aminta col.
e-ṭu:
		avaṉum nāṉum(colliṉ iṭiyilum īṟṟilum 	niṉṟatu)
		varuvāṉ pōlum(colliṉ īṟṟil niṉṟatu)
		muṉṉatu peyarit taḻuviyatu; piṉṉatu viṉiyit 	taḻuviyatu.
		eṉṉik kaṇṭāṉ - ai, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi urupu, colliṉ iṭiyē niṉṟatu.
		maṟṟavaṉ - maṟṟu, colliṉ mutalil niṉṟatu.
		iṭiyāvatu iṭam; naṭuvē eṉpataṉṟu iṭic col mūviṭattum varutal aṟika.
		uriccol= colliṉ aṭic collāy - ariya 	palavaṟṟiṉ mūlac collāy - aminta col.
e-ṭu:
		ceyyuṭkē uriya aruñcol aṉṟu. eḷitil makkaḷvaḻakkil varum collum uṇṭu. ārtal = niṟital. vayiṟāra uṇṭēṉ, neñcāra vāḻttu kiṟēṉ.
	       kūr= kūrmiyāṉa katti, kūrmiyāṉa paṭippāḷi.
	       koṭu= vaḷivu; puḷittuk koṭukukiṟatu.
coḷ 
coḷ eṉpatu arippu, kaṭippu eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum col. atu kaṭikkum kocuvik kuṟippatākap paḻaṉi vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. coḷ coḷḷeṉa aripput tāṅka muṭiyavilli eṉpatu nelli, mukavi vaḻakkukaḷām. coḷ coḷ iraṭṭic col.
coḷḷi 
cuḷ >  coḷ >  coḷḷi.
veppattāl kīṟiyatu uṭintatu keṭṭup pōṉatu āyavi coḷḷi eṉappaṭum. maṇpāṉi kīṟiṉālum pottalāṉālum coḷḷi eṉappaṭum. kāykaḷil veppu vakiyāl uṇṭākum nōyum citivum coḷḷi eṉappaṭum. puḻu uruvākit tuḷikkappaṭṭa kāy cūṉ eṉṟum cotti eṉṟum coḷḷi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. coḷ eṉpataṟku arippu eṉṉum poruḷum, vāynīr vaṭivu, coḷḷu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
coṟiciraṅku ariciraṅku 
	coṟiciraṅku		=	 peruñciraṅku.
	ariciraṅku		= 	ciṟuciraṅku.
muṉṉatu iṭiveḷipaṭap peritu peritākak kiḷampum; nīrum puṇṇum uṇṭām.
piṉṉatu iṭiveḷiyiṉṟi viyarkkuru pōla irukkum. ōyāmal arittalāl tēyttuk koṇṭirukka nērum.
coṟi ciraṅkaṉ coṟiyāmal iruntālum irukkalām; āṉāl, ariciraṅkaṉ coṟiyāmal irukka muṭiyātu. ātalāl, coṟi ciraṅkaṉi nampiṉālum ari ciraṅkaṉi nampak kūṭātu eṉpar.
coṉṟi 
col >  coṉ >  coṉṟi = cōṟu.
col = nel.
collarum cūlpatam eyti	- cin. 53
nellariciyāl āya cōṟē cōṟu eṉappaṭṭup piṉṉar maṟṟit tavacac cōṟukaḷiyum, cōḷaccōṟu, tiṉiccōṟu eṉak kuṟippatāyiṟṟu.
ataṉmēl paṉiyiṉ uṭcetumpu teṉṉiyiṉ uṭcetumpu kaḷḷi kaṟṟāḻi āyavaṟṟiṉ uṭcetumpum kūḻum cōṟu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum virivuṟṟu, mūḷic cōṟu eṉavum, eṉṟum nilipeṟṟa iṉpamām eṉak karutiya vīṭupēṟum cōṟu eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār cōṟṟiṉic coṉṟi eṉpar	- naṉ. 273. caṅ.







cō varicic coṟkaḷ

cō 
maḻi peyyum olikkuṟippu; calavi ceyvār olikkuṟippu; cō eṉṉum araṇ. ōreḻuttu orumoḻi.
cōṅkaṇ 
cōṅkaṇ eṉpatu ōrakkaṇ eṉṉum poruḷil akattīcuvara vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. ōrakkaṇ eṉpatu kaṭikkaṇ. pāl vaḻippaṭṭa pārviyik kuṟippatu atu. cōrakkaṇ eṉpatu cōṅkaṇ eṉa āki vaḻakkup peṟṟirukkak kūṭum. cōram pōtal, iṭakkaraṭakku.
cōṅku 
cōṅku:1
coṅku >  cōṅku. uḷḷīṭu illātati, iruntum valimi illātatic cōṅku eṉpatu potuvaḻakku. maturi māvaṭṭattil cōṅku eṉpatu koṭumi eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. kōṇ, kōṇal eṉṉum poruḷil nelli vaḻakku uḷḷatu.
cōṅku:2
cōṅku = uyartal.
inta maram cōṅkāka irukkiṟatu eṉpatum, nalla cōṅkāṉa āḷ eṉpatum vaḻakkil uḷḷavi. cōṅku eṉpatu ōṅkutāṅku eṉṉum iṇimoḻip poruḷi oruṅku koṇṭatām. uyaram ataṟku ēṟṟa uṭaṟkaṭṭu ōṅkutāṅku eṉappaṭum. attaṉmi yuṭiyatē cōṅku eṉka.
vaḷarntavar neṭṭi, kokku; kaṉattavar kutir, ural; iraṇṭum oruṅkamintavar cōṅkāṉavar. tēkku, tōtakatti pōṉṟa maraṅkaḷil cōṅkukkut taṉicciṟappu uṇṭu. pāri cōṅkāṉa (ōṅkutāṅkāṉa) āḷ eṉpatip puṟanāṉūṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
cōṅku = pāymaram.
kappaliṉ celavukkuk kāṟṟup pōkkil taḷḷic cellap pāriya maramum virinta pāyum vēṇṭum. pāymaram uyaramāka iruntāltāṉ peritākap pāykaṭṭavum, kāṟṟut taḷḷavum vāykkum ātalāl ataṉic cōṅku eṉṟaṉar.
cōki 
cōki:1
cōrki >  cōki.
cōrvuṟṟa niliyil kiḷarcciyaṟṟirukkac ceyyum nōy; maṉam cārnta innōy piṉṉē uṭalcārntu viṭukiṉṟatu. ceyalaṟavāl kālki taṭippum iyakkamiṉmiyum uṇṭāki viṭukiṉṟatu. paṭuttatu paṭuttatu, iruntatu iruntateṉa ākiṉṟatu.
cōki:2
karumpu, cōḷam, nel mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tōki potumakkaḷ vāyāl cōki eṉappaṭutal vaḻuvām.
cōṭi 
cōṭi:1
cōrvu. mayalcōṭi ellām taṇikkiṉṟa taruvē eṉpatil cōṭi ipporuḷatātal pulappaṭum (tiruvaruṭ.).
cōṭi:2
cōṭi = uḷḷīṭu illāmi.
nilakkaṭaliyuḷ cōṭi yuṇṭu. cōṭi eṉappaṭuvatu paruppu illāmal veṟṟuk kūṭāka irukkum kaṭali yākum. kaṭaliyil cōṭi mikuti eṉṟum, cōṭi pōkavilli eṉṟum kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. avvaḻakkil iruntu avaṉ oṉṟum cōṭiyilli eṉṟum avaṉ cōṭi eṉṟum vaḻakkūṉṟiṉa. aṟivāṟṟal, ceyal tērcci iṉmi cōṭiyākavum, aviyuṇmi cōṭi iṉmiyākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. coṅku cōṭi eṉac cilar taṉmiyic cuṭṭuvatu uṇṭu. coṅkum cōṭiyum eṉpatu oru poruḷ.
*  coṅku kāṇka.
cōṇāṭu 
cōḻar + nāṭu = cōṇāṭu.

o.nō.:
vāṇar = vāḻ + nar.
cōṇāṭṭelli	- kam. taṉip.
cōtā 
cōtā = uramillāta parumi.
naṭakka māṭṭāmal uṭal paruttuc cuṟucuṟuppu illāta kuḻantiyic cōtā eṉpar. periyavaruḷ cōtāvum uṇṭu. coṉṉāl cōtā ēṟpārā? caṇṭikku vantu viṭuvārē. ataṉāl kuḻantic cōtāvē nilipeṟṟatu. cota cota eṉpatu aḷaṟṟu nilattaṉmi. maḻi ciṟitu peytu niṉṟu viṭṭapiṉ naṭivaḻi cota cotappākac cēṟu paṭṭuk kiṭakkum. cota cota eṉṟu kiṭakkiṟatu eṉpar. erumit toḻuvamum cotacotappāka irukkum. iccota cotappām taṉmi pōl taci koḻa koḻa eṉa iruppatu cōtāvām. iṉṉum eṭṭu vikkāta cōtāp payal eṉpatu cōtāc ceyaṉmiyuri.
cōtāk kaṭā eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu mukavi nelli vaṭṭāra vaḻakku.
cōmāṟi 
com >  cōm + māṟi = cōmāṟi.
com = cottu, poruḷ. māṟi = piṟar poruḷit taṉporuḷāka māṟṟi eṭuttuk koṇṭu pōyviṭupavaṉ; tiruṭaṉ. 
cōmāṟutal 
nīr mika veppamāka iruntāl atil vēṇṭumaḷavu taṇṇīr viṭṭu ēṟṟa veppanili ākkuvar. ataṉic cōmāṟutal eṉpatu  ma.va.
vennīr mikac cūṭāka uḷḷatu. taṇṇīr viṭṭuc cōmāṟu eṉpar.
ēmam >  cēmam ākum. cēmam = pātukāppu.
cēmam >  cēm >  cēmāṟutal >  cōmāṟutal ākiyirukkalām.
cōraṉ 
cōraṉ:1
cōrvu kaṇṭu tiruṭic celpavaṉ cōraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ.
cōraṉ:2
kāvalkāraṉ cōrvi aṟintu otuṅkip pōy mēyum āṭu.
cōraṉ:3
oḻukkam keṭṭavaṉ.  (ma.va.)
cōrvu 
cōrvu eṉpatu, ayarvu taḷarvu nekiḻvu maṟati melivu kaḷavu tavaṟu mutalāya palporuḷ tarum col. ipporuḷkaḷuḷ etuvē yāyiṉum ākkam tārātu. ivaṟṟi vilakkiṉār tam niliyil maṭṭum allāmal, kuṭumpa nalamum kāttārām! maṉivi cōrntāl kuṭumpamē cōrntatām.
accāṇi illāta tēr muccāṇum ōṭātu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. maṉiyāḷ cōrnta vīṭu eṉṟum viḻunta vīṭē yaṉṟi eḻunta vīṭu ākātu. itu kaṇ kāṇum kāṭci! cōrntu kiṭakkum poṟuppillāk kaṇavaṉiyum iyakkavalla iyakkiyām maṉiviyē cōrntu viṭṭāl viṭivilli eṉpatu veḷiccam!
cōli 
colittu = paḷapaḷappuṭiyatu; oḷi ceyvatu.
kāmpu colittaṉṉa aṟuvi	- ciṟupāṇ. 236
poruḷ: mūṅkil āṭiyi urittā lotta mācillāta uṭi (uri: nac.)
mūṅkiliṉ uḷḷē uḷḷa melliya veṇpaṭalam; nūlāmpaṭi pōlvatu; oḷiyuḷḷatu.
kaḻipaṭu coliyiṉ iḻiyaṇi vārā
oṇpūṅ kaliṅkam	- puṟam. 383
mūṅkilākiya kōliṉ uṭpuṟattē peyartteṭukkappaṭum veḷḷiya tōl pōla neyyappaṭṭuḷḷa iḻikaḷiṉ varici kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭāta coli, kaliṅkattukku uvami (kuṟip u.vē.cā.).
māntaḷir pōlum uṭampu māmēṉi (poṉmēṉi) eṉpatām. ilikkut takaṭu eṉpatu peyar; takaṭu = poṉ, polivu, poṟpu. marattiṉ ilikaḷ, piñcukaḷ, kāykaḷ, kaṉikaḷ āyavi katiroḷi paṭṭatāl paṭṭoḷi ceyyum.
cōlikku orupeyar poṅkar eṉpatu. poṅkar = polivu; kāṇpōrik kavarvatu; aḻaku mikkatu. potumpu potumpar eṉpaṉavum cōliyē. veyiliṭip paṭā niḻalum taṉmiyum amintavi avi.
veyilnuḻi paṟiyāk kuyilnuḻi potumpar	- maṇimē. 4:5
iviyellām cōliyiṉ eḻilum oḷiyum taṇmiyum kaviṉum kāṭṭuvaṉa. ātalāl colippu uṭiyatām atu cōlip peyar peṟṟatākalām.
colippu >  cōlippu >  cōli.
cōḻak koṇṭal 
cōḻa nāṭṭup pakkam iruntu nīr koṇṭu vantu poḻiyum mukilic cōḻak koṇṭal eṉpatu nelli nāṭṭu vaḻakkākum. vaṭa kiḻakkup paruvak kāṟṟik kuṟippatu atu. koṇṭal = nīr koṇṭu varum mēkam.
cōḻar 
cōḻar eṉpār vaḷavar eṉavum, kiḷḷiyar eṉavum puṉalnāṭaṉ eṉavum peyar peṟṟaṉar.
vaḷamikka maruta nilattavar ātalāl vaḷavar eṉappaṭṭār. kāṭu keṭuttu nāṭākkik kuḷantoṭṭu vaḷam perukkiyatāl kiḷḷi eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉar. nīr vaḷamikka kāvirik kāvalar ātalāl puṉalnāṭar, nīrnāṭar eṉavum peyar peṟṟaṉar.
uṟiyūrit talinakarākak koṇṭamiyāl, ūraṉ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. cemmiyām naṭuvu nilic cālpāl cempiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl, cōḻar eṉappaṭṭatu teḷivākat tulaṅkavilli.
pāvāṇar colvatu:
cōḻanāṭu nelliṟku allatu cōṟṟiṟkuc ciṟantataṉāl nellik kuṟikkum col eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu appeyar peṟṟirukkalām.
col - (cōḷ) cōḻam - cōḻaṉ.
x.neh.:
kal - kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ - kāḻakam = karumi.
cōḷap peyariṉiṉṟu pirittuk kāṭṭavum cōḻam eṉṉum vaṭivu vēṇṭap peṟum.
cōḻanāṭu mutaṟkālattil nel mikutiyāy viḷikkappaṭṭatumaṉṟit tāṉāy viḷinta nilamākavum iruntirukkalām. māntarāl viḷikkappaṭum payirkaḷellām muṉpu tāmāy viḷintaviyē.
nilittiṇiyāl oru nāṭu peyar peṟuvatu iyalpē. ēḻu tīvukaḷuḷ nāvalan tīvu, iṟalittīvu, ilavan tīvu, kucit tīvu, tēkkan tīvu eṉṉum aintum nilittiṇiyāṟ peyar peṟṟavi. muḻukip pōṉa, kumarik kaṇṭattilum ēḻteṅka nāṭum ēḻ kuṟumpaṉi nāṭum iruntami kāṇka (tamiḻvaralāṟu pak.44).
ñāyiṟu pōṟṟutum ñāyiṟu pōṟṟutum
kāviri nāṭaṉ tikiripōl poṟkōṭṭu
mēru valamtirita lāṉ
ñāyiṟṟu vaḻiyar cōḻar; avar tikiri katirōṉ cuḻalvatu pōl ulakattuc cuḻalkiṟatu; ātalāl aññāyiṟṟip pōṟṟukiṟōm. itu cilappatikārattu maṅkala vāḻttup pāṭaliṟ kūṟum iraṇṭām vāḻttu.
itaṉ niṟivilēyum ulaka valam varum avaṉ āḷukiyiyē colli niṟikkiṟār.
uppāl uyarkōṭ ṭuḻiyatā
eppālum orutaṉi yāḻi uruṭṭuvōṉ eṉavē
cōḻaruḷ pukaḻvāynta oruvaṉ karikālaṉ avaṉ muṉṉōr paṟṟiya oru ciṟappip puṟanāṉūṟu pāṭukiṟatu.
vaḷitoḻi lāṇṭa uravōṉ maruka
kaḷiyiyal yāṉik karikāl vaḷava!	- puṟam. 66
eṉpatu atu.
paruvakkāṟṟiṉ niliyaṟintu kaṭalil kalam celuttiya pēraṟivum pērāṟṟalum vāyntavar vaḻiyil vantavaṉē eṉpatāl katir valam cuḻalal pōl, kalam kaṭal ñālam cuḻaṉṟami cuṭṭap paṭukiṉṟatu.
cōḻaṉiṭam vaḻakkurittu naṭuvunili kāṇa vanta mutiyar iruvar, avaṉ iḷamiyi aṟintu ivaṉā nam vaḻakkiyal aṟintu nēriya vakiyil muṭivu kūṟa vallāṉ eṉa vaḻakkik kūṟātē tirumpiyataṟintu, narit taliyaṉāy avarkaḷ muṉ tōṉṟi iruvarum oppa naṭumi kūṟiya ciṟappu paḻamoḻiyil (21) pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
urimuṭivu kāṇāṉ iḷamiyōṉ eṉṟa
narimutu makkaḷ uvappa - narimuṭittuc
collāl muṟiceytāṉ cōḻaṉ; kulavicci
kallāmal pākam paṭum
eṉpatu atu.
cūḻcci eṉpatu palaroṭum palakāl kalantu pēcit teḷivāṉa muṭipu eṭuppatākum.
cūḻvārkaṇ ṇāka oḻukalāṉ maṉṉavaṉ
cūḻvāric cūḻntu koḷal
eṉavum,
cūḻcci muṭipu tuṇiveytal attuṇivu
tāḻcciyuḷ taṅkutal tītu
eṉavum poyyāmoḻi (tiruk. 445, 671) pukalvatām cūḻccit tiṟam mētakkavarām cōḻar.
vañcakac cūḻcciyāka eṇṇum innāḷ makkaḷ vaḻakkoṭu, neñcaka niṟivām naṭumiyil āyntu ceyaṟpaṭuttiya annāḷ cūḻcciyi eṇṇutal ākātu.
cōḻa vēntar, ūr eṉak kāṇappaṭṭatu uṟiyūr.
ūreṉap paṭuvatu uṟiyūr
pūveṉap paṭuvatu poṟivāḻ pūvē
eṉpatu vaḻaṅku moḻi. uṟiyūrc ciṟappukaḷāka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ariya pativu āvaṇamākiyuḷḷatu oṉṟu. atu uṟiyūrkkaṇ irunta cāṉṟōr aviyam.
cāṉṟōrum uṇṭu kol cāṉṟōrum uṇṭukol (cilap. 16:54-56) eṉṟu kaṇṇakiyār maturiyil viṉā eḻuppiya cāṉṟōr uṇṭu eṉpatu avar piṟanta nāṭṭil tikaḻnta pukaḻ aviyamē yām.
cāṉṟōr aviyam ceṉṟu oruvaṉ taṉṉili kūṟiṉāṉ eṉiṉ, tīrāc cikkalum tīrntu nalam peṟuvāṉ eṉṉumaḷavu tamiḻakam muḻutaṟiyat tikaḻntatu cōḻartam uṟanti aviyamē yām.
muṉṉic cōḻar imaya veṟṟi kaṇṭu pulippoṟi, poṟittatuṇṭu. piṉṉic cōḻaruḷḷum irācarācaṉum irācēntiraṉum ilaṅki antamāṉ nakkavaram mālit tīvu maliyakak kaṭāram eṉpaṉa vellām veṉṟa perumi koṇṭaṉar. kaṅki koṇṭāṉ, kaṭāraṅ koṇṭāṉ, ciṅkaḷāntakaṉ eṉṟellām ciṟappup peṟṟa ūrp peyarkaḷ avarkaḷ vīṟukāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ.
cūḻum veṟṟiyum cūḻum nāṭu paṟṟalum cūḻum aṟivut tiṟamum cūḻum aṟivar aviyamum ākiyavellām oruṅkē eṇṇuṅkāl cūḻar eṉṉum peyar, cōḻar eṉap paṇṭē āyatō eṉa eṇṇac ceykiṉṟatu. cūḻcciyam eṉpatu nuṇtiṟamām. karikālaṉ kaṭṭiya kallaṇi ulakaḷāviya poṟittiṟa muṉṉōṭi allavō!
muṉṉic cūḻtiṟam pōlavē piṉṉiyum tikaḻnta cāṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭatē eṉiṉ, cōḻar peyarkku muṉṉic cāṉṟukaḷē cāṉṟām! piṉṉiyum avarnili iruntavāṟu aṟiyac collappaṭṭatām.
tañcip peruvuṭiyār kōyil kalimāṇpum, kaṅki koṇṭa cōḻapurak kōyil ciṟappum, cōḻavaḷa nāṭṭuk kōyilkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyum ellām cōḻarkaḷiṉ paṉmāṇ kalicciṟappik kāṭṭuvaṉa allavō!
caṅkam kaṇṭa perumi pāṇṭiyarkku uṇṭu eṉiṉum, paṭṭiṉappāli kūṟum kaṭalvaḻi vaṇika vaḷam cōḻarkku amintatu pōl avarkaḷukku amintilavē. kavāṭa muttum, koṟki muttum cērar miḷakum ulakaḷāviya pukaḻeytac cōḻar kalaṅkaḷ allavō kaṭal ulāk koṇṭaṉa.
ulakam cūḻum pukaḻāl cūḻar cōḻar eṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalāmō eṉa eṇṇac ceykiṟatu! mēlum teḷivu kiṭittāl tamiḻ nalamēyām!
cōḻiyaṉ 
cōḻiyaṉ = cōḻa nāṭṭiṉaṉ.
e-ṭu:
cōḻiyaṉ ēṉāti tirukkuṭṭuvaṉ 	(puṟam. 394).
cōḻiyaṉ kuṭumi cummā āṭātu 	eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
* ēṉāti kāṇka.
cōḷam 
uṭalil arippu uṇṭākkuvatu coḷ eṉappaṭum. accoḷḷi uṭiya tavacam cōḷam. attavacattiṉ mūṭi pōl amintatu coḷ ākum. atu paṭṭāl uṭal eriyum arikkum.
coḷḷuc coḷḷeṉa arikkiṟatu; cōḷap poṭṭup paṭṭuviṭṭatu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
cōḷattil mikat taṭitta coḷ uṭiyatu coṅku eṉappaṭum. ataṟkuc coṅkuc cōḷam eṉpatu peyar. nāṟṟuc cōḷam, karuñcōḷam eṉpatum ataṉ peyar.
cōṉimāri 
cōveṉa maḻi poḻikiṟatu eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. cōveṉa viṭātu maḻi poḻital cōveṉa māri poḻivatāy vaḻaṅkic cōṉāmāri eṉa innāḷ kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
cōṉi māri = viṭāmaḻi; toṭarmoḻi.

`ña varicic coṟkaḷ

ñaññi 
ni ni eṉak katti etaṉiyum colla māṭṭātu etic ceytālum payaṉpaṭāmal mayaṅki niṟka vittal. naḷinaya (avinaya) vakiyuḷ oṉṟu.
ñaññi = ninni.
o.nō.:
ñamali >  namali; ñamaṉ >  namaṉ.
ñaññi yuṟṟōṉ avinayam nāṭiṟ
paṉmeṉ ṟiṟukiya nāvaḻi vuṭimiyum
nuricērnta kūmpum vāyum nōkkiṉārk
kurippōṉ pōla uṇarvi lāmiyum
viḻippōṉ pōla viḻiyā tiruttalum
viḻuttaka vuṭimiyum oḻukki lāmiyum
vayaṅkiya tirumuka maḻuṅkalum piṟavum
mēviya teṉpa viḷaṅkumoḻip pulavar	
		- cilap. 1:3:13 aṭiyārk. mēṟ.
ñamali 
namali >  ñamali = nāy.
nammi neruṅki yiruppatum cuṟṟic cuṟṟi varuvatum perukak kurippatum perukiya aḷavil kuṭṭi pōṭuvatum ākiyatu ñamaliyām nāy.
amalutal = niṟital, neruṅkutal. amalutal >  PkYjš> ñamali.
toṭarppaṭu ñamali	- puṟam. 74
ñamaṉ 
camaṉ >  ñamaṉ.
ñamaṉ = naṭuvu niliyāḷaṉ.
terikōl ñamaṉ	- puṟam. 6
uḷḷār illār periyar ciṟiyar eṉṉāmal uriya kālattil uriyatu ceyyum camaṉāḷaṉ ñamaṉ eṉpatām.
iṉi namaṉ eṉpataṉ vaḻiyatu ñamaṉ eṉiṉ namakkut tīrā uṟavāḷaṉ kūṟṟuvaṉ ātalāl avaṉ namaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. namaṉ >  ñamaṉ.
emaṉ eṉpatum emmavaṉ eṉpatēyām. emakku uriyavaṉ eṉpatu.
ñamaṉkōl 
camaṉkōl >  ñamaṉkōl = niṟikōl.
naṭuvu nilimikkuc cāṉṟāṉatu atu. ñemaṉkōl eṉpatum atu.
* camaṉkōl kāṇka.
ñāṭpu 
nāṭpu >  ñāṭpu.
ñāṭpu = pōr.
ñāṭpiṉuḷ eñciya	- kaḷavaḻi. 2
vīrarāvār nāḷum poḻutum pōrililēyē īṭupaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṉum ārvattarāy īṭupaṭalāl nāṭpu >  ñāṭpu āyatām.
viḻuppuṇ paṭāta nāḷellām vaḻukkiṉuḷ
vikkumtaṉ nāḷi eṭuttu	- tiruk. 776
ñāṇ 
nāṇ >  ñāṇ = nāṇam, vaḷital, kayiṟu, nāṇal pul.
cāva nōṉñāṇ	- puṟam. 14
* nāṇ kāṇka.
ñāti 
uṟaviṉar cuṟṟattavar eṉṉum poruḷatu iccol. nāti eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. kēṭpataṟku orunāti illi eṉpar.
ñāy = tāy; emtāy ñāy.
yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō!
eṉpatu kuṟuntoki.
ñāyvaḻiyar ñātiyar; nātiyar āyatu.
ñāyil 
ēyil >  ñāyil.
ēppuḻi ñāyil	- pu. ve. 86
ātalāl ēyil >  ñāyil āyatām.
kōṭṭi matilmēl mēṭiyil iruntu ampu ēvutaṟku iṭamāvatu ñāyil eṉappaṭṭatu.
ñāyiṟu 
ñāyiṟu = katirōṉ.
ñāy + iṟu = ñāyiṟu. ñāy = tāy. iṟu = eṅkum taṅkuvatu.
kōḷkaḷukkut tāyaṉṉa mūlamāka amintatum, avaṟṟukku oḷi taruvatum māṟi maṟiyā oḷiyuṭiyatumām atu ñāyiṟu eṉappaṭṭatu.

ñāy

ñāṉ >  nāṉ. nāṉākiya eṉ tāy ñāy.

ñālam

ñāṟu 
ñāṟu eṉpatu nāṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷil viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. nāṟu eṉpatu nāṟṟu.
e-ṭu:
ñāṉ >  nāṉ; ñāṟu >  nāṟu.
ñāṟu eṉpatu tolpaḻa vaḻakku. yāṟu >  āṟu; yāṉi > āṉi eṉpavi pōla. nāṟutal = muḷittal.
ñāṉam 
ñāṉam:1
yāṉ >  nāṉ >  ñāṉ. taṉmip peyar. ñāṉ + am = ñāṉam.
taṉṉi aṟiyum aṟivu ñāṉam. taṉṉi aṟintāṉ talivaṉi aṟintāṉ; ākaliṉ, ñāṉam potumiyuṟṟu aṟivup poruḷ tantatu. ñāṉamikkāṉ ñāṉi āṉāṉ. ñāṉanūl = meyyiyal nūl. meykaṇṭār aruḷiyatu civañāṉa pōtam; ataṉ vaḻiyatu civañāṉa cittiyār.
ñāṉam:2
ñāṉam eṉpatu aṟivup poruṭṭatām colleṉiṉum, meyyaṟivup poruḷilēyē ñāṉap peyar nūlkaḷ cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
caṭṭimuṉi ñāṉam, akattiyar ñāṉam, urōmar ñāṉam, tirumūlar ñāṉam, cuppiramaṇiyar ñāṉam, kākapucuṇṭar ñāṉam eṉap pala nūlkaḷ cittar tokutiyuḷ uḷḷaṉa. caṭṭi muṉi nāyaṉār muṉñāṉam nūṟu; piṉñāṉam nūṟu eṉavum iru nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivviraṇṭumē eṇcīr viruttaṅkaḷāl āṉavi. muṉpiṉ eṉpaṉa kālamuṟic cuṭṭē ākalām. cittar ñāṉam kūṟum nūlkaḷ perumpālum eṇcīr viruttaṅkaḷālēyē amintuḷa. piṟa pāviṉap pāvakikaḷ aritākavē uḷa.
ñāṉṟu 
ñāl >  ñāṉ >  ñāṉṟu = poḻutu.
alappeṉ tōḻi avarakaṉṟa ñāṉṟē	- kuṟun. 41
ñālattiṉ (ulakattiṉ) cuḻaṟciyāl ēṟpaṭuvatu poḻutu. ñālam = ulakam. ñālutal >  nālutal = toṅkutal. avaṉ nāṉṟu koṇṭu iṟantu viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
ñāṉṟu, iṭamum poḻutum kuṟikkum col. ivviraṇṭum kūṭiyatu oṉṟaṉōṭu oṉṟu toṭarpuṭiyatu ñāṉṟu. yāṇṭu, muṉ, piṉ eṉpaṉavum nilamum poḻutum kuṟippaṉavē.
ñiṇam  
niṇam >  ñiṇam.
ūṉtaci, ūṉ nīr.
piññiṇam parutta pacuveḷ ḷamali	- puṟam. 177
ñimiṟu 
vaṇṭu vakiyuḷ oṉṟu.
im > imir > ñimir > ñimiṟu eṉa āyatu. oliyiṉ vakiyāl peṟṟa peyar itu. tumpi, curumpu eṉpavi ivvaṇṭu vakiyiṉavē.
matukaram ñimiṟoṭu vaṇṭiṉam pāṭa	- cilap. 25:20
ÁØÈ 
nīli eṉpatu vāḻiyuḷ oruvaki. nīli ñīli ākum. piññīli eṉṉum ūr pāṭupukaḻ peṟṟut tirup piññīliyākak koḷḷiṭa vaṭakariyil viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ñekiḻam 
nekiḻam > ñekiḻam.
nekiḻtal iṭampeyartal; iṭampeyarntu olittal.
kōṭīr ilaṅkuvaḷi ñekiḻa	- kuṟun. 11
ñekiḻam = cilampu; ataṉ uḷḷiṭu paral. iṭampeyarntu olittalāl peṟṟa peyar.
aracuṟi kōyil aṇiyār ñekiḻam	- cilap. 18:25
* kaḻal kāṇka.
ñekiḻi 
nekiḻi >  ñekiḻi.
ñekiḻi = taṉmuṉ uḷḷati vetuppi urukkiyum karukkiyum nekiḻac ceyvatu, atu tī.
cēṇōṉ māṭṭiya naṟumpuki ñekiḻi	- kuṟun. 150
ñeṇṭu 
naṇṭu > ñeṇṭu.
naḷ = kuḷir; naḷ + tu = naṇṭu. naṇṭu = kuḷirilē vāḻvatu. nīr nilikaḷic cārntu vaḷiyamittu vāḻvatum nīrniliyuḷ vāḻvatumām atu naḷir, naḷiri, kuḷir eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
kokkiṉ
pārval añciya paruvaral īrñeṇṭu	- kuṟun. 117
ñemal

ñemal = niramputal, niṟital, mikutal, ceṟital.
ñemiṭutal 
ñemiṭutal = kayakkutal (kacakkutal)
katir, payaṟṟukkāy mutaliyavi kiyāl kacakkip pacciyākavē tiṉṉap paṭuvaṉa.
ñemiṭutal, nimiṇṭutal eṉa ikkālattut tirintu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. (naṟ. 22 piṉ.a.nā.). kiviralkaḷāl tōlip pitukki viḷiyāṭum iḷiyar nimiṭṭām paḻam eṉṟu ataṉik kūṟuvar.
muntuviḷi peruṅkural koṇṭa manti

miṉṉuṟaḻ iḷamuli ñemuṅka	- kuṟun. 314









ta varicic coṟkaḷ

ta 
tamiḻ akara variyiṉ valliṉa eḻuttil nāṉkāvatākavum uyirmey yeḻuttil ēḻāvatākavum aminta eḻuttu.
takaramutal takara aukāram īṟākiya paṉṉīreḻuttum moḻi mutalāka varuvatu. uyirmey variciyil ivvāṟu paṉṉīreḻuttum colmutalāka varuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ iḵtu iraṇṭām eḻuttām. eñciyavi ka, na, pa, ma eṉpaṉa.
atō, itō, antā, intā eṉpavaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyākat ta pār eṉac cuṭṭum makkaḷ vaḻakku uṇṭu.
takaṭu 
pūviṉ puṟa itaḻ.
katiroḷipaṭṭa aḷavil takatakaveṉap paḷicciṭṭuk kāṭṭiya pūvitaḻ takaṭu eṉappaṭṭup, piṉṉar maṟṟip poṉ cempu mutaliya māḻit takaṭukaḷiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu.
karuṅkāl vēṅki māttakaṭ ṭoḷvī
iruṅkal viyalaṟi varippa	- aiṅkuṟu. 219
takaṭūr 
takaṭūr eṉpatu innāḷit tarumpuriyiṉ paḻam peyar ākum. takaṭūr eṉṉum peyarip patiṟṟup pattu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu (pati. 8). takaṭūriṉ teṉkiḻakkil ēḻu (ayiram) kilō mīṭṭar tolivil uḷḷa atamaṉ kōṭṭi eṉṉum ūr atiyamāṉ kōṭṭi eṉpataṉ citivē ākum. atiyamāṉ kōṭṭiyiṉ citipāṭukaḷi iṉṟum kāṇalām. kōṭṭiyum citintu, koṇṭa peyarum citintu pōya nilimiyilum taṉ paḻavaralāṟṟi niṉivūṭṭum atiyamāṉ kōṭṭi vāḻvatāka! atiyamāṉ niṉivu tōṉṟap putuvatām niliyam oṉṟum kiḷarntuḷatu.
takaṭūr takaṭi, takaṭṭūr, takaṭāpuri eṉṉum peyarkaḷālum vaḻaṅkiyatu. takaṭu eṉṉum col pala poruḷkaḷit tarum. avaṟṟuḷ takaṭṭu vaṭivap poruḷ, poṉ, pūviṉ puṟa itaḻ ākiya mupporuḷkaḷilum caṅkattār āṇṭuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟuḷḷum,
karuntakaṭ ṭuḷippū marutu	- muruku. 27
tūttakaṭ ṭetirmalar	- naṟ. 52
vēṅki māttakaṭ ṭoḷvī	- puṟam. 202
karuṅkāl vēṅki māttakaṭ ṭoḷvī	- aiṅkuṟu. 219
kāṉap pātirik karuntakaṭṭu	- akam. 261
eṉap pūviṉ puṟavitaḻ eṉṉum poruḷē payila vaḻakkup peṟṟuḷḷatu. poṉṉiṉ vēṟupaṭuttutaṟku nāṟāt takaṭu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟik koṇṭu akavitaḻip pātukākkum puṟavitaḻ pōṉṟa matili uṭiyatu eṉpār pulavar pāṇṭiyaṉār (eḻiṉi. 12). āṉāl atiyamāṉiṉ kutirimali, takaṭāka amintatu. ataṉ vaḻiyatu takaṭi, takaṭūr eṉpatu iyaṟki iyipiṉatu.
e-ṭu:
maṭṭappāṟi, kuṭṭippāṟi, taṭṭippāṟi, cippip pāṟi eṉpavaṟṟi nōkkuka.
takaṇi 
takaṇi:1
oru neṭumarattit tuṇṭippār kōṭariyāl kuṟukkē kuṟukkē tarittup piḷappatu vaḻakkam. ak kuṟukku veṭṭukkut takaṇi eṉpatu nelli vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. takaṇiyip patti eṉpatu tirumaṅkala vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. oru kaṭaṉip palakāl pakuttut tarutalum peṟutalum tavaṇi eṉappaṭum vaḻakkatti eṇṇalām. tavaṇi > takaṇi. karumput tuṇṭit takaṇi eṉpatum vaḻakku.
takaṇi:2
mutalukkuriya tokiyum vaṭṭiyum tavaṇi tavaṇiyāka vaḻaṅkutal tavaṇi. oru pāṟiyip peyarttal vari vittup piḷattal takaṇiyām. paṉam paḻattic cīvit tuṇṭu tuṇṭut takaṭukaḷāka eṭuppatit takaṇi eṉpatu kumari mutaliya teṉ tamiḻaka vaḻakku.
takattaka 
oḷikkuṟippu takataka eṉpatu.
taṅkam, takattaka eṉa miṉṉukiṟatu  ma.va.
 takattakāyam eṉpār pāratiyār.
pāraṭiyō vāṉam takattakāyam	- pārati. pāñ.
takattaka eṉa amintatu taṅkam.
* takaṭu kāṇka.
takaram 
takar + am = takaram.
al maṇatti akaṟṟi (a) takarttu nal maṇamām naṟumaṇam tarum mayirccāntu.
tuvarap pularntu tūmalar kañalit
takaram nāṟum taṇṇaṟuṅ katuppiṉ
putumaṇa makaṭūu	- akam. 141
makaḷir takaram pūciyatu itu.
eṇṇey nīviya curivaḷar naṟuṅkāḻt
taṇṇaṟun takaram kamaḻa maṇṇi	- kuṟiñ. 107-108
āṭavar takaram pūciyatu itu.
takar 
mōtuvatil muṉippuṭiyatu cemmaṟiyāṭṭuk kaṭā. ātalāl ataṉi mōtti eṉṟu poruntap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. am mōttikkut takavoru peyarum iṭṭaṉar. atu takar eṉpatu. takarppatu - muṭṭi mōti aḻippatu - takar tāṉē!
takar veṉṟi eṉpatu puṟattuṟikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. pōr vīrar tam vīramum veṟṟiyum mēlum mēlum peruka vēṇṭik kāṇpatu takarkaḷi mōtaviṭalām.
ūkkam uṭiyāṉ oṭukkam porutakar
tākkaṟkup pērum takittu	- tiruk. 466
takar eṉṉātu porutakar eṉṟum, tākkaṟku eṉṟum kūṟiya arumi aṟika.
māntaṉ mōtik koḷḷap pōrp payiṟci tarum porutakar porunti vāḻumō?
takavu 
takkatu eṉac cāṉṟōrāl pārāṭṭappaṭum paṇpu takavu ākum.
takutiyāṉatu etu vō, atu takavu.
takuti eṉavoṉṟu naṉṟē pakutiyāṟ
pāṟpaṭ ṭoḻukap peṟiṉ	- tiruk. 111
eṭuttuk koṇṭataṉ muḻumiyum kuṟiyāt takutiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum. avar takavukku niṟivāṉa nilitta aṭiyāḷam. avar ulakil vittup pōkum uyarnta ceyaṟpāṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷē. eṇṇam aṉṟu; col aṉṟu; ceyal! ceyaṟkariya ceyal.
takkār takavilar eṉpa tavaravar
eccattāl kāṇap paṭum	- tiruk. 114
takuti eṉpatum itu.
takuṇi 
taṇivu eṉpatu takuṇi, takaṇi eṉa vaḻaṅkutal tiruvātavūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. taṇinta ōciyuṭiya icik karuvi oṉṟu takuṇiccam eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟutali nōkkiṉ takuṇi eṉpataṉ taṇivup poruḷ viḷakkamākum.
taki 
taki:1
taki = aḻaku.
takavum, takavām taṉmiyum takiyām. aṉpu, aruḷ, oḻuṅku, perumi amintatu aḻakiṉṟi yamiyumō? ākaliṉ, takiyum, takimiyum aḻakeṉap peṟṟaṉa. peruntaki, takiyōr eṉpavi uyariya taṉmi eṉpataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
taki:2
takippu = kaḷippu, cōrvu.
ōṭi vantāl takippu ēṟpaṭum. māṭu vēli ceytu vēli viṭutalit takippāṟutal (tippāṟutal) eṉpar. takippiṉ mūlam taki. taki eṉpatu nīr vēṭkiyākum. takiyāka irukkiṟatu taṇṇīr kuṭikka vēṇṭum eṉpatu teṉ tamiḻaka vaḻakku.
takicāṉṟa col 
coṟkāttal eṉpatu iruvakiya. oṉṟu tāṉ collum collik kāttal, maṟṟoṉṟu piṟar collum naṟcollik kāttal.
collukkuk kūṟṟu eṉpatoru peyar. palar tam ceyalāl iyalāl nallarāka iruntum, tam collāl paḻikkum tuyarkkum āḷāvatik kāṇum pōtu, collukkuk kūṟṟu eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiya muntu mutalvaṉi, māli cūṭṭi makiḻa vēṇṭum eṉpatu tōṉṟum!
pallakku ēṟuvatum collālē; pal uṭipaṭuvatum collālē eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiṉum teḷivākac coṟpayaṉ kuṟittuc colliviṭa muṭiyumā?
yākāvār āyiṉum nākākka kāvākkāl
cōkāppar colliḻukkup paṭṭu
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar (tiruk. 127) teḷivākak kūṟiṉār. naṉmikkum tīmikkum iṭamāka irukkum collik kāttavar, pukaḻik kāttārām!
colli maṭṭum collittāṉ piṟar paḻippārā? ceyalic colla māṭṭārā? iyalpic colla māṭṭārā? avar kūṟum paḻi taṉṉaḷavil niṟpatu illiyē! piṟanta kuṭumpam pukunta kuṭumpam eṉpavaṟṟukkum tāvumē! ātalāl, piṟar kūṟum paḻikku āḷākāmi ciṟappām. ēṉeṉil, paḻikku āḷākā vāḻvē aṟavāḻvu eṉpatu vaḷḷuvam (tiruk. 49).
takivu 
takivu:1
takkatu aṉṟu eṉa otukkat takkatu takivu ākum. at takivu piṉṉē ākātu eṉa etaṉit taṭuppatiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu.
takital = vilakkutal, taṭuttal.
takivu:2
takivu = kaṭṭuppaṭutal, ēṟṟal.
oru poruḷi vilikkut taruvārum peṟuvārum oppuk koṇṭu kaṭṭuppaṭutal allatu ēṟṟal takivu eṉpatu ma.va. anta vili takiyavilli eṉpar.
takkaṇam 
takku + aṇam = takkaṇam. takku = paḷḷam; aṇam = poruntutal.
taṇivāṉa nilapparappiṉāl takkaṇam eṉappaṭṭatu. takkut toṇṭi. aṭittoṇṭi ma.va. (pāvāṇar).
takkatu 
takkavarkaḷ takkatu eṉa ēṟkat takkatu, takkatu ākum. takkavar eṉpār takutiyāl takka ceyal ceyyum takavāl uyarntōr āvar.
takkār takavilar eṉpatu avaravar
eccattāl kāṇap paṭum
eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (114).
takka tiruppaṇiyum aṟakkāvalum iṟimik kaṭaṉum ceyvār eṉṟu tērnteṭukkappaṭṭut tirukkōyilkaḷukku amarttap peṟuvār, takkār eṉa aḻikkappaṭutal iṟṟi nāḷ nili.
takkatu maṉṟa mikkōṉ kūṟṟu	- peruṅ. 2:14:72
takavu, takuti eṉpatum itu.
eṉciṟumiyiṉ iḻintu nōkkāṉ
taṉperumiyiṉ takavu nōkkik

kaṉaveṉa maruḷa vallē naṉaviṉ
nalkiyōṉē	- puṟam. 387
variyaḷan taṟiyāt tiriyaru nīttat
tavala maṟucuḻi maṟukaliṟ
ṟavalē naṉṟumaṟ ṟakutiyum atuvē	- puṟam. 238
takkam 
tarukkam > takkam.
taṭi eṉṉum poruḷil takkam eṉpatu virutunakar vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. taṭukkal, taṭukku, taṭukkam eṉpavi taṭuttal, niṟuttutal poruḷ taruvaṉavām. taṭṭut taṭaṅkal eṉpatu iṇiccol. iṉit tarukkam eṉpatum taṭuttu niṟuttutal poruḷil tokuttut takkamāki yirukkavum kūṭum.
takki mukki 
	takkutal		= 	aṭi iṭaṟutal.
	mukkutal		= 	mūccut tiṇaṟutal.
uṭal paruttum akavi mutirntum uḷḷavar uyaramāṉa iṭattiṟku ēṟuvatu kaṭumiyāṉatu. appaṭi ēṟuṅkāl aṭi taḷḷāṭutalum, mūccu irittalum uṇṭām. anniliyil avar takkimukki ēṟuvār. itaṉit tatti mutti eṉpatum uṇṭu.
takku 
takku = paḷḷam, kuḻi.
toṇṭiyiṉ kīḻppakuti takkut toṇṭi eṉappaṭum.
* takkaṇam kāṇka.
takku mukku 
takku= taṭi, taṭuppu, tāḻvu eṉṉum poruḷatu.
mukku= mūccu muṭṭutal, perumūccu vāṅkutal.
mēlmūccu, kīḻmūccu vāṅkutal eṉṉum poruḷatu. vaṭakku uyarat tāḻnta teṟku takkaṇam eṉappaṭuvataṟika.
takki 
kaṉamaṟṟa paḻuppu eṉpatu takki eṉa vaḻaṅkum. kātu kuttit takki vippatu muṉṉi vaḻakkam. puṭṭikaḷiṉ mūṭi takkiyākum. iṉi virutunakar vaṭṭārattil eḻutti aḻikkum tēyviyi (irappari)t takki eṉal aṟiyap peṟukiṉṟatu. tēyvi = tēyppāṉ, aḻippāṉ.
takkōlam 
takkōlam:1
aivaki naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ataṉik kāṭṭu veṟṟili eṉpar. civanta vāyi mēlum civakkac ceyvatum naṟumaṇam ūṭṭuvatumām atu.
maṟṟi naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷ: ilavaṅkam, ēlam, karuppūram, cātikkāy eṉpaṉa.
takkōlam = kāṭṭu veṟṟili (ve.vi.pē.).
takkōlam tiṉṟu taliniṟiyap pūccūṭi	- nālaṭi. 43
takkōlam:2
ōrūr.
arakkōṇam cārntatōr ūr. maliyakattilum ivvūrp peyariya ūr uṇṭu. ataṟkut talit takkōlam eṉpatu peyar. ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntatākat tālami cuṭṭukiṟār. annakar paṇṭit tamiḻarkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭatu eṉpar. ātalāl ataṟku muṉṉatu tamiḻakat takkōlam eṉpar. paḻaṅ kaṟkālak karuvikaḷ ip pakutiyil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa (vāḻviyal kaḷañ. 9:718, 719).
taṅka iḻi 
taṅkatti urukki nūliḻipōl ākkic ceyyappaṭum muṟiyāl iḻi nūliyum aṇikalattiyum, aṇikalam aṇintavariyum kuṟittatu, potuvaḻakku.
intiya nāṭṭiṉ taṅka iḻi eṉat tērvu ceyyappaṭṭatu caṇalnār ākum. caṇalnārppayaṉ karutiya peyarīṭu iḵtām; aḻakup poruḷ ākkattiṟkum payaṉporuḷ ākkattiṟkum payaṉpaṭutal nōkkiya peyarīṭu itu.
kaṟṟāḻi nār oṉṟum kuṟintatilli. ataṉi muṉvittiruntāl caṇali veṉṟirukkum. puḷicci nārum appaṭiyē! āṉāl caṇalpōl vaṭanāṭṭu viḷiporuḷkaḷāka illāmal tamiḻakap poruḷkaḷ āyiṉavē ivi! eppaṭi mutaṉmiyuṟum?
taṅkac cintu 
taṅkamē eṉṉum viḷiyuṭaṉ varum cintu taṅkac cintu eṉappeṟum.
muṉṉaṭi nāṉku cīrkaḷiyuṭiyatākavum iṟutiyaṭi irucīrkaḷi yuṭiyatākavum etukit toṭiyuṭaṉ amintu, taṅkamē eṉṉum taṉic colluṭaṉum eṭuppuṭaṉum muṭiyum. taṅkac cintu iraṇṭaṭi mutal pala aṭikaḷākavum varum.
taṅkarittal 
taṅkiyiruttal eṉpatu taṅkarittal eṉat tirumaṅkalam maturi vaṭṭāraṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. nellip pakutiyilum kēṭkalām. taṅkutal poruḷatu itu. avaṉ ōriṭattil taṅkarikka māṭṭāṉ eṉpar. taṅkarikka muṭiyātu eṉpatu kāttal eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkalum uṇṭu.
taṅkarittal = taṭuttu niṟuttutal.
taṅkal 
taṅkumiṭam taṅkal ākum. nām vāḻum ūril iruntu vēṟōr ūrkkuc ceṉṟu kuṟitta kāla aḷavu taṅkiṉāl avviṭam taṅkal ākum (Reside). taṅkal eṉṉum ūrppeyarkaḷ uṇṭu.
*  taṇkāl kāṇka.
taṅkaḷ 
taṉ > tam > taṅkaḷ.
paṭarkkip palarpāṟ peyar. aṉṟiyum muṉṉiliyāriyum paṭarkkic ciṟappup peyarāka vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkākavum uḷḷatu.
eṅkaḷ uṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ eṉpavi taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkkikaḷ.
taṅkaḷ utavi kālattāl vāyttatāl kaṭaṉ tolliyil iruntu mīṇṭēṉ; kavaliyum pōṉatu eṉpatu muṉṉilic ciṟappup peyar paṭarkkic ciṟappup peyarāyatām. iṉi mutal nīṇṭu tāṅkaḷ eṉpatumām.
nāṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ eṉa mūviṭaṅkaḷukkumām.
taṅkālam 
kālam eṉpatē maḻikkālattik kuṟittal vaḻakku. kālappayir, kōṭippayir eṉpatu uḻavar vaḻakku. kālam, taṟkālam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. taṉ eṉpatu maḻiyik kuṟippatu paḻavaḻakku. taṟpāṭiya taḷiyuṇaviṟ puḷ eṉpatu vāṉampāṭi paṟṟiya paṭṭiṉappāli aṭi (4). taṟ kālam > taṉkālam > taṅkālam eṉappaṭum. tiruccentūr, nelli vaṭṭāraṅkaḷil taṅkālam eṉpatu maḻikkālam kuṟittu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
taṅku taṭi 
	taṅku		=	 taṅkukiṟa allatu niṟkiṉṟa nili.
	taṭi		=	 taṭukkappaṭṭa nili.
taṅku taṭi illāmal varalām eṉṟum, taṅku taṭiyillāmal pēcu eṉṟum kūṟak kēṭkalām. tāṉē oru kāraṇattāl taṭippaṭṭa niliyō, piṟarāl taṭukkappaṭṭu niṟuttum niliyō illāmal varalām pēcalām eṉpatām.
taṅkutal taṉviṉiyum, taṭi piṟarviṉiyumākak karutuka.
taṅki 
aṅki eṉṉum iḷiyavaḷ peyar. tam eṉṉum uṟavurimi oṭṭuṭaṉ tam + aṅki = tamaṅki > taṅki āyatu. tam + akki = tamakki āvatu pōla. taṅkāy, taṅkiyē eṉpaṉa viḷikaḷ.
tacumpu 
nīrk kuṭattit taliyilō iṭuppilō koṇṭu cellum pōtu nīr alampum! alamputal acital. atu taḷamputal, taḷumputal eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. nīr taḷumpal pōlavē matu (kaḷḷu)k kuṭam koṇṭu pōkum pōtum taḷumpum. ataṉit tacumpu tuḷaṅku irukki eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattu (42). ap peyarālēyē appāṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. vaṇṭiyil koṇṭu cellum matukkuṭanīr acivu paṟṟiyatum. ataṉ aṭimaṇiyām irukki paṟṟiyatum attoṭar.
taci 
taci:1
taccu > tacci > taci.
oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu ceṟintu cērntu kaṭṭuṟṟu niṟpatu. taci nār eṉṟum ūṉ eṉṟum koḻuppu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvatu. yākki eṉṉum kaṭṭamivukku mūlakkaṭṭamippāy uṭal tōṟṟamāy valuvāy vaḷamāy amivatu taciyām. ataṉ aḷavu kuṉṟalum kūṭalum uṭalukku nōyum tuyarumām.
māṉtaci corinta vaṭṭi- puṟam. 33
taci:2
ceḻumi, celvam.
taciyāka irunta pōtu koṭiyāḷaṉtāṉ; ippōtu veṟuṅkiyāy viṭṭatu. eṉṉa ceyvāṉ? eṉpatu ma.va.
taci keṭṭavaṉ = valimiyum, vaḷamiyum illātavaṉ.
paci keṭṭavaṉ eṉpatum itu.
taccakkāṇi 
taccarkaḷukkuriya kāṇiyāṭci nilam; taccar tam toḻilurimikkāka iṟiyili nilamāka aracāl tarappaṭṭatu.
ivvūrt taccakkāṇi cempātiyuṭiya taccaṉ vaṭukaṉātaṉ ka.ka.co.a.
taccu 
tittu > tattu > taccu.
oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu iṇittu vēli ceyvatu taccu ākum. tittal eṉpatum atuvē. taccu vēli, kaltaccu, marattaccu eṉpavi. taccuc ceytal eṉpatu maṉikōlak kālkōḷ ceytalākum. ippoḻutu apporuḷ aṟivārāmal pūmipūci ākkiviṭṭaṉar. nilatti aḷantu kayiṟiṭṭu muḷikkāl ūṉṟi mañcaḷ nīriṭṭu vaḻipaṭutalum taccup pārppavarkkuc ciṟappuc ceytalum atu. taccu vēli ceyvār taccar.
vikal
eṇtēr ceyyum taccaṉ	- puṟam. 87


tañcam 
tañcamāvatu eḷimi; vēṇṭuvavellām iyalpākavum eḷitākavum kiṭippatu tañcamākum.
tañcak kiḷavi eṇmip poruṭṭē	- tol. 751
eṇmiyāvatu eḷimi.
tañcam nī ivvulakam tāṅkuvāy	- kam. ayōt. 945
tañcu eṉpatum atu.
tañceṉat taṉmayamākkum taṉmi	- kam. āraṇ. 492
tañci 
tañcam eṉpatu aṭikkalam eṉṉum poruḷatu. pātukāppāka vittiruttal kāttal eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum tañcam eṉpatu tañci eṉa akattīcuvaram vaṭṭāra vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. tañci eṉpatu pi eṉṉum poruḷatu. poruṭ pātukāppākiya piyit tañci eṉpatu poruḷ potinta vaḻakkākum.
taṭaccaṭṭi 
tēr akalamāṉatāyiṉ taṭantēr eṉappaṭum. taṭa eṉpatu akaṉṟa periya uyarnta eṉṉum poruḷ tarum uriccol taṭantāḷ nāri eṉpatu kuṟuntoki (349). akaṉṟa caṭṭiyākiya akal, oṭṭaṉcattira vaṭṭārattil taṭac caṭṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatu ilakkiya vaḻakkum makkaḷ vaḻakkum ottiyalum cāṉṟām.
taṭam 
taṭa eṉpatōr uriccol.
perumip poruḷ taruvatu. palar naṭanta vaḻi taṭam eṉappaṭum. taṭavunilip palavu (puṟam. 140). kamalit taṭam eṉpatu ēṟṟak kiṇaṟṟil māṭu muṉṉum piṉṉum ceṉṟu varumvaḻi. taṭam periya nīrnili. taṭākam eṉpatum atu.
taṭam māṟal 
taṭam māṟal = oḻuṅkaṟṟa vaḻiyil naṭattal.
taṭam eṉpatu celvataṟkeṉṟu uriya nērvaḻi allatu tiṭṭappaṭuttiya vaḻi. at taṭatti māṟi vēṟu taṭattil pōvatu eṉpatu muṟikēṭu, oḻuṅkiṉmi eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatām.
ōṭṭap pantayattil, kōṭu pōṭuvatum avaravar kōṭṭil ōṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatum oḻuṅkumuṟi. cilar puṟappaṭum iṭam cariyāka irukkum. ōṭum pōtu taṭam māṟi viṭuvar. iṟutiyil uriya taṭattiṟkup pōy veṟṟi peṟṟu viṭuvar. itu taṭamāṟali viḷakkum. taṭṭuk keṭutal, taṭṭaḻital eṉpaṉavum ipporuḷatē.
taṭaval 
taṭaval = illāmi, taṭavi.
poruḷ nirampa iruntāl aḷḷik koḷḷalām; kuṟivāka iruntāl eṭuttuk koḷḷalām; tēṭip piṭittu eṭukkum aḷavil iruntāl taṭavittāṉ eṭukka vēṇṭi varum. avvāṟu taṭavi eṭukkum aḷavu curuṅkip pōvatē taṭaval eṉappaṭuvatām. ippoḻutu taṭavalāka irukkiṟatu; piṟaku pārkkalām eṉpatil illāmik kuṟippuṇmi aṟika.
cōṟu taṭaval, kaṟi taṭaval eṉpatum itu. iṉit taṭavi eṉpatum taṭaval eṉa vaḻaṅkum. mūṉṟu taṭaval kēṭṭāṉ eṉṟāl mūṉṟu muṟi kēṭṭāṉ eṉpatām.
taṭavutal nīvutal 
taṭavutal= oṉṟil oṉṟu paṭumāṟu taḻuvip parapputal.
nīvutal= taṭaviyati aḻuntat tēyttu viṭutal.
taṭavutal muṟpaṭu ceyal; nīvutal piṟpaṭu ceyal. oru kaḷimpik kālilō kiyilō toṭṭu vittup paravac ceytal taṭavutalām. ataṉi ēṟṟa vaṇṇam mēlum kīḻum pakkaṅkaḷilum taci nilikku ēṟṟavāṟu aḻuttit tēyttu uruvutal nīvutalām.
taṭavit taṭavi nīvutalum, nīvi nīvit taṭavutalum vaḻakkāṟu. oṟṟaṭam vittu nīvutalum uṇṭu. aṅkut taṭavutal illiyām. kāl valikkiṟatu ikkaḷimpik koñcam taṭavi nīvuṅkaḷ eṉpatu naṭimuṟi. pāṟumayirk kuṭumikku eṇṇey nīvutalic cuṭṭum puṟanāṉūṟu (279).

taṭaṟu 
taṭuttu niṟuttuvatu (a) taṭiyiṭṭu niṟuttuvatu.
taṭaṟu = nīrniliyiṉ kari.
nāri vāḻ taṭaṟu	 kam. kiṭ. 938
taṭā 
taṭā:1
taṭā eṉpatu taṭavu ākum.
taṭavum kayavum naḷiyum perumi
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (803).
oru peruvaki maram itu. periya maram ātalāl taṭā eṉappaṭṭatu. nīṇṭu peritāka vaḷarvatu immaram.
taṭaviṉ ōṅku ciṉi
eṉkiṟatu kuṟuntoki (160). ciṉi = kiḷi.
taṭā:2
	taṭā = periya pāṉi. taṭa >  taṭā.
	taṭa 	= akaṉṟa periya. taṭantōḷ, taṭantēr.
	taṭā = perumpāṉi; miṭāppāṉi eṉpatum atu.
taṭāka eḻil (taṭāka ciṅkāram):
talivaṉ ulāvaral, cōliyilum malarppoy kiyilum ulāvutal, miṉpōlum kaṉṉi yoruttiyik kāṇal, iruvarum miyaluṟal, makiḻtal ākiyavaṟṟi yellām pāṭal taṭāka ciṅkāram eṉappaṭum taṭāka eḻilām.
maṉpavaṉi cōli malarmali vāvivara
miṉṉorutti miṉpōl viyaṉeytak - kaṉṉikku
iṟimōkat tāṟcōli ēṟṟavelām pāṭal
aṟiyun taṭākaciṅkā ram	- pira. tiraṭ. 12

taṭākam 
taṭāakam > taṭākam; akam = iṭam. periya nīr nili taṭākam ākum. tāmarit taṭākam eṉpatu tāmarik kuḷamām. ūrp peyarum atu.
taṭātaki 
piṟarāl vella iyalāp peruṅkuṇamum peruntiṟamum peruvīṟum uṭiyavaḷ taṭātakiyām.
maliyāṉ makaḷākak kūṟum toṉmam. maliyāṉ makaḷ eṉpatu tēvā. tirunā.
avaḷiyum oru vakiyāl veṉṟāṉ iṟivaṉ; maṇamum koṇṭāṉ; eṉpatu tiruviḷiyāṭal.
taṭāri 
taṭam > taṭā > taṭāri.
periyatām tōṟpaṟi taṭāriyām. pēriki paṭakam, tāvil taṭāri.
callari pampi taṭāri takki
palvakip paṟiyiṉ peyarppāla vākum	- tivā.
taṭi 
taṭi eṉpatu taṭittatu. veṭṭutal, taci mutaliya pala poruḷtarum iruvaki vaḻakkumuṭiya col. ap potup poruḷ aṉṟi, taṭi eṉpatu ērkkāl eṉṉum poruḷil ciṅkam puṇari vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. ērttaṭi eṉpatu mukavi vaḻakku.
taṭinilam 
akaṉṟa nilattit taṭiyāl aḷaviṭṭup piritta, tuṇṭu nilam. annila aṭiyāḷak kaṟkuṟi ḷa. 
kīḻpulac ceytaṭi ḷa nilaṉ ēṟṟit taṭi oṉpatu 	te.ka.to. 14:44
taṭiviḷiyāṭṭu 
cilampu viḷiyāṭṭu paḻamiyāṉatu. atu cilampāṭṭam, kampāṭṭam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. peṭṭavāyttali vaṭṭārattil cilampāṭṭam taṭiviḷiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭukiṟatu. taṭikkampu, ūṉṟutaṭi eṉpaṉa kampu eṉṉum poruḷil varutal aṟiyalām. taṭi tūkkiyavaṉ ellām taṇṭaṟkāraṉ eṉpatu koṭuṅkōl kuṟitta paḻamoḻi.
taṭumāṟṟam 
taṭumāṟṟam:1
taṭuttu urittal. māṟṟam = maṟuttu moḻital. taṭuttum maṟuttum moḻitalām. maṟumāṟṟam eṉpatum itu.
taṭumāṟṟam:2
col taṭiyātal.
vaḻakkuriñar kēṭṭa viṉāvukkuc cāṭci taṭumāṟip pōṉāṉ.
taṭumāṟṟam:3
kāl taḷarvāl nilimāṟi viḻutal. mutumi, viḻippiṉmi āyavaṟṟāl uṇṭāvatu. mutumit taṭumāṟṟam taḷḷāṭutal eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. intat taḷḷāṭum vayatilum ippaṭi aliya vēṇṭumā? eṉpatu ma.va.
taṭumāṟṟam:4
kalakkam.
vīḻvārkaṇ tōṉṟum taṭumāṟṟam ñālattuḷ
vāḻvārkaṭ kellām varum	- kalit. 145
taṭiccaṭṭi 
ulipoṅki vaḻiyāmal mūṭikku ūṭē vikkum nīrccaṭṭiyit taṭiccaṭṭi eṉpatu tiṇṭukkal vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. taṭuttu niṟuttum caṭṭi taṭic caṭṭiyām. potumakkaḷ pārviyil viḷaṅkum paṭṭaṟivuc ceyal aṟiviyal kūṟu amintatu eṉpati viḷakkum āṭcikaḷuḷ ītoṉṟu.
taṭiviṭi 
taṭi= taṭuttuk kēṭkum viṉā.
viṭi= taṭuttuk kēṭkum viṉāviṟkut tarum maṟumoḻi.
mēṉilit tērvukaḷilum, vātāṭṭu araṅkukaḷilum taṭiviṭi āṭciyuṇṭu. taṭiviṭikaḷāl niṟuvuka eṉpatu viṉāviṉ amitiyām. ikkaṭāvukku viṭi eṉṉi? eṉpatil varum kaṭā eṉpataṉ poruḷ viṉā eṉpatām. ivviṉā potuvāka viṉavappaṭuvatām. taṭi attakiyataṉṟu. eḻuppappaṭṭa karuttit taṭuttu allatu maṟuttu viṉāvuvatām. taṭi eṉpatu taṭukkum iyalpiṉavaṟṟi yellām taḻuvip piṉṉē poruḷāl virintatu.
taṭṭal 
taṭu > taṭṭu > taṭṭal.
oṉṟi oṉṟu taṭuttalum, muṭṭalum, oliyeḻuppalum taṭṭal eṉpatām. kālum kālum naṭiyil taṭṭal, muṭṭi taṭṭal, kiyum kiyum taṭṭal, pārāṭṭal. oruvar naṭakkum pōtu kaltaṭṭal eṉpavi ellām vaḻakkil uḷḷavi.
koṭṭut taṭṭal eṉpatu paṟimuḻakkal. ōṭum nīrit taṭuttu aṇikaṭṭi niṟuttal taṭṭal eṉpatu paḻanāḷ vaḻakku.
taṭṭōr amma ivaṇ taṭṭōrē
taḷḷā tōrivaṇ taḷḷā tōrē	- puṟam. 18
taṭṭōr = nīrit taṭuttu niṟuttiṉōr tam pukaḻiyum pōkāmal niṟuttiṉōr. nīrit taṭuttu niṟuttātōr tam pukaḻiyum nili peṟac ceyyātōr.
taṭṭōr x taḷḷātōr.
taṭṭaḻi (koṭṭi) 
orumukat tōṟpaṟi; tampaṭṭam. tamukku eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku. ūr aviyam kūṭuvataṟkāka aṟiyap peṟum paṟi.
kārttiki nāṉṟu taṭṭaḻi koṭṭi peruṅkuṟi cāṟṟi maṇṭapattē kūṭṭi (ka.ka.co.a.).
taṭṭi aḻippatāl taṭṭaḻi eṉappaṭṭup piṉṉart taṭṭaḻiyāki irukkalām.
taṭṭi 
taṭṭi eṉpatu taṭuppu eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkuvatu potuvaḻakku. taṭṭi piṉṉal, taṭṭi yaṭittal eṉpavi vaḻaṅku coṟkaḷ. teṉṉaṅkīṟṟāl muṭipavi taṭṭi eṉṟum taṭukku eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. iṉit taṭṭi eṉpatum taṭṭaṭṭi eṉpatum māṭiyik kuṟittal mukavi, maturi vaḻakkukaḷ ākum. taṭukku, taṭuppu, taṭi, taṭṭu eṉpaṉa vellām oruvaḻic coṟkaḷ.
taṭṭik kaḻittal 
taṭṭik kaḻittal = colliyatik kēḷāmal otuṅkutal (maḻupputal).
oṉṟic coṉṉāl ataṟkut takkavāṟāṉa oru maṟuppi allatu kāraṇattic collic coṉṉatic ceyyāmal otuṅkupavarik kaṇṭu eṉṉa taṭṭik kaḻikkiṟāyā? eṉpatu vaḻakku. taṭṭik kaḻippatil periya āḷ; etic colluṅkaḷēṉ, ataṟkum oṉṟu avaṉ vittiruppāṉ eṉpatum kēṭpatē.
iṅkē taṭṭutal, kaḻittal eṉṉum coṟkaḷukku nērporuḷ illi. iraṇṭum cērum poḻutu maḻupputal poruḷ tarutal kaṇṭu koḷka.
taṭṭik koṭuttal 
taṭṭik koṭuttal = pārāṭṭal.
oru ceyalic ciṟappākac ceytu muṭittālum, oru pōṭṭiyil veṉṟālum, pārāṭṭat takka paṇpuṭaṉ naṭantu koṇṭālum av vēḷiyil taṭṭik koṭuttal, taḻuvik koḷḷal ākiyavi nikaḻttuvar. ātalāl taṭṭik koṭuttal eṉpataṟkup pārāṭṭutal eṉṉum poruḷ uṇṭāyiṟṟām.
taṭṭik koṭṭi 
	taṭṭutal	= 	maṇ kalaṅkaḷik kiyāl taṭṭip 		pārttal.
	koṭṭutal	= 	taṭṭip pārtta piṉṉar, viral maṭittuk 	koṭṭip pārttal.
taṭṭik koṭṭip pārāmal maṇkalaṅkaḷi vāṅkuvatilli. av vaḻakkam veṇkalak kalaṅkaḷukkum vaḷarntatu. piṉṉar oruvari ētāvatu kēṭṭāl, eṉṉit taṭṭik koṭṭip pārkkiṟāyā? eṉṉum aḷavukkuc ceṉṟuḷḷatu.
taṭṭutal, ōṭṭi uṭivu kīṟalik kāṇa utavum. koṭṭutal, vēkkāṭṭi aṟiya utavum. taṭṭik koṭṭutal āyvu nōkkiṉatu. ātalāl, āyvup poruḷ taṭṭik koṭṭutalukku vantatu.
taṭṭi muṭṭi 
	taṭṭutal	= 	iṭaṟutal.
	muṭṭutal	= 	taliyil paṭutal
takki mukki eṉṉum iṇiccol pōlvatu itu.
taṭṭutal perumpālum kālil taṭṭutaliyum allatu kāl taṭṭutaliyum, muṭṭutal perumpālum taliyil muṭṭutaliyum allatu talimuṭṭutaliyum kuṟikkum. taṭṭi viḻutal eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum muṭṭu muṭṭu eṉṟu kuḻantikaḷukku muṭṭukkāṭṭutaliyum karutuka. taṭṭutal, taṭukkutalām.
taṭṭu 
taṭṭu eṉpatu ōr olikkuṟippu; oṉṟaṉōṭu oṉṟu mōtuvatāl āvatu. avvoli taṭṭuttaṭṭu; taṭataṭa eṉalāyatu. oṉṟaṉ ōṭṭattit taṭuttu niṟuttutal taṭṭu ākum.
ōṭum māṭṭiṉ kompip taṭṭātē eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. taṭṭutal iyakkattit taṭuppatākiṉṟatu. katavu mūṭi iruppatit taṭṭukiṟōm avarkaḷ ceyvati niṟutti nammip pārkkac ceykiṟatu atu. kālil kaltaṭṭutal, kaṭṭiya uṭiyē taṭuttal uṇṭu. uṟaṅkuvāri eḻupputal taṭṭi eḻupputal, uṟakkattit taṭuttu eḻuppuvatu atu. ōṭum nīrit taṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttuvatu vāḻnāḷip pōkāmal niliniṟuttuvatu eṉpatu puṟappāṭal (18).
taṭṭōr amma ivaṭṭaṭ ṭōrē
taḷḷā tōrivaṇ taḷḷā tōrē
taḷḷātōr = taṭātōr.
taṭṭi oru ceyporuḷ. maṟippu atu; piṟar pārviyi maṟikkat taṭṭi kaṭṭuvatu vaḻakku. taṭṭic ceyyum poruḷ taṭṭam. taṭṭiyamitta vaṭivam taṭṭi; taṭṭutal vaḻiyāka olippatu taṭṭip paṟi; kal oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu taṭṭāmal, ki oru kalli eṭukkum pōtu maṟṟoru kalli alukkāmal āṭum viḷiyāṭṭu taṭṭāmāli; taṭṭāmal toṭarum kalloḻuṅku. taṭṭāmpūcciyiṉ uṭalip pārttāl taṭṭut taṭṭāka neṭuvākkil talimutal vālvari irukkak kāṇalām. ippūcci mēlēṟik kīḻē varuvatu pōl kalli eṟintu piṭittu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu taṭṭāṅkal viḷiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭukiṟatu.
taṭuppatu taṭi; kālit taṭukkum vāḻinār taṭi eṉappaṭum. puḷkuṟi, kāṇpār cokiṉat taṭi (cakuṉattaṭi) eṉpar. taṭṭi vaṭivil aminta pāṟi taṭṭip pāṟi; payaṟu vakiyuḷ uruṇṭu tiraḷāp payaṟu taṭṭippayaṟu eṉappaṭum. taṭṭut taṭṭāka aminta cōḷam, kampu, karumpu, nāṇal ākiyavaṟṟiṉ tāḷ taṭṭi eṉappaṭum. cōḷattaṭṭi, kammantaṭṭi, karumputtaṭṭi, nāṇal taṭṭi.
taṭuttu niṟuttat taṭipaṭāmal ōṭa aminta ōṭṭam taṭṭōṭṭam; viḷiyāṭṭu kiḷittaṭṭu.
taṭṭic ceyyappaṭṭa vaṭṭa vaṭivap poruḷ taṭṭam (tāmpāḷam). taṭṭit toḻil ceyyum kammakāraruḷ oru pirivar taṭṭār. kaṭṭi vaṇṭiyil pāram pōṭuvataṟkut taṭṭi kaṭṭutal vaḻakkam. pōṭappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ veḷiyē pōkāmal taṭuppatu atu. māṭṭukku vikkōl tāḷ mutaliyavi pōṭṭut tiṉpataṟku veḷiyēṟāmal avaṟṟit taṭukkat taṭṭi amippar.
taṭṭut taṭṭāka variciyuṟa amintamiyāl tēṉkūṭu taṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. teṉṉaṅ kīṟṟit taṭuttut taṭuttup piṉṉutal taṭṭu ākum. ataṉi maṟippākak kaṭṭutal taṭṭi eṉappaṭukiṟatu. maṟivu, maṟical eṉpaṉavum atu. kālpaṭam vari toṅkum vēṭṭiyit taṭuttu mēlē eṭuttuk kaṭṭutal taṭṭuṭiyākum; taṟṟuṭi eṉpatum atu. tārppāycci eṉavum collappaṭum.
taṭṭutal vaṟumip poruḷtarum. taṭṭuppāṭu eṉpatum atu. uṇavut taṭṭuppāṭu, miṉ taṭṭuppāṭu, taṇṇīrt taṭṭuppāṭu eṉa eṇṇaṟṟa taṭṭuppāṭukaḷ uṇṭu.
taṭṭik koṭuttal pārāṭṭutalākum. cila iṭaṅkaḷil uṉakku iraṇṭu taṭṭut taṭṭiṉāltāṉ oḻuṅkukku varuvāy eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
kalaṅkaḷil taṭṭu, kātaṇiyil taṭṭu, tēṉtaṭṭu, aṭṭaḷit taṭṭu, taṭṭup palaki eṉat taṭṭukaḷ makkaḷ vaḻakkil peruki amintuḷa. taṭṭu, taṭṭam eṉappaṭum iṭamum uṇṭu.
taṭṭu eṉpatu taṭṭi eṉṉum poruḷil paḻaṉi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. taṭṭut taṭi eṉpatu iṇiccol. taṭṭuppaṭutal tōṉṟutal poruḷatu. kaṇṇil taṭṭuppaṭṭāḷ eṉpatu pāvēntar pāṭiya aḻakiṉ cirippu.
taṭṭutal eṉpatu kiṭittal eṉṉum poruḷil cīrkāḻi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. taṭṭa māṭṭēṉ eṉap pōkiṟatu eṉpatu mukavi vaḻakku. atuvum kiṭittal poruḷatēyām.
taṭṭu tāmpāḷam 
taṭṭu= tirunīṟu, cūṭaṉ vikkum ciṟutaṭṭu.
tāmpāḷam= tēṅkāy, paḻam, veṟṟili vikkum peruntaṭṭu.
tāmpāḷattiṉ uḷḷē taṭṭu aṭaṅkum kōyil vaḻipāṭṭukkut tāmpāḷam taṭṭu iraṇṭum koṇṭu celvatu vaḻakkam. akalam peritu ciṟitu eṉpatu maṭṭumallāmal amippu muṟiyilum iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
iṉit tāmpāḷattip peruntaṭṭu eṉpatum uṇṭu. tarācuttaṭṭu, iṭṭavit taṭṭu, uṇkalat taṭṭu eṉappiṟa taṭṭukaḷum uḷa.
taṭṭut taṭaṅkal 
	taṭṭu	= 	oṉṟic ceyya muṉivārkku mutaṟkaṇ  ētēṉum  taṭiyuṇṭāku māṉāl atu taṭṭu eṉappaṭum.
	taṭaṅkal	=	ac ceyalic ceyyuṅkāl iṭi 	iṭiyē ēṟpaṭum 
   taṭikaḷ taṭaṅkal eṉappaṭum.
cilar eṭuttuk koṇṭa ceyalkaḷ toṭakka mutal iṭiyūṟu etuvumiṉṟi iṉitu muṭivatāy amiyum. avar, taṭṭut taṭaṅkal iṉṟic ceytu muṭittatāka makiḻvar.
taṭiyuṇṭāṉāl taṭantōḷ uṇṭu	 eṉpatu puraṭci uri.
taṭṭut taṭumāṟi 
	taṭṭutal		=  ētāvatu oṉṟu iṭaṟutal.
	taṭumāṟutal	=  iṭaṟutalāl vīḻtal.
itu, taṭṭut taṭumāṟi eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. taṭṭutal taṭiyātalām. taṭumāṟṟam eṉpatu kāl taḷḷāṭutal mikumutiyarō oḷiyiḻantavarō naṭattaṟkum taṭṭit taṭumāṟutal kaṇkūṭu. taḷḷāṭutal taḷḷamāṭutal eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. taḷḷa māṭṭāmal irukkiṟēṉ eṉpatu iṟappu varavilliyē eṉṉum ēkkattil varuvatu.
* takkimukki, taṭṭi muṭṭi eṉpavi kāṇka.
taṭṭuttaṭṭutal 
uṉṉi orutaṭṭut taṭṭiṉāltāṉ oḻuṅkāka vēli pārppāy eṉpatil taṭṭut taṭṭutal eṉpatu aṭittal poruḷil varukiṉṟatām.
taṭṭik koṭuttalukku etiriṭi, taṭṭut taṭṭutal.
taṭṭumāṟal 
ōṭṭap pantayattilum - variciyiṭṭuk kaḷi veṭṭu katiraṟuppu naṭakkiyilum - avaravarkkuriya taṭṭil (varampil) niṉṟu iyaṅkal vēṇṭum. illiyēl taṭṭumāṟal eṉpar. avaravar varampil ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu itu. avvāṟu ceyyārit taṭṭumāṟi eṉpar.
tirumaṇa uṟutippāṭṭu nikaḻvil, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭārum tāmtām koṇṭu vantirunta taṭṭukaḷi (paḻaṅkaḷ malarmāli uṭi mutaliyavi yuṭiyavi) tāymāmaṉmār eṭuttut taṭṭumāṟṟi maṇamakkaḷ peṟṟōrkaḷiṭam vaḻaṅki, avarkaḷ tampiḷḷikaḷukku vaḻaṅki, uṭiyi uṭuttuk koṇṭu avikku varac ceytalit taṭṭumāṟṟal eṉpatu vaḻakkamākum.
māṟalukkum māṟṟalukkum evvaḷavu vēṟṟumi!
evarkol paṇṇavarkaḷ evarkol maṇṇavarkaḷ
etukol poṉṉula keṉattaṭṭu māṟavum
eṉak kumarakuruparar oru taṭṭumāṟalic cuṭṭukiṟār. ivaṇ taṭṭumāṟal taṭumāṟalām.
* taṭam māṟal kāṇka.

taṭṭu muṭṭu 
	taṭṭu		= 	uṇṇaṟkum mūṭutaṟkum aminta taṭṭam mūṭi			 	   pōlvaṉa.
	muṭṭu		= 	aṭukkaḷi allatu camiyaṟ kaṭṭil neruṅkik		 		   kiṭakkum 
				pāṉi, caṭṭi, aṇṭā kuṇṭā mutaliya poruḷkaḷ.
taṭṭu muṭṭup poruḷkaḷ eṉpatu kikkum kālukkum taṭṭavum muṭṭavum iruppavi. eṉiṉum, eṭukka vittirukkum poruḷkaḷām.
taṭṭu		 = 	taṭṭutal; muṭṭu = muṭṭutal; viṉi.
taṭṭu 		= 	taṭṭam pōlvaṉa; muṭṭu = muṭṭi; peyar.
pāḷi cīvi muṭṭi kaṭṭutal vaḻakkam. picci muṭṭi eṉṟoru vaḻakkum uṇṭu.
taṭṭu vaṇṭi 
mēlmūṭu illāmal muṉṉum irupakkaṅkaḷilum taṭṭu vitta oṟṟi māṭṭu vaṇṭiyit taṭṭuvaṇṭi eṉpatu nākar kōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. av vaḻakkam, piṉṉark kutiri iḻukkum mūṭu vaṇṭikkum peyarāyiṟṟu. mēl taṭuppum uṭiyatu mūṭu allatu kūṇṭu vaṇṭi.
taṭṭūṭi 
taṭṭu + ūṭi = taṭṭūṭi. taṭṭu amittu ūṭupalaki parappi amitta kaṭṭilit taṭṭūṭi eṉpatu nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. vāyāṭuvatu vāyāṭi āvatu pōla, ūṭāṭuvatu ūṭāṭiyāyatu. palakik kaṭṭil ūṭum pāvum palakiyāka iruttalik kāṇka.
taṭṭi 
taṭṭi:1
kōlāl taṭṭi yeḻuppiṉ oli uṇṭākkum tōṟpaṟi vakiyuḷ oṉṟu taṭṭi.
paṟavi yōṭṭuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttum tōṟpaṟi taṭṭi.

taṭṭi:2
cōḷam, kēḻvaraku, varaku, tiṉi ākiya payirkaḷiṉ taṇṭu, taṭṭi eṉappaṭum. ūṭu ūṭu taṭutta vari allatu kaṇukkaḷi uṭiyavi eṉpati aṟika.
taṭṭi:3
māvit taṭṭip pōṭṭu vaṭṭat takaṭākac ceyyappaṭum ciṟu tīṉi taṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
* taṭṭu kāṇka.
taṭṭi tāḷ 
	taṭṭi	= 	karumpu, cōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ 				aṭittaṇṭu.
	tāḷ		= 	nel, pul mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṭittaṇṭu.
taṭṭiyum tāḷum pulliṉattaṉavē. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ aṭittaṇṭu taṭṭi, tāḷ eṉap pakuttu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōli, kīṟṟu, tōki eṉpaṉa pulliṉattiṟkuriya palvēṟu ilikaḷē allavō, atu pōla eṉka. māṭṭukkut taṭṭi tāḷ cērttup paṭappi pōṭṭuk koḷḷutal vēḷāṇ toḻiṟkuriyatām.
taṭṭoli 
taṭṭoli = karumār taṭṭār āyōr paṭṭaṭi amittut toḻil ceytaṟkuk kaṭṭum vari. taṭṭārak kāṇi eṉpatum itu.
taṭṭārap paṇicey kāṇi 	(te.ka.to. 5:515).
taṇattal 
taṇattal = pirital.
pirital vaḻiyāl maṉattil vetuppu - veppu - uṇṭātalāl taṇattal eṉappaṭum. taḻal > taṇal = veppu.
maṇantaṉi aruḷāy āyiṉum pipayat
taṇantaṉi yāki uymmō	- aiṅ. 83

taṇal 
taṇ + al = taṇal = taṇmi aṟṟatu; veppu. taḻal eṉpatum itu.
taḻalkoṇṭu vāṭā tampi - aṇṇaṉ
kiyi erittiṭuvōm	- pārati. pāñ.
taṇiki 
taṇi + ki = taṇiki. ki = iṭam.
taṇinta iṭam, taṇiki. irupālum uyarnta malikaḷukku iṭiyē taṇivāka uḷḷa mali taṇiki; tiruttaṇi. toṉmak kati, tirumurukaṉ ciṉam taṇinta iṭam eṉpatu.
taṇ 
taṇi > taṇ.
veyilālum neruppālum vetumpiyirunta nīr veppilāp pōḻtilum veppilā iṭattilum taṉ vemmi taṇitalāl taṇmi āyatu. taṇkatir, taṇcuṭar eṉa nilavum, taṇkāl eṉat teṉṟal kāṟṟum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
maṉavepput taṇital āṟutal iḷakutal kuḷirtal eṉappaṭalāyiṟṟu. taṇṇeṉal, taṇmi eṉpaviyum kuḷumip poruḷ tantaṉa.
taṇkatir maṇṭilam	- akam. 277
taṇtamiḻ eṉpatu payilvārkku uṇṭākum uḷḷak kuḷirmi paṟṟiyatu (pari. tira. 8).
taṇṇīr x vennīr.
taṇkāl 
taṇkāl eṉpatu paḻamiyāṉatōr ūr.
taṇkāl = taṇṇiya teṉṟal kāṟṟu.
taṇkāl = nīrvaḷamikka kālvāy; kālvāy vāynta ūr.
paḻantaṇkāl, tiruttaṅkal eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ap peyarkkut takap puṉivum uṇṭu. tiruttaṅkiya iṭam eṉpār. ip peyarmāṟṟam ap paḻiya varalāṟṟiṉ maṟippāki ūravarkkum uṇmi puriyāmal ākkiviṭṭatu.
taṇkāl ūril vāḻnta caṅkap pulavar, taṇkālpūṇ kollaṉār (puṟam. 326). pūṇkollar = taṭṭār.
taṇṭaka māli (puṇarcci māli) 
veṇpāviṉāl munnūṟu ceyyuṭ kūṟuvatu taṇṭaka māliyākum. veṇpuṇarcci māli eṉpatum itu. pāvaki, veṇpā; poruḷvaki, puṇarcci; ākaliṉ veṇpuṇarcci māli eṉṟār.
veṇpā vāṉmun nūṟu virippatu
taṇṭaka māliyām cāṟṟuṅ kālē	- muttuvī. 1068
taṇṭattukku aḻal 
vēṇṭum celavukkeṉat tēṭivitta poruḷi vēṇṭāc celavukkuk kaṭṭāyamākac celaviṭa nērum pōtu ataṉit taṇṭattukku aḻutatākak kūṟuvatu ma.va.
vēṇṭum eṉṟā celavaḻittēṉ; taṇṭattukku aḻutēṉ eṉpar.
taṇṭayam 
civiki eṉṉum ūrti, pallakku, taṇṭiyal eṉa vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. capparam eṉpatu kōyil ulā ūrti. iv vūrti kaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum kampu vaḷiya vēṇṭumāṟellām vaḷintu taruvatum vaḷi eṉṉum peyaruṭiyatu mākiya mūṅkil ākum.
taṇṭiyalukkup payaṉpaṭuvatām mūṅkilit taṇṭayam eṉpatu nākarkōyil vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum.
taṇṭali 
taṇ + tali = taṇṭali.
tali = iṭam; itu kākkaḷ malkiya iṭam.
taṇṭali; taṇṭalip puttūr; kuḷir taṇṭali - ūrppeyarkaḷ.
taṇṭaliyār catakam - ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
taṇṭavāṇikam 
taṇṭattiṟkuriya poruḷi viṟkum vaṇikam. viṟṟaṟkuk kūṭātu eṉappaṭṭa poruḷi viṟpatu taṇṭattiṟkuriya vaṇikamākum (ka.ka.co.a.).
taṇṭā tacukkā 
	taṇṭā		= 	vari vāṅka allatu vari taṇṭa 				vantavaṉā?
	tacukkā		=	 koḻuttup pōy vantavaṉā?
nāṉ avaṉiṭam naṭantatu eṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉ taṇṭā tacukkā eṉṟu kutittu viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku.
aracu vari taṇṭa vantavaṉ aṭāvaṭi āṇi celuttu vatiyum, taṭi tūkkiyavaṉellām taṇṭakkāraṉā eṉpatiyum karutiṉāl taṇṭā eṉpataṉ poruḷ viḷaṅkum. taṇṭu eṉpatu paṭi; tacukku eṉpatu koḻutta ūṉ.
taṇṭu 
taṇ + tu = taṇṭu.
taṇṇīril tōṉṟum koṭi, taṇṭu. tāmarit taṇṭu. atu vaḷarnta vaki kāṇka.
tāmarikku maṭṭumō taṇṭu niṉṟatu, kuvaḷit taṇṭu, allit taṇṭu eṉa nīrk koṭikku vaḷarntatu.
kīri vakiyuḷ oṉṟu taṇṭaṅkīri allatu taṇṭukkīri. ataṉik kīrit taṇṭu eṉpatum vaḻakkē. atu taṇṭi maṭṭum kuṟippatām.
ciṟukīri, aṟukīri (aṟikkīri) vēḷik kīri, akattik kīri, muruṅkik kīri eṉpavaṟṟuḷ kīri potup peyarāyum muṉcol ciṟappup peyarāyum iruttal teḷivu, avvāṟu nōkkat taṇṭuk kīriyiṉ mutaṟcollē ciṟappup peyar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. taṇṭu eṉpatu etu? ataṉ vēraṟṟa tūrē taṇṭeṉka.
ciṟukīriyum aṟukīriyum koṭi vaki; taṇṭuṭiyaṉa alla. avaṟṟi vilakkit taṇṭuk kīri taṉ tūrt taṭippic ciṟappākak koṇṭatām. taṇṭi taṭi aṉṟō!
taṇṭaṅkōri eṉak kōriyuḷ oṉṟaṟku virintatu. mūṅkil taṇṭu, nāṇal taṇṭu, vāḻit taṇṭu eṉap perukiyatu.
taṇṭu viḷakku (Tube Light) eṉap puttāṭciyum peṟṟatu.
mūkkāntaṇṭu, kātuttaṇṭu, mutukantaṇṭu (muḷḷantaṇṭu)  eṉṟu uṭal uṟuppukaḷukkum tāviyatu. kātil pōṭum taṇṭoṭṭiyoṭum kūṭa oṭṭik koṇṭatu. nilit tiṇiyiṉ (tāvarattiṉ) aṭippakutiyām taṇṭu, taṇṭi eṉṉum kaṉap poruḷum tantatu. taṭi eṉa iṭik kuṟiyāki, vēppantaṭi, tēkkantaṭi eṉavum vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
ūṉ taṭi eṉavum ūṉṟiyatu. taṭi taṭippu eṉa vaḷarntatu. tōltaṭippu valuttu uḷḷat taṭippukkum iṭantantatu. taṭimaṉ, taṭumaṉ, taṭumam eṉa mūkkut taṭippu nōyp peyarum āyiṟṟu. taṭi, taṭiyaṉ, taṭicci eṉa uvamiyāl kuṟiyīṭṭup peyarum āyiṟṟu. taṭi, taṭital (veṭṭutal), akaṟṟutal eṉṉum viṉikku mūlamum āyatu. taṇṭu, taṭṭu ākit taṭṭiyum āyiṟṟu. taṭi ivvaḷavil niṟkat taṇṭukkuc celvōm.
taṇṭu, taṭi allatu kōl eṉṉum poruḷil vaḷarntatu aṉṟō! taḷarnaṭi mutiyar taṇṭūṉṟic cellutal vaḻak kāyiṟṟu. taṇṭu kāl ūṉṟic cellum mutiyaṉi maṇimēkali urikkum (14:30). toṭittali viḻuttaṇṭiṉip puṟappāṭal viḷakkum (243).
toṭiyāvatu vaḷivu; vaḷiyalukkut toṭi eṉṉum peyarum vaḷiyal toṭuttal eṉṉum vaḻakkum uṇmi aṟika. toṭuvi eṉṉum karuvi, vikkōl tiraṭṭutaṟkākak kaḷappaṇiyāḷar kiyakat tiruppatum aṟika. vaḷinta taliyi allatu ucciyi uṭiya taṇṭu (kampu) toṭit talit taṇṭām. atu kālpōl payaṉ tantu taḷarcci nīkkum takavu nōkki viḻuttaṇṭu (ciṟanta taṇṭu) eṉappaṭṭatām.
toṭittali viḻuttaṇṭu eṉṟu āṭci ceyta pulavar peyari oḻiya viṭṭa pulavar ulakam, avartam collāṭci koṇṭē avarkkup peyar cūṭṭip pōṟṟi vittamiyāl toṭittali viḻuttaṇṭiṉār eṉṟoru pulavari nām aṟiya vāyttatām.
taṇṭu (kōl) koṇṭu pōrukkuc celvatu paṇṭi vaḻakku. ātalāl taṇṭukkup paṭi eṉṉum poruḷ uṇṭāyiṟṟu. paṭittalivar taṇṭa nāyakar eṉappaṭṭār. taṇṭaṭittal eṉpatu paṭiñar pāḷiyam iṟaṅkutalāyiṟṟu.
pōriṉ mēl taṇṭeṭukka eṉpatum (382) taṇṭa nāyakar kākkum eṉpatum (386) kaliṅkattup paraṇi.
iṉi, vari vāṅkac celvār taṇṭu koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. taṇṭu koṇṭu vari vāṅkiyatāl vari taṇṭutal eṉa vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. ataṉi vāṅkupavar taṇṭalar taṇṭaṟkārar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ talivar taṇṭal nāyakar āṉār.
taṭi tūkkiyavaṉ ellām taṇṭalkāraṉ eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum taṇṭal nāyakar eṉṉum kalveṭṭu moḻiyum, māvaṭṭa āṭciyāḷarit taṇṭal nāyakar eṉa vaḻaṅkum moḻiyākkamum karutattakkaṉa. vari taṇṭutaṟku āḷ varukiṟatu; avaravar variyic celuttuka! eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka aṟiyappaṭṭa paṟi taṇṭaṟi. atu taṇṭōr taṇṭōrā eṉappaṭṭatu. ‘v‹d j©nlhuh¥ nghL»whŒ? eṉpatu iṉṟum vaḻaṅku moḻi.
vari kaṭṭutaṟku maṟuttālō, kuṟṟam ceytu etiriṭṭālō avarit taṇṭāl (kōlāl) māṭṭuvatu (aṭippatu) vaḻakkamāka iruntatu. ataṉ vaḻi vantatē taṇṭaṉi! piṉṉarc ciṟiyaṭippum, poruṭpaṟippum piṟa piṟavum taṇṭaṉip poruḷil vaḷarntaṉa.
kaṭumoḻiyum kiyikanta taṇṭamum 	eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (567).
taṇṭamum taṇiti paṇṭiyiṟ peritē	 eṉpatu puṟam (10).
taṇṭu eṭuttavaṉiṉ koṭumikku añcik koṭutta koṭumi taṇṭattukku aḻutal āyiṟṟu. taṭiyaṉukkup pōṭum veṭṭic cōṟu - pōṭāviṭṭāl etuvum ceyvāṉē ataṉāl - taṇṭac cōṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. taṭiyaṉukku añci vaṇaṅkiyatē taṇṭaṉiṭutalāyt teṇṭaṉiṭutalāy vaḻaṅki, iṟivaḻipāṭṭukkum ēṟiyatu. taṭiyaṉ eṉpāṉ taṭiyuṭiyavaṉum taṭittavaṉum tāṉē. taṭi eṉpatu ūṉ ātal ūṉ taṭi piṟappiṉum eṉa varum puṟappāṭṭāl pulappaṭum (74).
taṇṭi eṉpatōr peyar! taṇṭittavar peyarē taṇṭiyāyiṟṟu. avarē taṇṭiyaṭikaḷ nāyaṉār eṉpār. tanti coṟpaṭi tāḷaṟat taṇṭittavar avar. tāḷaṟat taṇṭitta taṇṭi eṉpatu civarakaciyam (pāyiram. 7).
taṇṭu koṇṭavaṉ taṇṭaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. appeyarālē māntar palar iṉṟum uḷar. taṇṭum ataṉoṭu vēlum kūṭiya paṭikkalam taṇṭu, taṇṭam eṉavum paṭṭatu. taṇṭāyutam eṉpatu teṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻiyām irumoḻip piṟappi! avaṟṟiṉ vaḻippaṭṭaṉavē taṇṭāyutaṉ, taṇṭapāṇi eṉṉum murukaṉ peyarkaḷ taṇṭumāri eṉṉum ammi peyarē ataṉ poruḷviḷakkam purivikkum. appaṭiyē taṇṭu vināyakar eṉpatum.
taṇṭu paṭikkaruviyāka maṭṭum niṉṟu viṭavilli. taṇṭu valittal paṭakukku uṇṭaṉṟō! taṇṭutāṉē kuyakkalam ceytaṟkuc cuḻaṟṟukkōl! tēr untutaṟku ākum cavaḷamaram taṇṭu kōl eṉappaṭṭatē. vil taṇṭu, vīṇit taṇṭu, camaṉkōl (tarācut) taṇṭu, kāvaṭit taṇṭu eṉpavi palvēṟu payaṉtaṇṭukaḷ. āṇuṟuppum taṇṭeṉa vaḻakkil niṉṟatu. iraṭṭi (mituṉam) eṉṉum viṇmīṉ tōṟṟam taṇṭu pōl iruttalāl atu, taṇṭu eṉappaṭum.
mūṅkil taṇṭu mukattal aḷavuk karuviyākavum orukāl payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. oru kaṇuvukku aṭiyilum ataṉmēṟ kaṇuvukku aṭiyilum aṟuttut tūkku ākkuvatum paḻavaḻakku. attaṇṭu, kūḻum kañciyum koṇṭu cellap payaṉpaṭuttiya vaḻakkuṇṭu. matuppey taṇṭum ilakkiya āṭciyil uṇṭu.
mūṅkil taṇṭu koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa ūrti taṇṭiki yāyatu. atu vaḷḷuvar kālattil civiki eṉappaṭṭatu. taṇṭikik kaṉakarāyaṉ paḷḷu eṉpatoru ciṟṟilakkiyam. taṇṭiki, celvac cerukkar ūrtiyāka iruntatum, tuṟavu maṭattar ūrtiyāka iruntatum, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum kaṇṭatu.
kampup poruḷil irunta taṇṭu, kampip poruḷum taruvatāy virintatu. appoḻutu karumpoṉ, cempoṉ, veṇpoṉ mutaliya kaṉimak kampiyum taṇṭākavum, taṇṭiṉ tiripākavum vaḻakkil ūṉṟiyatu. viḷakkut taṇṭu paḻamiyāṉatē.
vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻiyi viḷakkukku oppiṭṭuc collukiṟatu tiruvaḷḷuva mālip pāṭṭoṉṟu. aṟam, takaḷi; poruḷ, tiri; iṉpu, ney; col, tī; kuṟaṭpā, taṇṭu eṉkiṟatu atu.
tirukip poṉṉeṭun taṇṭil tiraṇṭavāl eṉṉum kampar toṭarāl poṟkampi taṇṭeṉap paṭṭati aṟiyalām (cun. 1193).
kōṭṭi vāyilkaḷiṉ uṭkāppākap pariya taṭikaḷik kuṟukkiṭa vippatu paṇṭi vaḻakku. tuḷiyiṭṭa pariya tūṇkaḷ irupālum niṟutti, ataṉ tuḷikkuḷ taṭiyic ceruki vitta annaṭimuṟiyē innāḷ katavukaḷil amikkum aṭitaṇṭāvukku mūlam. irumpup paṭṭitāṉē aṭitaṇṭā!
neṭitākavum valitākavum ceyyappaṭuvatum, toṭarvaṇṭi ōṭutaṟku vaḻiyāy amikkappaṭuvatum ākiyatu taṇṭavāḷam. taṇṭa vālam eṉṉum peyarē taṇṭavāḷam āyiṟṟām. vālam - nīḷam; vāl - neṭiyatu, neṭiya kiḻivu vālamākak kiḻintateṉak kūṟappaṭum. nīṇṭu kuṟukiya nilam, vālam eṉappaṭum. kuraṅku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vāliyum, vāli eṉṉum peyariyum karutuka.
kuḻantikaḷukku melliya kampi vaḷiyattik kālil pōṭuvatum, caṟṟē periya kuḻanti āṉapiṉ ataṉik kaḻaṟṟiviṭṭut taṇṭi pōṭuvatum aṟika.
parupporuḷāl virinta taṇṭu, karuttup poruḷām nuṇporuḷāyum virintatu.
taṇṭiṉ ceyal alittalum alikkaḻittalumākaliṉ taṇṭukku apporuḷkaḷ uḷavāyiṉa.
parāri vēvi parukeṉat taṇṭi eṉpatu porunarāṟṟup paṭi (104). itil varum taṇṭi eṉpataṟkup palkāl alittu eṉap poruḷ kūṟiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. taṭiyum kiḻaṅkum taṇṭiṉar tarīi eṉṉum malipaṭukaṭāttiṉ taṇṭikkum apporuḷē avar kūṟiṉār (415).
taṇṭutalām alittalāl nīṅkutal uṇṭām. ataṉāl, yāṉ taṇṭavum tāṉ taṇṭāṉ eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉ taṇṭutal nīṅkal poruḷil vantatu (384) taṇṭā nōy eṉpatu ip poruḷil vanta tirukkuṟaḷ (1171).
taṇṭuṭaṉ taṇṭu mōtuṅkāl atu pōrtāṉē! ātalāl pōrp poruḷum, pōriṭuvār pakivar ātalāl pakip poruḷum uṇṭāyiṉa.
taṇṭa lillātu uṭaṉkūṭṭal 	eṉpatu kūrma purāṇam (cūta. 33).
tāḷniḻal nīṅkiya taṇṭalar	 eṉpatu cētu purāṇam (maṅkala. 8).
taṇṭātu irappiṉum eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟiyatu pōl (poruḷ. 99) taṇṭātu perukuvatu taṇṭu pōlum!
taṇṇaṭi 
taṇ + naṭi = taṇṇaṭi.
naṉṉaṭi nalkal vēntarkkuk kaṭaṉē
eṉṉum puṟappāṭalil (312) varum nalnaṭi, naṉcey nilam ākum. it taṇṇaṭi nīrvaḷam cārnta nilam eṉpatām.

taṇṇīr kāṭṭutal 
taṇṇīr kāṭṭutal = tappi viṭutal.
oru koḷḷik kūṭṭam kaṭṭuppāṭāṉa anta ūrkkut taṇṇīr kāṭṭiyirukkiṟatu, uṉakku orunāḷ taṇṇīr kāṭṭāmalā viṭuvāṉ; appoḻutu uṇmi puriyum eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻakkukaḷ taṇṇīr kāṭṭutal eṉpataṟkut tappi viṭutal eṉṉum poruḷ uḷḷati viḷakkum. tappiviṭutalum nayavañcaka māka ēmāṟṟit tappi viṭutalām.
taṇṇīr teḷittal eṉpatu tāri vārttal eṉpatu pōṉṟatē. tāri vārttal eṉpatu koṭip poruḷ. taṇṇīr teḷittal eṉpatu kaḻittuk kaṭṭal eṉṉum poruḷatām.
uṉṉit taṇṇīr teḷittu viṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟāl uṉṉik kiviṭṭu viṭṭārkaḷ, puṟakkaṇittu viṭṭārkaḷ, otukki viṭṭārkaḷ eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatām.
koṭutta poruḷ, koṭuttavar urimiyi viṭṭu nīṅkiviṭuvatē yaṉṟō! ap poruḷil varuvatu taṇṇīr teḷittalām.


taṇṇīrkkāl 
aṅkaṇam eṉṉum iruvaki vaḻakkum aminta col, taṇṇīrkkāl eṉṟum, taṇṇīrkkiṭi eṉṟum kumarimāvaṭṭa mēlpura vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. ōṭum nīr kāl; ōṭāmal kiṭakkum nīr kiṭi; iv viruvakiyilum (ōṭṭamum tēkkamum) uḷḷami kaṇkūṭu.
taṇṇīrppaḻam 
taṟpūcuṇi eṉṉum nīr vaḷamikka kāyic ceṉṉi vaṭṭāram taṇṇīrp paḻam eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. teṉtamiḻakap pakutiyil taṇṇīrkkāy eṉak kaṟiyākkiyuṇṇat takka kāy uṇṭu. curikkāy (kuṇṭuc curikkāy) pōṉṟatu atu. taṟpūcuṇi eṉpatil uḷḷa taṉ eṉpatu taṇṇīrp poruḷatu. taṟkālam = kārkālam, maḻikkālam. taṟpāṭiya taḷiyuṇavu (paṭ. 3). taṟpū eṉpatu kocciyum vaḻuvumām.
taṇṇumi 
taṇinta ōciyuṭiya tōṟpaṟi taṇṇumiyākum. taṭārikku nērmāṟu ōciyuṭiyatu.
